 
K.A.ccheRAgANkṣA kṣjñkR 
td ghgrgrNA - 1963

Vākyapadīyā

atha sādhanasamuddeśaḥ | 

(1) 'dik sādhanam' iti diśo'nantaraṃ sādhanamuddiṣṭaṃ
lakṣayitumāha--
svāśraye samavetānāṃ tadvadevāśrayāntare |
kriyāṇāmabhiniṣpatau sāmarthyaṃ sādhanaṃ viduḥ || 1 ||
(2) kriyānirvṛtau dravyasya śaktiḥ sādhanam, sādhyate'nayā kriyeti
bhāṣyakāraprabhṛtayo viduḥ | tathā hi 
"giṇaḥ sādhanam " (g.eṇa. od gh. 3.2.115)
iti bhāṣya uktam | śaktirevātrādhārapāratantryādanyasmācca svāśrayasya
bhedakatvād guṇaśabdenoktā |
kṛdākhyātābhihitayorbhāvayorbhedābhidhānaprasaṅge tu yad
bhāṣyam--- "dravyaṃ kriyābhinirvṛtiṃ pratiṃ sādhanabhāvamupaiti"
(g.eṇa.od gh. 3.1.67)
iti tadanyatrāhatya dravyasya sādhanatvavyudāsāccchaktivyavadhānāśrayeṇa
gamayitavyam | śaktaṃ hi dravyaṃ sādhanam, na tu nirādhārā śaktiḥ
sambhavati |
"parokṣe liṭ" (gh. 3.2.115)
ityatrāpi 
"yadi guṇasamudāyaḥ sādhanam, sādhanamapyanumānagamyam |
athānyadguṇebhyaḥ sādhanam, bhavati pratyakṣaparokṣatāyāḥ saṃbhavaḥ"
(g.eṇa. od gh. 3.2.115)
iti yad bhāṣyaṃ tadapyevameva netavyam | śakternityaparokṣatvāt
kriyāvadviśeṣaṇamavyabhicārādayuktamiti
parokṣatāviśeṣaṇasāmarthyāccchaktyāviṣṭaṃ dravyaṃ sādhanamupanyastam
| evam 
"upasargāccchandasi dhātvarthe"(gh. 5.1.118)
ityatra 
"sādhane'yaṃ bhavaṃlliṅgasaṃkhyābhyāṃ yokṣyate"
(g.eṇa. od gh. 5.1.118)
iti bhāṣyaṃ sādhanaśaktiviśiṣṭe dravya iti vyākhyeyam | tathā
hi-dhātāvityevokte śabde vṛtasambhavāt kriyāpratipatau
siddhāyāmarthagrahaṇāddhātukṛto'rtha iti samāsappatipatyarthād
dravyamāśritam | vastuparyāyo hyarthaśabdaḥ | dravyasya ca svarūpeṇa
siddhasvabhāvatvāt kriyayā karaṇānupapateḥ karmādikārakarūpeṇaiva
tasya karaṇam | kriyā hi kārakāṇi karoti,
tadapekṣatvātadvyavahārasyeti sādhanātmanā dravyasya kriyayā
karaṇādevamuktam | dhāturarthaḥ prayojanamasyetyetatu
bhāṣyāvyākhyānamayuktam | evaṃ hi śaktereva kriyā sādhyatvena
prayojanamiti dravyaṃ na pratyāyyeta | yatu
"sādhanaṃ vai dravyam, na ca dravyasya prakarṣāpakarṣau staḥ"
(g.eṇa. od gh. 5.3.55)
ityadravyaprakarṣavyākhyāne bhāṣye dravyasya sādhanatvābhidhānaṃ
taccchaktiśaktimatorbhedāvivakṣayetyavagantavyam | tathā hi-tiṅntāt
kriyāsādhanābhidhāyinaḥ prakarṣapratyaya iti śakteḥ
kriyāvakṣaṇakāryānumeyāyāḥ kāryadvāreṇaiva prakarṣa iti kriyāprakarṣe
pratyayasyeṣṭasya saṃgrahe dravyādhāratvāccchakteḥ kāryamukhavat
kāraṇabhūtadravyamukhenāpi kathaṃ na prakarṣa ityāśaṅkya dravyasya
tadabhāvamabhidhātumetaduktamiti vyatireko'nādṛtaḥ tatra
kartṛkarmaṇoḥ kriyā samavaitīti svāśrayasamavotakriyāniṣpatau
tayoḥ kārakatā, karaṇādīnāṃ tu parāśrayasamavetāyāṃ kriyāyāṃ
sādhanabhāvaḥ | na hi karaṇādiṣu kriyā samavaiti | kriyāṇām iti
vyaktibhedāpekṣaṃ bahuvacanam | guṇakriyāpekṣayā vā sarveṣāṃ
svāśrayasamavetakriyāpekṣaṃ sādhanatvam | guṇakriyāyāṃ hi kartāraḥ
sarve| akiṃcitkarasya sādhanatvānupapateḥ | ata eva karaṇaṃ
kārakamityādisādhanasāmānādhikaraṇyopapatiḥ | itthaṃ ca kriyāṇām
iti bahuvacanaṃ suṣṭhua gamitaṃ bhavati | kartṛkarmasthā tu pradhānakriyā
karaṇādīnāmāśrayāntarasamavetā || 1 ||
kathaṃ punaretadavagamyate śaktiḥ sādhanaṃ na
punardravyamityāśaṅkyāha---
śaktimātrāsamūhasya viśvasyānedharmmaṇaḥ |
sarvadā sarvathā bhāvāt kvacit kiṃcid vivakṣyate || 2 ||
(3) ghaṭādayo bhāvā viśvaśabdavācyāḥ | te ca
tatadudakāharaṇādikāryasādhikānāṃ śaktīnāṃ samūharūpāḥ | ata eva
tāḥ śaktayastatra mātrā bhāga iti śaktisamāhāramātraṃ ghaṭādayaḥ | tāśca
śaktayo'nekavidhāḥ | kāścit svahetoreva prabhavanti,
āśrayavināśamanvapi ca vinaśyanti | yathā bodhapradīpādīnāṃ
prakāśaśaktayaḥ | kāścit pauruṣeyyaḥ sthita evāśraye nirudhyante | yathā
balādiśaktayaḥ | tā hi śramābhyāsena vṛṣyaddravyopayogena ca
jāyante | anyāḥ satyevāśraye puruṣaprayatnena vidhāryyante | yathā
viṣasya māraṇaśaktiḥ | bījasyāṅkurajananaśaktiḥ | anyāḥ
prabāvātiśayavatā viparivartyante | yathā yoginā sarvabhāvānāṃ
rūpaparivṛtirādhīyate | kāścit kālaparivāsena vyajyante, yathā
dharmādharmādiśaktiḥ phaladāna ityādyanekavidhamunneyam |
evamanekasvabhāvatve śaktisamudāyasya vicitre kārye yathāśakti bheda
vivakṣāyāṃ pratiniyatasādanabhāvopapatiḥ | dravyasya tu sādhanatve
tasyaikarūpatvāt kāryavaicitryaṃ na syādityarthaḥ | taduktaṃ bhāṣye 
"guṇasamudāyaḥ sādhanaṃ" (g.eṇa. od gh. 3.2.115)
iti | pradhānakriyāyā ekaikaśaktisādhyatvābhāvāt samudāyagrahaṇam |
guṇakriyāpekṣayā tu pratyekaṃ śakteḥ sādhanatā | tasyāśca
pradhānakriyāpekṣayā vaicitryāt karmādirūpatā | yad vā pratyekaṃ
sādhanatve'pi samudāyagrahaṇaṃ śaktīnāṃ sādhanatve tāsāṃ bhedādekasya
kāryabhedopapatiravyāpanārtham | tenānekaśakterapi padārthasya
sadaiva tathāvasthāne'pi kāciccchaktiḥ kvacidudbhūtā vivakṣyata iti
ghaṭṃa paśya, ghaṭenodakamānaya, ghaṭe udakaṃ nidhehītyādi
karmakaraṇādibhāvo niyamenopapadyata iti na kārakasāṅkaryaprasaṅgaḥ
| dravyasya tvekasvabhāvatvāt karmakaraṇāditvaṃ nopapadyata iti
garbhīkṛteyamatra yuktiḥ śakteḥ sādhanatve boddhavyā || 2 ||
nanu ca yatra santi śaktayastatra yuktaṃ yanniyamena vivakṣyeran | yatra
dṛśyante punarasatyastatra kathaṃ tāsāṃ vivakṣā | tadyathā śaktimādadhāti,
śaktyā sādhayati, śaktāvāyatate, śaktervyapaitīti | na hi śakteḥ
śaktyantarayogaḥ, anavasthāpātāt | tathā dhanavināśaṃ karoti,
dhanābhāvena yukta iti nirūpākhye bhāve kā śaktiḥ | sthālyāṃ pacati,
sthālyā pacati, iti sambhavanadhāraṇopakārā sthālī
karaṇādhikaraṇabhinnā kathamityāśaṅkyāha--
sādhanavyavahāraśca buddhyavasthānibandhanaḥ |
sannasan vārtharūpeṣu bhedo buddhyā prakalpyate || 3 ||
(4) vivakṣārūpāyā buddheḥ śabdanimitā ye'vasthāviśeṣāḥ
pratiniyataśabdanibandhanāḥ tadāśrayaḥ kartrādikārakavyavahāraḥ | tathā
hi--nirūpite'rte śabdaprayogād yathānirūpaṇaṃ śabdopapatiḥ |
nirūpaṇaṃ cānapekṣyāpi bāhyamarthaṃ svātantryāt parikalpayati
tāṃstānarthaviśeṣān | tatra śakterdravyāyamāṇāyā buddhayā
svātantryeṇa nirūpaṇāccchabdenābhidhāne śaktyantarayogo viruddhaḥ |
na hi tadā śaktyavasthāyāmeva tasyāṃ tadyogo yenānavasthā syāt,
api tu dravyāvasthāyāmabhāvo'pi buddhyā nirūpitākāro
dravyāyamāṇa eva śabdavācyaḥ | evaṃ tanutarakapālatvāt sthālī
pāke'napekṣitakartṛviniyogot svātantryeṇa vivakṣyata iti kratrī,
indhanopakārānapekṣayā tasyā eva sādhakatamatvavivakṣāyāṃ tu karaṇam,
vāstavādhāravivakṣāyāṃ tvadhikaraṇamiti sarvamupapadyate | ata eva
hantyātmānamātmanā, ātmanaḥ svātmanyavasthānam, ityādayo'pi
prayogāḥ prathitasādhimānaḥ | vakṣyati caitat---
"buddhyaikaṃ bhidyate bhinnam ekatvaṃ copagaccchati "
ityādi | dṛśyavikalpayorabhedādhyavasāyena
vyavahāropapaterbahiranyāvyāvṛtiparatayā samanvayo'tra
samarthanīyaḥ, svātmani nānyasmin, svātmana eva
nāparasyāvasthānamityādi | yathā śilāputrakasya śarīramiti | itthaṃ ca
vākyādapoddhṛtasyāpi padasya saṃskāre prathamata evāprayukte padāntare
vivakṣitasyāpi viśeṣasya rūpasāmānyādanavadhāraṇe saṃbhavatyeva
padāvadhikamanvākhyānaṃ saṃbadhisāmānye'pekṣite | anyathā buddhyā
niyatamartharūpaṃ kriyādisaṃbandhyapekṣaṃ kathaṃ nirūpyeta |
anapekṣitasaṃbandhirūpasyārthasāmānyasyābhāvādabhyupagataṃ
padāvadhikamanvākhyānam | śāstre 
"dhātusaṃbandhe pratyayāḥ"(gh. 3.4.1)
ityevamādīnāṃ pratyākhyānāt | yatra ca sarvathā'saṅgatiḥ
sthālīmodanena kāṣṭhe pacatītyādau tad vākyameva na bhavatīti
kataṃ vākyādapoddhṛtānāṃ padānāmanvākhyāne'tra prasaṅgaḥ | ata eva
cākṣīṇi me darśanīyānītyāderviprayogasya nivṛtaye
śāstramarthavadeva | sāmānyamapi hi
padārtharūpamarthāntarasaṃsargayogyameva saṃstūyate, na tvaṅgulyagre
kariśatamāsta ityādyasamanvitameva | evaṃ ca kṛtvā cchandasi 
"caṣālaṃ ye aśvayūpāya takṣati"
ityādavasādhutvaśaṅkāpanutaye vyatyayo'rthavāneva |
niyatānupūrvīkasya cchandovākyasya saṃbhavātatastathaiva padāpoddhārasya
nyāyyatvāt | loke tu nānavasthitaṃ niyatānupūrvīkamasaṅgata
vākyamiti na tato'poddhāraprasaṅgaḥ | tasmāddhākyādapoddhṛtya
padānuśāsane'pi nirdoṣameva padāvadhikamanvākhyānam | tatra ca
samastaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭasyārthasya niraṃśatvādapoddhāre sāmānyamātrā
buddhiparikalpitaśarīraiva | upakrama eva tu viśeṣāpekṣāyāṃ
vākyāvadhikamanvākhyānaṃ kathyate | na tu vākyasyānantyāt
sākṣādanvākhyānaṃ yujyate | prakṛtipratyayādivibhāgakalpanayā hi
padasyaivānuśāsanumupapadyate | tasya ca kevalasyāsaṃbhavādvākyasthatve
saṃbandhisāmānyāpekṣāyāṃ padāvadhikaṃ
tadvākyopātasaṃbandhiviśeṣāpekṣāyāṃ tu
vākyāvadhikamanvākhyānamitīyānanayoḥ pakṣayoḥ viśeṣaḥ || 3 ||
bauddhe sādhanavyavahāre bhāṣyakārābhyupagamasaṃvādakamāha---
buddhayā samāhitaikatvān pañcālān kurubhiryadā |
punarvibhajate vaktā tadā'pāyaḥ pratīyate || 4 ||
(5) kurubhyaḥ pañcālā abhirūpatarā iti 
"pañcamī vibhakte" (gh. 2.3.42)
iti pañcamyarthaṃ sūtraṃ bhāṣye pratyākhyātam, bauddhasyāpāyasya
sambhavādapādāna ityeva siddheḥ | tathā hyābhirūpyeṇa samānena guṇena
kurubhiḥ saha pañcālān buddhyaikatvaṃ
samudāyabhāvamāpādyātiśayābhirūpatāvaśāt kurubhyaḥ pañcālāḥ pṛthak
kriyanta ityasti saṃśleṣapūrvako viśleṣo'pāyaḥ | taduktaṃ bhāṣye 
"yastaiḥ sāmyaṃ gatavān sa evaṃ prayuṅkte"(g.eṇa.od gh. 1.4.24)
iti | taiḥ kurubhiḥ paṃcālānāṃ sādṛśyaṃ
sampradhāraṇāsāmarthyādābhirūpyeṇa prathamaṃ yo jñātavānityarthaḥ | anena
caikabuddhiniveśitvena kurupaṃcālānāṃ deśapratyāsatimāha
bhāṣyakāraḥ | tatpūrvakastu sātiśayasya niratiśayādvibhāgaḥ |
paṃcamīśabdo'pādānasaṃjñānatvānnokto bhāṣyakṛtā |
sūtrāraṃbhavādināpi nirdhāraṇāśrayatvenaiṣāṃ buddhyaiva
samudāyabhāvaḥ samānaguṇatvādāśrayaṇīyaḥ | anyathā
nirdhāraṇādhikārādeva vibhāge labdhe
vibhaktagrahaṇādatthantavibhāgapratītau na vāstavaḥ samudāyabhāvo
yujyate | tadevaṃ bauddhaḥ sādhanabhāvo'smādavasīyate | kārakaprakaraṇe
ca tamabgrahaṇāt prakarṣo na vivakṣyata iti
gauṇe'pyatrāpāye'pādānasaṃjñā bhavati || 4 ||
anyadapi bhāṣyoktaṃ saṃvādakamāha--
śabdopahitarūpāśca buddherviṣayatāṃ gatān |
pratyakṣamiva kaṃsādīn sādhanatvena manyate || 5 ||
(6) "hetumati ca" (gh. 3.1.26)
ityatra
"ākhyānāt kṛtastadācaṣṭe...."(ja. od gh. 3.1.26)
ityevamādyaupasaṃkhyānaṃ 
"na vā sāmānyakṛtatvād dhetuto hy aviśiṣṭam"(ja. od gh.  3.1.26)
ityevaṃ pratyākhyātam | citrapustādau hi pratikṛtirūpasya
prayojyaprayojakabhāvādhyavasāyāt kaṃsaṃ ghātayati, baliṃ bandhayatīti
siddham | evaṃ kṛtānukaraṇe nāṭaḷe'pi naṭānāṃ
kaṃsavāsudevānukāreṇa sādṛśyātadrūpatopapatiḥ | tatra
" granthikeṣu katham "(g.eṇa. od gh. 3.1.26)
iti coditam | tatra granthikāḥ kathakā iti vṛtarūpeṇaiva
kaṃsavadhamācakṣate | tatra ca pratikṛtirūpasyābhāvāt kathaṃ
prayojyaprayojakatvamiti paryanuyogaḥ | atra parihāra uktaḥ |
"te'pi hi teṣāmutpatipṛbhṛtyā vināśādṛddhīrvyācakṣāṇāḥ sato
buddhiviṣayān prakāśayanti " (g. eṇa. od gh. 3.1.26)
iti cirakālātītatvāt kaṃsādīnāṃ bahirasatvāt buddhigocaratayā
sato vidyamānāṃstān prakāśayanti śabdenāvabhāsayanti |
buddhipratibhāsyeva hyākāro śabdārtho na vastvarthaḥ | tathā ca kathakaḥ
śrotari kaṃsādyākārapratyayajanatāt buddhivāṃsudevena buddhikaṃsaṃ
ghātayatīti prayojakatvasamāropāt prayogopapatiḥ | tathā
hi-kathakodīritaśabdasāmarthyopajātarūpaviśeṣān
śrotṛbuddhigocaramāpannān savyāpārarūpān kaṃsādīn
karmādisādhanatayā bhāṣyakāro manyata iti buddhyavasthānibandhanaḥ
sādhanavyavahāraḥ siddhaḥ | gauṇe'pi ca prayojake kārakaprakaraṇe
prākarṣānāśrayāddhetusaṃjñāyāṃ tadvayāpāre'tra ṇic sautraḥ | pratyakṣam
ityavyayībhāvaḥ | mananakriyāviśeṣaṇaṃ caitat | indriyanimitena
jñānena parisphuṭānavadhārayatītyarthaḥ | etacca 'ṛddhīrvyācakṣāṇā' iti
bhāṣyāsyābhiprāyavyākhyānam | prabhāvātiśayayukte hi
vastunyādaravaśāt spaṣṭarūpaḥ pratyayaḥ prasūyate | spaṣṭe ca
jñāne'vabhāsamāna ākāro bāhyātmako'vasīyate || 5 ||
tadevaṃ buddhyā sādhanavyavahāropapatau ekasyāpi vastunaḥ
śaktinānātvaṃ bauddhamupapadyata iti nigamayati | 
buddhipravṛtirūpaṃ ca samāropyābhidhātṛbhiḥ |
artheṣu śaktibhedānāṃ kriyate parikalpanā || 6 ||
(7) buddheḥ pravṛtiḥ vyāpāraḥ paricchedalakṣaṇaḥ | tasyā rūpamākāro
viṣaya ābhāsamānaḥ | tamartheṣu bāhyaeṣvadhyasya teṣāṃ śaktinānātvaṃ
parikalpyate vaktṛbhiḥ | ayayarthaḥ |
dṛśyavikalpayorabhedādhyavasāyād bauddhaḥ śaktibhedo bāhya
evetyabhimānāt tatra vicitraṃ sādhanabāvamabhimanyante |
buddhipratibhāsasya svapratiṣṭhatvenānavagamātatra kriyābhedaṃ
pratipatṛvyapekṣāṃ cānuvartya vastvena nānāśaktikamavagamyata iti
bahirarthe śabdaḥ pramāṇam | kriyākārakasaṃsargasya bādhakābhāvāt sati
saṃvāde ca vākyasya pramāṇyamavagamayanti vyavahartāraḥ | saugatānāṃ
vikalpapratibiṃbasya bhedānadhyalasāyāt bahiṣvapravṛtiḥ | prāmāṇyaṃ tu
vaktrābhiprayā eva śabdānāṃ na bāhyae vyabhicārāśaṅkanāt,
anyavyāvṛtimātraniṣṭhatā tu bahiḥ | vaiyākaraṇānāṃ tu
vyāvṛtavastuviṣayatā, tathādhyavasāyāt tatraiva prāmāṇyamiti
darśanabhedaḥ | niyatopādiniyamitatvācca na sāṅkaryam |
upakārakāśca śaktayaḥ pratyupādhibhinnāḥ
saṃmūrccchitaśarīrātvādvastunastatvānyatvābhyāmanirvācyā iti
nānupādhyupakārāṅgatvāderaprasaṅgaḥ || 6 ||
buddhyāvasthāsamāśraye ca sādhanabhāve satīdamapi siddhaṃ
bhavatītyāha---
vyaktau padārthe śabdāderjanyamānasya karmaṇaḥ |
sādhanatvaṃ tathā siddhaṃ buddhirūpaprakalpitam || 7 ||
(8) jātau padārthe tajjātīyābhedenānāgataṃ nirūpya
yogyasādhanaviniyogājjātidvāreṇa kriyāsiddhāvaṅgatā
vyaktidvāreṇa tu nirvartyamānatā karmaṇo yujyata ityuktam 
"nirvartyamānaṃ yatkarma jātistatrāpi sādhanam "
iti | vyaktau tu padena pratyāyyāyāṃ śabdaṃ janayati saṃyogaṃ
karotītyādau janyamānasya nirvartyasya śabdādeḥ
karmaṇo'nāgatasyāpi vartamānavyaktisārūpyādasatyapi jāteḥ
pratyāyane bhedānadhyavasāyād buddhyākāramullikhya
yogyasādhanaviniyogāt bauddhasyaiva bahīrūpatādhyavasāyād bāhyaena
rūpeṇa nirvartyamānatā, bauddhena tu sādhanabhāvo yujyata ityarthaḥ |
janyamānasyetyanena vikāryaprāpyayorvyavacchedaḥ || 6 ||
vikāryaṃ hi prakṛtirūpeṇa sthitameva | prāpyamapi svata eva kiyadvā
buddhyā nādhyāropyata ityāha---
svatantraparatantratve kramarūpaṃ ca darśitam |
nirīheṣvavi bhāveṣu kalpanopanibandhanam || 8 ||
(9) sadasatpakṣabhedena kāryakāraṇabhāvasya nirākṛtatvāt sarve
bhāvāḥ kriyārahitāśceṣṭāśūnyāḥ | tatra kriyayāṃ
svātantryapāratantryādilakṣaṇaṃ kartṛkaraṇādirūpaṃ kārakatvam |
kriyārūpameva ca sakramatvaṃ yathāvabāsamanādimithyābhyāsavāsanāvaśena
kalpanābuddhyuparacitasatatvam | apūrvāparasyāpi bhāvato viśvasya
pūrvāparatayā svātantryādinātrāvabhāsanāditi
mityāvabhāso'yamartheṣu kriyākārakabhāva ityarthaḥ | advaitanaye hi
sarvamupaplavate, asatyatvāditi vikalpaparighaṭitaḥ śābdo
vyavahāro yathāpratītyavicāritasiddho'bhyupagantavya iti
digādipadārthavat sādhanapadārthasiddhiḥ |ṭhdarśitam'
ityadvaitanayāvalambibhirbhāṣyakāraprabhṛtibhiḥ, "kūlaṃ pipatiṣati"
ityādiprayogasiddhyarthamākhyātamityarthaḥ || 8 ||
evaṃ tāvad dravyavyatiriktaśaktidarśanena sādhanaṃ lakṣitam |
avyatiriktaśaktidarśanenāpyāha---
śaktayaḥ śaktiṃmantaśra sarve saṃsargavādinām |
bhāvāsteṣvasvaśabdeṣu sādhanatvaṃ nirūpyate || 9 
(10) samavāyavaśāddharmadharmiṇorabhedamiva bhede'pi ye vadanti te
saṃsargavādino vaiśeṣikaḥ | te ca svarūpaṃ sahakāriṇaśca bhāvānāṃ
svakāryajanane śaktiriti pratipannāḥ | sahakāriṇaḥ śaktiḥ, kāraṇaṃ
śaktimat | evaṃ sahakāriṇāmapi kāraṇaṃ śaktiḥ svakārye sahakāri
rūpamiti śaktiśaktimantaḥ sarva eva bhāvāḥ, na tvanyā kācidatīndriyā
śaktiḥ ṣa
ṭpadārthalyatiriktā vidyate | evamapi sādhanatvaṃ bhāvānāṃ
padārthānāmapādānādiśabdairna pratyāyyate, api tu vibhaktibhireva |
tathā ca pratyāyanaṃ yogyatāmātreṇa tvapādānādiśabdapravṛtiḥ | 'ghaṭṃa
paśyati' ityādāveva dvitīyādibhirmahatvādyādhāraniyataṃ pratyāyyamānaṃ
śaktiḥ sādhanaṃ bhavati | ādhārānavaccchinnastu mahatvādiḥ
karmādiśabdavācyo na kiṃcanāpi sādhayatīti
vibhaktyekasamadhigamyaḥ | prātipadikādādhāravācinastadutpateḥ |
ādhārasamavāye sādhanatvasya saṃsparśādupakārāvasthāyāṃ
śaktitvamupakāryāvasthāyāṃ tu śaktimatvaṃ pramāṇadṛṣṭamiti na
kācidanupapatiḥ | ata eva śaktyavasthāyāṃ
śaktyantaremāyogādanavasthāpi nāsti | abhāvasyāsmin darśane
spatamapadārtharūpatvena bhāvyatvāccchaktiyogo'viruddhaḥ || 9 ||
tatra ko bhāvaḥ kasya kva śaktirityudāharaṇamāha--
ghaṭasya dṛśikarmatve mahatvādīni sādhanam |
rūpasya dṛśikarmatve rūpatvādīni sādhanam || 10 ||
(11) 'ghaṭṃa paśyati' iti darśanakriyākarmabhāvāpannasya ghaṭadravyasya
mahatvānekadravyavatvarūpāṇi śaktisvasamavotāni sādhanaṃ karmakārakam
| tathā hi kaṇādasūtram ---
"mahatyanekadravyavatvādrūpāccopalabdhiḥ" (jat.cdv.4.1.6)
iti | asyārthaḥ | anekadravyaṃ samavāyikāraṇaṃ yasya tadanekadravyaṃ
dvayaṇukādi | tadyasyāsti tadārabdhasya tadanekadravyavat |
tadbhāvastatvam | tasmāddhetorupalabdhirmahati dravye | tena
bahuvrīhiṇā matupā ca dvau matvarthāvuktau | tatraikena
paramāṇoryavvacchedaḥ | sa hi rūpavānapyanekasya
dravyasyāraṃbhakasyābhāvānnopalabhyate | apareṇa tu matvarthena
dvayaṇurādervyavacchedaḥ | ata eva sūtrāntaramuktam ---
"adravyatvātparamāṇāvanupalabdhiḥ"
iti | paramāṇugrahaṇamatrādṛśyamānasyopalakṣaṇārtham | mahati
dṛśyamāne cānekadravyavatvaṃ darśanaheturiti tryaṇukasya
kāraṇabahutvamahatvapracayebhyo mahatvasyotpatiriti
tribhirdvyaṇukairāraṃbhānmagato'nekadravyavatve'pi tadabhāvaḥ |
mahato hi dṛśyamānasyānuvādenedaṃ vidhīyata ityadṛsye nāyane
raśmau nāsyādṛśyatāpatiḥ | na hi mahatvamapyatra darśanakāraṇaṃ
vidhīyate | evaṃ hyaekasyā ākṛteścaritaḥ prayoga iti nyāyāt prakramo
bhedārthaḥ paṃcamyeva kriyeta | anekadravyavataśca mahatvāvyabhicārān
'mahati' iti na hetunirdeaśaḥ, api tu viṣayopalakṣaṇameva | 'rūpāt'
iti ca dvitīyo darśanahetuḥ | nīrūpasya
vāṇormahato'nekadravyavataścāpyanupalaṃbhāt | taduktaṃ sūtram---
"rūpasaṃskārābhāvācca vāyāvanupalabdhiḥ"(jat cdv.4.7.1)
iti | yataścānekadravyatvasya rūpasya darśanahetutvaṃ na mahatvasyāpi
tato dvayoreva vyatirekaḥ pradarśitaḥ | mahatvasyākāraṇatve'pi ca
tadviṣayatvāddarśanasya pāramparyeṇopayogān 'mahatvādīni sādhanam'
iti bahuvacanam | yadvā dravyatvādīnāṃ
sāmānyaviśeṣāṇāmapyādiśabdena saṅgrahaṇāt bahuvacanam | atra
sāmānyaviśeṣā hi pradhānaṃ darśananibandhanam |
tadupādhikatvenārthānām, pararūpavyāvṛtisyātmana upalaṃbhāt |
'mahati ityeva tūkte vyabhicāra ākāśādinetyanekadravyavatvāditi
matupā'vayavidravye pratītirabhivyajyate | 'anekadravyavatvāt' iti
punarucyamāne tatpuruṣasaṃbhāvanayā dravyapracayamātraṃ gamyeta | 'rūpaṃ
paśyati' ityatra rūpasya darśanakriyāyāṃ rūpatvaṃ svasamavetaṃ
sāmānyamanekadravye ca dravye samavāyaḥ sādhanam | tathā ca sūtram---
"anekadravyasamavāyādrūpaviśeṣācca rūpopalabdhiḥ" (jat. cdv.4.1.8)
iti | aneke āraṃbhakā yasyāvayavidravyasya tadanekadravyam, tena
samavāyāt, tatra samavetatvāt | rūpasya viśeṣa udbhūtatvam | ato
nāyanaraśmirūpaṃ bāhyataijasadravyarūpeṇābhibhūtamapohyate | anye tu rūpaṃ
viśeṣyate vyāvartyate'nenānyasmādrasāderiti rūpatvameva
sāmānyaviśeṣa evamupadiṣṭa iti varṇayanti | anekadravyatvābhāvācca
paramāṇostadgatarūpasyādṛśyatā | yogyadeśatvādayo'pi
sāmarthyāt sādhanamiti bahuvacananirdeaśaḥ || 10 ||
svaiḥ sāmānyaviśeṣaiśca śaktimanto rasādayaḥ |
niyatagrahaṇā loke śaktayastāstathāśrayaiḥ || 11 ||
(12) rasaṃ rasayati, gandhaṃ jighrati , sparśaṃ spṛśati, śabdaṃ śṛṇoti
iti ra,tvādayaḥ sāmānyaviśeṣāḥ samavetāḥ rasanādīnāṃ
rasanādikriyāsādhanam | tathā hi taistaiḥ niyatagrahaṇāḥ niyataṃ
svajātibalādeva gṛhyante, na tu dravyavadaniyatagrahaṇāḥ | dravyaṃ hi
kadācitsāmānyopādinā gṛhyate 'dravyam' iti, kadācid guṇena
śuklamiti, kadācit karmaṇā 'gaccchati' iti | rasatvādīnāṃ tu
śaktīnāṃ svādhārā evāvacchedakāḥ svarūpagrahaṇe śaktiḥ | yathā hi
rasasyopalaṃbhe rastavamapyupalabhyate tadviśiṣṭasya
tasyopalaṃbhādityāśritākṣepaḥ tathā rasatvopalaṃbhe'pi svāśrayākṣepaḥ
| tathā rasasyopalaṃbhe tadāśrayaṃ dravyamapi śaktirityāpi jñeyam |
pūrvavaccānekadravye samavāyo'pi gṛhyate, paramāṇugatasya
rasāderanupalaṃbhāt | evaṃ śeṣeṣvapi vācyam | itthaṃ parasparaṃ
śaktiśaktimanto bhāvāḥ | tathā ca na kācinniyatā śaktiḥ | saṃsargiṇāṃ
parasparaṃ śaktiśaktimanto bhāvāḥ | tathā ca na kācinniyatā śaktiḥ |
saṃsargiṇāṃ parasparaṃ tatvāt, anyasya ca yogyadeśatvādeḥ
sahakāriṇaḥ tathātvāt || 11 ||
indriyārthamanaḥ kartṛsaṃbandhaḥ sādhanaṃ kvacit |
yadyadā yadanugrāhi tatadā tatra sādhanam || 12 ||
(13)ātmendriyamano'rthasannikarṣāt jñānaṃ niṣpadyate rūpādāviti
saṃyoga eva sādhanam | sukhādau tvātmāntaḥ karaṇasaṃyogāditi
'kvacit' ityāha | kiṃ vātra parigaṇanayā, yadyatropakaroti tatatra
sādhanamiti yathāyogaṃ sarve bhāvāḥ śaktiśaktimantaḥ | tathā hi saṃkhyāḥ
parimāṇāni pṛthaktvaṃ saṃyogavibhāgau karma ca
rūpadrivyasamavāyāccākṣuṣāṇyuktāni | avayavānāmavayaviniṣpatau
saṃyogaḥ śaktiḥ | guṇaniṣpatau tvavayavinaḥ samavāyaḥ śaktiḥ iti
paropakāri paratantraṃ sarvaṃ śaktilakṣaṇamanupatati || 12 ||
sā ca svakāryaparatantrāvasthā sādhanādinā siddhasvabhāvārthābhidhāyinā
śabdena nābhidhātuṃ śakyate , api tu yathāyathaṃ vibhaktyādirasyāḥ
pratyāyane samartha ityāha--
svaśabdairabhidhāne tu sa dharmo nābhidhīyate |
vibhaktyādibhirevāsāvupakāraḥ pratīyate || 13 ||
(14) sa dharmaḥ, kriyāṃ prati śeṣabhāvaḥ | kārakaṃ
sādhanamapādānamityādiśabdaiḥ kriyopakāropalakṣitaṃ
dravyamabhidhīyate prādhānyena, na tu kriyopakāralakṣaṇaḥ sādhanabhāvaḥ
samudbhūto'smāt pratīyate | tathā hi-atrāpi dravyarūpatayā
sādhanamāmukhīkaroti | kriyopakāralakṣaṇo dharmo dvitīyāṃ
pratipādyata iti parigṛhītakriyopakāraṃ dvitīyādivibhaktipratipādyaṃ
vastviti śaktirvibhaktivācyā | kvacitaddhitavācyā | śatena krītaḥ śatyaḥ
śatika iti kraye sādhakatamatvaṃ pratyayavācyam | 'yatra' 'tatra'
ityādhārabhāvastralvācyaḥ | payaḥ payo jarayati, madhu nirīkṣate,
dadhi samaśnāti iti prātipadikādeva śaktiḥ pratīyate | antarā tvāṃ ca
māṃ ca kamaṇḍaluparityantarāśabdo'vyayo'dhikaraṇamāhetyādigrahaṇam
|| 13 ||
kaḥ punarasāvupakāralakṣaṇo dharmaḥ sādhanamityāha---
nimitabhāvo bhāvānāmupakārārthamāśritaḥ |
natirāvarjanetyevaṃ siddhaḥ sādhanamiṣyate || 14 ||
(15) sādhyasvābhāvāyāṃ kriyāyāmupakārāya siddhasvabhāvānāṃ bhāvānāṃ
nimitabhāvo hetubhāvaḥ | natiḥ namanamupakārābhimukhyam,
'āvarjanam' aṅgabhāvena svātmapratyarpaṇam,
kriyā#epajananatātparyyam | iti śabdaḥ prakāre | ityevamādibhiḥ
śabdairabhidhīyamāno'nyo'pi siddhaḥ niṣpannatayā pratīyamānaḥ, evaṃ
bhūtena vā dharmeṇa niścitaḥ, na vastu saṃbhavamātreṇa sādhanamityucyate
|| 14 ||
evaṃ mīmāṃsakavaiśeṣikadarśanena śaktiḥ sādhanamityupapādya svadarśane
vyatirekāvyatirekagrahaṃ tyajati |
sa tebhyo vyatirikto vā teṣāmātmaiva vā tathā |
vyatirekamupāśritya sādhanatvena kalpyate || 15 ||
(16) 'saḥ' iti hetubhāvaḥ śaktyaparaparyāyaḥ |
"śaktayaḥ sarvabhāvānāṃ kāryārthāpatigocarāḥ"
ityatīndriyāḥ padārthād vyatiriktāḥ śaktayaḥ keṣāṃcid bhavantu |
sahakārisamavadhānopakṛto bhāvaḥ svarūpeṇaiva kāryajanakaḥ
kimadṛṣṭaśaktiparikalpanenetyātmaiva bhāvānāṃ śaktiḥ | saṃsargiṇaḥ
sahakāriṇo vārthāntarabhūtāḥ | svakāraṇaparaṃparayā hi padārthaḥ
samupajāto viśiṣṭo yastatatsahakārisaṃparkea tatatkāryajanane śakta
iti kimadṛṣṭayā vyatiriktyā śaktyā | tatkalpane tasyā
niyamaheturbhāvasvabhāva eva vaktavya iti varaṃ sa evāstu |
tadatrāvyatirekavādo vāstu vyatiriktā vā śaktayaḥ santu |
nāsmākamatra kaścid graho vyatirekāvyatirekavāde | śabdapramāṇakā
hi vayam, yaccchabda āha tadasmākaṃ pramāṇam | śabdaśca padārtānāṃ
vyatiriktameva sādhanabhāvaṃ pratyāyayati ghaṭasya dṛśikriyāyāṃ karmabhāva
ityādāviti vyatirekopāśrayaḥ śabdanibandhano vyavahāraḥ | tathā ca
prātipadikena dravye'bhihite śaktau kṛtabandhā śaktiḥ pravartate || 15 ||
"yad yadā yadanugrāhi tatadā tatra sādhanam"
iti vyāptimuktāṃ kriyāyā api sādhanatvena bhāṣyakārābhimatena
saṃvādayitumāha--
saṃdarśanaṃ prārthanāyāṃ vyavasāye tvanantarā |
vyavasāyastathāraṃbhe sādhanatvāya kalpate || 16 ||
pūrvasmin yā kriyā saiva parasmin sādhanaṃ matā |
sandarśane tu caitanyaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ sādhanaṃ viduḥ || 17 ||
(17) saṃpradānasaṃjñāyām 
"kriyāgrahaṇamapi kartavyam" (ja od gh. 1.4 32)
ityukte iha kaścit kaṃcidarthaṃ sampaśyati, sa dṛṣṭvā prārthanayā
vyavasāyaṃ karoti, adhyavasāye prāraṃbhaḥ, prāraṃbhe phalaprāptiriti
sandarśanādibhirāpyamānatvāt "kriyāpi kṛtrimaṃ karma" iti bhāṣya
uktam | tatra sādhyasvabhāvāyā api sandarśanādibhirāpyamānatvāt
"kriyāpi kṛtrimaṃ karma" iti bhāṣya uktam | tatra sādhyasvabhāvāyā api
sandarśanādikriyāyāḥ sādhanatvamāpekṣikam | samyak samantādvā
darśanaṃ sandarśanaṃ phalaviṣayaḥ saṃkalpaḥ | tat prārthanāyāṃ
phalopāyaviṣaye'bhilāṣe, sādhanatvāya kalpate, sādhanaṃ bhavati |
arthadarśane hi satyabhilāṣotpatiḥ | vyavasāyo dṛḍhagrāhitā,
kriyāviśeṣasya ulasādhanatvena niścayaḥ | tatra 'anantarā' prārthanā
sādhanam, abhilāṣe sati niścayakaraṇāt | vyavasāyaścāṃrabhakriyāyāṃ
mānasyāṃ pravṛtau sādhanam, vyavasāyabalenārthakriyākaraṇāṃrabhāt |
tayā ca kriyayā''pyamānatvād bhautikī kriyā karma bhavati saṃkṣepataḥ
| pūrvasmin pūrvasmin vyāpāre'pekṣite yā kriyā kartavyatvāt
saiva pūrvasmānnivṛtā pūrvakālabhāvinī kriyā parasmin
utarotarabhāvini sādhye sādhanaṃ bhavati | tadātmalābhe
siddhatvenopayogāt | tadayamarthaḥ | pūrvasmin sandarśane yā kriyā
prārthanādikā saivotarāṃ prati sādhanaṃ matā | atha sandarśanakriyāyāḥ
kiṃ sādhanamityāha---
"sandarśane tu caitanyam"
iti | 'tuḥ' viśeṣadyotakaḥ | cidgūpa ātmā yo bhuṅkte bhāvān |
viśeṣo'sya phalamilāṣayogyatā | yadyapi ca kriyā
sādhyasvabhāvā'niṣpannā tathāpi niṣṭhāvasthāyāṃ siddhatvāt,
sādhanaśabdena ca śaktestatrāsamavāye'pi vyatirekeṇāśrayād yuktaḥ
sādhanabhāvaḥ | kriyāyāṃ ca kriyāntarasyāsamavetatvāt saiva
kriyāntarabhāvane kartuḥ karaṇam | ayamatra paramārthaḥ | yadapekṣayā
yat siddhaṃ tatasya sādhanaṃ niṣpādakaṃ kārakaṃ bhavati | tadānīṃ hi
kartuḥ kriyāniṣpatau karaṇāntaraṃ nāsti | bhāvī cāṃśo
bhūtenāṃsenāpyamānatvāt karma bhavati | kartā hi kriyayā yadīpsati
tatkarma | atra ca kriyāṃśa evepsyate'pareṇa siddhena
kriyāṃśenetyāraṃbheṇa mānasavyāpāreṇāpyamānatvād bhautikī kriyā
karmeti tenābhipreyamāṇasya saṃpradānatvaṃ siddham, 'yuddhāya sannahyate',
'śrāddhāya nigahlate' iti | gatyarthadhātuviṣaye saṃdarśanādīnāṃ
bhedābhedavivakṣā, pacyādāvabhedavivakṣaiva, śrāddhāya nigahlata ityādau
bhedavivakṣaiveti ca vakṣyate | tadevaṃ 'vyatirekamupāśritya
sādhanatvena kalpata' iti kriyāyā api sādhyasādhanabhāvo
vyatirekāśrayeṇopakāryopakārakabhāvena bhāṣyokta udāhṛta iti
sthitametat śaktivyatirikta#a kriyopakārārthamāśritā sādhanamiti
sāmānyenocyata iti || 16,17 ||
vārtike kārakamiti saṃjñā sādhanamiti saṃjñinirdeaśena kartavyā |
anvarthasaṃjñāvijñānādvā sādhakasya lābha iti |
"anvarthamiticedakartari kartṛśabdānupapatiḥ"
(ja. od gh. 1.4.23)
ityuktam | tatrākartari karaṇādau kartṛśabdasya kārakamityasyopapatiṃ
samarthayitumāha-- niṣpatimātre kartṛtvaṃ sarvatraivāsti kārake |
vyāpārabhedāpekṣāyāṃ karaṇāditvasaṃbhavaḥ || 18 ||
(18) sāmagrīsādhyatvāt kriyāṇāṃ sarve kārakatvaṃ
kriyāniṣpativiṣayabhedena pratipadyante iti | yadā tu kaḥ kathaṃ
pacatīti vivakṣā tadā pradhānakriyāniṣpādane vicitravyāpārāṇi
kārakāṇi yathāsvaṃ vyāpārātiśayavaśena karaṇādisaṃjñāḥ pratilabhante
| tathā hi sādhanāntaraviniyogavyāpāraḥ kartā |
nirvṛtivikāraprāptyāhitasaṃskāraṃ kartuḥ kriyeyapsitatamaṃ karma |
jvalanādyanubhavat kārakāntaravyāpārairavyavahitavyāpāraṃ karaṇam |
preraṇānumananānirākaraṇavyāpāraṃ karmaṇā saṃbandhayamānaṃ
saṃpradānam | apāyamavadhibhāvopagamanena sādhayadapādānam |
rtṛkarmavyavahitakriyādhārakamadhikaraṇam | svatantraṃ preṣayaddheturiti
| sāmānyapūrvakatvācca viśeṣasyāvāntaravyāpāre'pi
kārakatvamanuvṛtimiti karaṇaṃ kārakamiti sāmānādikaraṇyopapatiḥ
| akārakasya ca viśeṣasaṃjñābhāvaḥ, vṛkṣasya parṇaṃ patatīti | anapekṣite
ca kartṛvyāpāre sthālī pacati, edhāḥ pacanti, asiścchinati iti
pradhānakriyāyāmevaiṣāṃ kartṛtvam | saṃpradānāpādānayoḥ
svavyāpāre svātantryavivakṣā nāsti |
dvitīyasyādāturapagantuścāpekṣaṇāt | ata eva 
"siddhaṃ tu pratikārakaṃ kriyābhedāt..."(ja. od gh. 1.4.23)
iti pradhānakriyārtheṣu yathāsvaṃ kārakavyāpāreṣu pacādīnāmeva
vṛtirvarṇitā | tadetadbhāṣya uktam---
"sāmānyabhūtā kriyā vartate, tasyā nirvartakaṃ kārakam"
(g.eṇa. od gh. 1.4.23)
iti || 18 ||
atraiva dṛṣṭāntamāha---
putrasya janmani yathā pitroḥ kartṛtvamucyate |
ayamasyāmiyaṃ tvasmāditi bhedo vivakṣayā || 19 ||
(19) pitarau putraṃ janayata ityavivakṣite jananavyāpārabhede
janikriyā kartṛtvamubhayoḥ prasiddham | kaḥ kathaṃ janayatīti
bhedavivakṣāyāmayaṃ pitā bījamādadhadasyāṃ mātari garbhamādhate | iyaṃ
tu mātā'smāt pituḥ patad bījaṃ dhārayantī
janikriyākartṛtvamanubhavatītyadhikaraṇatvaṃ mātuḥ |
kartṛtvamapādānatvaṃ ca pituḥ, kartṛtvaṃ māturiti bhidyate pravṛtiḥ |
sāmānyapūrvakatvācca viśeṣasya jani sāmānyenātmalābhavacano
viśeṣaviṣaye preṣaṇaviṣayabījadhāraṇabījacyutivacanaḥ | yadvā
prakṛtyarto'viśiṣṭaḥ, pratyayārthastu ṇijartho janayatīti preṣaṇādiḥ
yatrāyaṃ vibhāgaḥ | sādhayapakṣanikṣiptamapīdaṃ prasiddhatvānnidarśanam ||
19 ||
tadevaṃ pradhānakriyāyāmeva sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvena kārakaṃ
karaṇamityādisāmānādhikaraṇyamupapāditam | idānīṃ
guṇakriyāpekṣayā svātantryaṃ pradhānakriyāpekṣayāsvātantrye
satyapyanapagatamiti prakārāntareṇa 
'sāmānyabhūtā kriyā vartate' (g.eṇa. od gh. 1.4.23)
ityetadbhāṣyaṃ gamayitumāha---
guṇakriyāṇāṃ kartāraḥ kartrā nyakkṛtaśaktayaḥ |
nyaktāyāmapi sampūrṇaiḥ svairvyāpāraiḥ samanvitāḥ || 20 ||
karaṇatvādibhirjñātāḥ kriyābhedānupātibhiḥ |
svātantryamutaraṃ labdhvā pradhāne yānti kartṛtām || 21 ||
(20) jvalādyavāntaravyāpārarūpāṇāṃ guṇakriyāṇāṃ svātantryāt sarve
kartāraḥ, pradhānakriyāviṣaye tu kartrā svatantreṇa viniyoktrā
nyakkṛtā śaktiḥ svātantryameṣāmiti tena yathāyathaṃ
nyagbhāvamasvātantryamāpāditāḥ santaḥ nyaktāyāṃ nyagbhāve'pi
svavyāpāre svātantryānapagamāt akiṃcitkarasya kriyāsiddhau
hetubhāvānupapateḥ yathāsvaṃ svavyāpārānuṣṭhāne
pradhānakriyāṅgabhāvagamanāt kartṛviniyāge'pi yathāyathamātmīyaiḥ
sarvaireva jvalanādivanyāpārairaviyuktāḥ tatkriyāviśeṣaviṣayaiḥ
karaṇādisaṃjñārūpairadhigatāḥ tadrūpamatyajanta iva
viniyogotarakālamapi svavyāpārānuṣṭhānalakṣaṇaṃ svādhīnatvāmāsādya
svasmin kriyābhede svatantrāḥ pradhānakriyāyāmeva kartṛtāṃ kārakatvaṃ
bhajanta iti karaṇaṃ kārakamityādyaupapatirityarthaḥ | karaṇādīni hi
kartṛpraiṣe nyagbhūtāni svavyāpāramanutiṣṭhanti tathā bhavantīti kartrā
svavyāpāre kartṛtvārthamevaiṣāṃ niyogāt, niyuktānāṃ satāṃ
svavyāpāre svātantryamanubhavatāṃ karayaṇāditve
pradhānakriyāniṣpatirityavirodhaḥ svātantryapāratantryayoḥ | yadeva hi
svavyāpāre svātantryaṃ tadeva pradhānakriyāyāṃ pāratantrya#ṃ nānyat
kiṃcit | tacca kratṛviniyoge satyutarakālameva bhavati | pūrvantu
kartṛviniyogādyogyatāmātreṇa svātantryamucyate | itthaṃ ca karaṇaṃ
kārakamiti viṣayabhedo'pi nāsti| svavyāpāre, svātantryasyaiva
pradhānakriyāyāṃ karaṇāditvāt | tadetataduktam |
"siddhaṃ tu pratikārakaṃ kriyābhedāt pacādīnāṃ karaṇīdhikaraṇayoḥ
kartṛbhāvaḥ"
(ja. od gh. 1.4.23)
iti | tatra
'adhiśrayaṇodakāsecana...'(ja. od gh. 1.4.23)
ityādinā kartṛkaraṇādhikaraṇānāṃ guṇakriyāmuktvā
'apādānādīnāṃ tvaprasiddhiḥ'(ja. od gh. 1.4.23)
iti punaścodite 
"na vā svatantraparatantratvātayoḥ paryāyeṇa vacanaṃ nacanāśrayā ca
saṃjñā"
iti punaḥ siddhāntitam | tathā hi valāhako vidyotate iti
vidyaudabhedāvivakṣāyāṃ prayogāt svavyāpāre'styevāpādānasya
svātantryam ityāhuḥ | grāma āgaccchatīti
tūkte'rthāntarāvagamādapādānavyāpārānavasāyāt prayogābhāvaḥ |
asti tu grāmasyāpādānasya kriyāsiddhau kaścid
vyāpāro'vadhibhāvāpatilakṣaṇaḥ | anyathā grāmasya
samīpādāgaccchatītivat grāmādāgaccchatītyatrāpi grāmasyākārakatvaṃ
syāt | saṃpradānasyāpi anirākaraṇāt kartuḥ, preraṇānumatibhyāṃ ca
tatvamityastyeva svavyāpāraḥ | brāhmaṇo dadātīti tu prayogābhāvaḥ,
saṃpradānatve brāhmaṇasyāsmādvivakṣitārthānavasāyāt | ata
evāpādānasaṃpradānavyāpāre dhātorna vṛtiḥ, api tu
karmakaraṇādivyāpāra eva, pacyate odanaḥ svayameva, asiścchinati,
sthālī pacatītyādau | tadevaṃ sarvatra kriyābhedasaṃbhava iti tatra
svātantryāt karaṇāditve'pi kartṛśabdaḥ pravartate | yadyevaṃ
karaṇāditve'pi svātantryāt kartṛsaṃjñā syāt | naitadasti |
kārakādhikārāllabdhe svātantrye svatantraḥ karteti punaḥ śrutiḥ
pāratantryānācchuritasvātantryapratipatyartheti mukhya eva svatantre
kratṛsaṃjñāvatiṣṭhate | evamapi paratvāt karaṇādīnāṃ
karaṇādisaṃjñāṃ bādhitvā kartṛsaṃjñā prāpnoti | naiṣa doṣaḥ |
karaṇādisaṃjñānāmanavakāśatvāt tābhiḥ kartṛsaṃjñāyāṃ atra bādhanāt
| kārakavyapadeśastavanuvṛtisāmarthyādbhavati | atha vā
svātantryanimitā kārakasaṃjñeti nātra bādyabādhakabhāvo
nimitanimitinoḥ | atra hi svātantryanimitaṃ pāratantryamiti
karaṇādibhāve'pyanumitaṃ svātantryaṃ niviśata eva
kārakasaṃjñātyapādānādisaṃjñābhiḥ samāveśa evāsyāḥ | yatra tu
pāratantryavivakṣānāntarīyikā svātantryavivakṣā tatra karaṇaṃ parāṇi
bādhanta ityucyate | tadyathā dhanuṣā vidhyatīti | tatra hi dhanuṣaḥ
sādhakatamatvāvinābhāvinī svātantryavivakṣā | svatantraḥ kartetyatra ca
naivamavadhāryate yat sarvāsvavasthāsu svatantramiti, api tu yat
svatantrameveti sthālī pacatītyādāvapi kartṛsaṃjñāsiddhiḥ |
adhikārasāmarthyācca
karaṇādyavasthāyāmapyanumitasvātantryahetukakārakaśabdapravṛtiraviruddheti sarvaṃ sustham | tadevaṃ pūrvaṃ kriyāyā abhedamāśritya niṣpatimātre kartṛtvamiti sāmānyabhūtā kriyā niṣpatimātrasvabhāvā vyākhyātā | idānīṃ tu guṇapradhānabhāvena kriyābhedamapyāśra#tyākirtari kartṛśabdupapāditaḥ | tatra ca sāmānyabhūtā sādhyatvena sarveṣāṃ sādhāraṇatvaṃ prāptā pradhānakriyā vartata iti bhāṣyārthaḥ || 20,21 ||
svavyāpāre svatantrāṇāmeva pradhānavyāpāre pāratantrye
dṛṣṭāntamāha---
yathā rājñā niyukteṣu yoddhṛtvaṃ yoddhṛṣu sthitam |
teṣu vṛtau tu labhate rājā jayaparājayau || 22 ||
tathā kartrā niyukteṣu sarveṣvekārthakāriṣu |
kartṛtvaṃ karaṇāditvairutaraṃ na virudhyate || 23 ||
(21) yathā rājñā svāminā niyukteṣu preṣiteṣu yoddhṛtvaṃ
vyūhavibhāgādinā yoddhṛṣu sthitam | teṣu yudhyamāneṣu ca yā
vṛtiḥ śatrumahgadeśotsādanādikā tasyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ rājā jayaṃ
parājayaṃ vā'pi paryāyeṇa labhate | etadāha | yoddhāro yoddhṛtve
kartāraḥ | pūrvaṃ tadvyāpārasya tatrasthatvāt | punā
rājaniyogādasvatantrāḥ | punaśca tanniyoge nirapekṣāḥ svatantrāḥ |
evaṃ kartrā niyukteṣu saṃbhūyārthaṃ pākādikaṃ kurvatsu bhṛtyeṣviva
karaṇādiṣu yat svātantryaṃ jāyate niyogādutarakālabhavaṃ
tatkaraṇatvādibhirniyogakālabhāvibhirna virudhyate ityarthaḥ | tadevaṃ 
'kāraka iti saṃjñānirdeaśaścet saṃjñino'pi nirdeaśaḥ | itarathā
hyaniṣṭaprasaṅgo grāmasya samīpādāgaccchatītyakārakasya' (ja. od gh. 1.4.23)
ityādinā sādhakaṃ nirvartakaṃ vā saṃjñi nirdeaṣṭavyamiti codite,
anvarthasaṃjñāvijñānādviśiṣṭasaṃjñisiddhyā parihṛte,
adhikāreṇotaratrānuvṛteḥ kārakasaṃjñāyāḥ
karaṇādiṣvanupapaterudbhāvitāyāṃ niṣpatimātrāpekṣyā
kartṛtvāśrayaṇena pratikārakaṃ kriyābhedena guṇakriyāyāṃ
svātantryāśrayaṇena ca parihṛtamiti prakaraṇārthaḥ || 22,23 ||
anvarthasaṃjñāśrayeṇa kriyānirvartakasya sādhakatve'bhihiteheturapi
kriyānirvarta iti karaṇa ityeva siddhāyāṃ tṛtīyasyāṃ kimarthaṃ
hetāviti punarvidhinamityāśaṅkyā hetoḥ sādhanādvayatirekaṃ
darśayati |

\textbf{anāśrite tu vyāpāre nimittaṃ hetur iṣyate |
āśritāvadhibhāvaṃ tu lakṣaṇe lakṣaṇaṃ viduḥ || 24 ||}

(22) vyāpāramanāśritya vivakṣitārthasiddhau yogyatāmātreṇa
nimitatvenāśriyate yaḥ sa hetuḥ | sa cākārakavibhakteḥ śeṣalakṣaṇāyā
viṣayaḥ tṛtīyasyāḥ saṃbandhaṣaṣṭhībādhikāyāḥ | tadyathā adhyayanena
vasatīti | adhyayanaṃ hetuḥ, vāso hetumān | tayoḥ saṃbandhe
śeṣaṣaṣṭhaḷaṃ prāptāyāṃ hetau tṛtīyā | naiyāyikāstvācakṣate,
guṇadravyayoryannimitaṃ sa hetuḥ | sa cākārakavibhakterviṣayaḥ | yatu
kriyāpadopātāyāḥ kriyāyā nimitaṃ tatkārakameva | yathā 'vidyayā
vasati' ityādāviti
'anarlakṣaṇe' (gh. 1 4.84)
ityatra hetorapi lakṣamaśabdenākhyānādaviśeṣamanayoḥ
saṃbhāvyāpākarotyāśritetyādinā | lakṣaṇe ityatra lakṣaṇaśabdo
bhāvasādhanaḥ | 'lakṣaṇaṃ viduḥ' ityatra tu karaṇasādhanaḥ | lakṣaṇe
lakṣyamāṇavastuprakāśane | yad 'āśritāvadhibhāvam,'
āśrito'vadhibhāvo maryādārūpaṃ yatra tallakṣyate'neneti lakṣaṇaṃ
jñāpakaṃ viduḥ jānanti tajjñāḥ | yatrāvadhibhāvaḥ prajñāyate hetāvapi
tallakṣaṇam | ata eva tuḥ' viśeṣāvadyotakaḥ | anye tvāhuḥ |
kāryotpatāvupayogo hetoḥ, pratyayotvatau tu lakṣamasyeti |
tadasat | hetorapi pratyayotpatau vyāpāradarśanādvidyāderapi hi
vāsādiniśraye vyāpārāllakṣyamāṇavastūpajane
yogyatāmanādṛtyāvadhitvenāśrito janako bhavati mā vā bhūditi
lakṣaṇam | tadyathā vṛkṣamanu vidyotate vidyauditi vṛkṣo lakṣaṇaṃ
vidyotanasya | vṛkṣaṃ prāpya vidyotata ityarthaḥ | kathaṃ tarhyanurlakṣaṇa ityatra
śākalyasya saṃhitāmanu prāvarṣad ityudāharaṇam | yāvatā saṃhitā
nāvadhimātreṇa varṣaṇasya paricchedikā pratīyate , api tu janakatvena
| janakaśca hetuḥ, na lakṣaṇamiti heturevātra
jñāpakatvasāmānyāllakṣamaśabdenokta ityavirodhaḥ | nanu ca
dhūmo'gninā prasiddhasāhacaryo'saka#ﬁddarśanāvadhṛtaḥ
kevalo'pyadṛśyamānamagniṃ kāraṇāntaraprabhavaṃ lakṣayatītyevaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ
syāt | saṃhitā tu prathamameva dṛśyamānā, na tasyā asakṛdṛrśanaṃ
darśanāvṛtirastīti kathamasau lakṣaṇaṃ syāt | atrocyate |
yadyapyetadasti loke yenāsakṛllakṣyate tallakṣaṇamiti tathāpi pratītā
kāryodaye śaktiryasya kāraṇasya tathāvidhamapi kāraṇaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ bhavati |
kāryasya hi yaḥ paricchedaḥ tatra hetubhāvena kāraṇamapi lakṣaṇam,
kāryaparicchedakatvāt | yathaiva hi bhūyodarśanena
gṛhītāvinābhāvo'nyaṃ gamayati tathā heturapi parānapekṣo
janayan kāryasyāvyabhicārāt paricchedakatvena bhavati lakṣaṇam |
yathā meghodayo vṛṣṭeḥ kāraṇatvena niścito'satyāmapi vṛṣṭau
tatparicchedaka iti lakṣaṇaṃ bhavati yalloko meghodayaṃ dṛṣṭvā
vṛṣṭimanuminoti | nanvanvayavyatirekabalāddhetutvaṃ niścīyate |
yasmin sati yadbhavati anyasmiṃstu satyapi vinā tena na bhavati sa
tasya hetuḥ | anvayavyatirekau cāsakṛdṛrśanādbhavataḥ |
sakṛddarśane'nvayavyatirekayoraniścayāt | saṃhitā ca yadyapi kāraṇaṃ
pravarṣaṇasya tathāpi sakṛtpravṛterna niścīyate tatra kāraṇatvam,
tatkālasannihitārthāntarādapi kadācit kāryotpateḥ saṃbhāvanāt |
evaṃ ca meghodayavanna lakṣaṇamasau yujyate | atra brūmaḥ sakṛddarśanādapi
hetubhāvo'vagamyate | yasya hi yajjanakaṃ na bhavati tasmāt sakṛdapi
tanna dṛśyate, yathā śālyaṅkuro yavāt | na ca tatkālabhavo'nyo
janako'nyadā tasmādadṛṣṭeḥ | tathā cāhuḥ 
yasmin sati bhavatyeva yatato'nyasya kalpane |
taddhetutvena sarvatra hetūnāmanavasthitiḥ ||
iti | na ca kākatālīyanyāyena tata utpatiḥ, atatsvabhāvasya
satṛdapyajananāt | saṃhitā ca pratyakṣeṇa dṛśyate varṣaṃ janayantīti
sakṛddarśanādapi hetubhāvo'tra niścīyate | kiṃ cāgamādapi
hetutvamatra niścitaṃ saṃhitāyāḥ pravarṣaṇa iti heturevātra
jñāpakatvasāmānyāllakṣamamityuktam | taduktaṃ bhāṣye 
'lakṣaṇena heturapi vyāptaḥ' (g.eṇa. od gh. 1.4.84)
iti | lakṣamasya vyāpakatvāt | kāraṇamapi hi kāryaṃ lakṣayallakṣaṇaṃ
bhavati, yathā meghodayo vṛṣṭeḥ | na ca vyabhicāraḥ |
samagrasyāvikalasya janakatvāt | tathā vivecane cāvyabhicārāt |
prāpaṇaśaktiśca prāmāṇyaṃ na prāpaṇameveti nyāyaḥ | yadi tarhi
lakṣaṇaśabdena padārthāntaraparicchedahetubhāvo varṇyate
tadāśritāvadhibhāvetyetat katham | āśritāvadhibhāve'pi lakṣaṇe
paricchedahatutvameva lakṣaṇamanyathā kathamalakṣayallakṣaṇaṃ syāt |
iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ, yad vṛkṣo'vadhibhāvena paricchedahetuḥ,
saṃhitādestvavadhitvānāśraye'pi jñāpakatvasāmānyāllakṣaṇatvam |
bahudhā hi lakṣaṇaṃ bhavati | kvacidavadhibhāvena yathā
vidyotamānāyāstaḍito vanaspatiravasāyanibandhanam | kvacit
kāraṇāvacchedena yathā dhūmo vanheḥ | kvacit
kāryaparicchedahetutayā yathā meghodayo vṛṣṭeḥ | saṃhitāpi
coktena prakāreṇa lakṣaṇaṃ hetuśceti | yadyevaṃ 
'lakṣaṇetthaṃbhūtākhyāna....'(gh. 1.4.90)
ityādinā sāmānyalakṣaṇenaiva siddhāyāṃ karmapravacanīyasaṃjñāyāṃ
'anurlakṣaṇe' (gh. 1.4.84)
iti punarvidhānamanarthakam | taduktam |
'anurlakṣaṇevacanānarthakyaṃ sāmānyakṛtatvāt'(ja. od gh. 1.4.84)
iti | atrotaram---
'hetvarthaṃ tu vacanam' (ja. od gh. 1.4.84)
ityuktam | nāyamabhidheyaparyāyo'rthaśabdaḥ, kiṃ tu nivṛtivacanaḥ |
kārye ca kāraṇa śabdamupacarya hetuśabdena hetutṛtīyoktā |
bādho'pi bādhyānnānyeti 'heturevārtho nivartyo yasye'ti samāsaḥ
| tena hetusaṃjñābādhanārthaṃ punaḥ saṃjñāvidhānamityuktaṃ bhavati | nanu
ca punaḥ saṃjñāvidhānāt pradeśeṣu saṃjñipratyayaḥ sthirataro bhavet |
tṛtīyā tu kathaṃ bādhyeta | paratvena hi bādhikāsau | naitat |
asakṛdvidhīyamānā saṃjñā 
'karmapravacanīyayukte dvitīyā'
ityādipradeśeṣu dvirupatiṣṭhate | tatraikabhupasthānaṃ dvitīyārtham |
yatu dvitīyamupasthānamanurlakṣaṇa iti tadanarthakaṃ mā bhūditi
hetutṛtīyaṃ bādhitvā dvitīyameva sthirayati | nanu dvirupasthānāt
saṃjñipratyayo dvirbhavet, na tu dvitīyāvidhiḥ pracayamanubhavati |
kāryārthā saṃjñā guṇabhūtā | kāryaṃ dvitīyākhyaṃ pradhānam | na ca
guṇabhede pradhānabhedaḥ | kimartha tarhi guṇo dviruktaḥ | nirarthako
bhavatu, na tviyatā pradānamāvartate | yadyapi ca pradhānamātmasaṃskārārthaṃ
guṇadeśaṃ gaccchati yathoddeśe saṃjñāparibhāṣe tathāpi sākāṃkṣasya
gumadeśagamanādekenaiva gumena nairākāṃkṣyādaparaguṇadeśaṃ kimarthaṃ
vrajediti ekaiva dvitīyā | tāṃ paratvātṛtīyā bādheta | nanu ca
pradhānamantareṇa guṇānāṃ svarūpahānaprasaṅgāt guṇāntaramakṛtārthaṃ
pradhānāntaramāvirbhāvayet | yathā amātyā rājābhāve svātmano
guṇarūpamīhamānā rājāntaraṃ syāpayanti | tatsthāpane
teṣāmātmarūpaṃ saṃpadyate | rājānamantareṇāmātyāderabhāvāt |
naitat samānam, rājāmātyayoḥ parasparamupakāryopakārakabhāvāt
| guṇapradhānabhāvaniyamasyaivāniścayāt | tathā hi rājā
prakṛtīrdharmaśāstroktayāṃ maryādāyāṃ sthāpayati | svayaṃ cānuvartate
| tāḥ prakṛtayo'pi rājānaṃ vinayantīti pradhānabhedādeva tatra
guṇabhedaḥ | evaṃ tarhi upāyavidhirevopeyavidhirbhavati | tadyathā
'anīkastho bhava' ityukte'nīkasthatā
hastināmannayavasarakṣāsaṃpādanamiti anīkasthatāvidhānadvāreṇa tadeva
kāryaṃ vihitaṃ bhavati | evaṃ karmapravacanīyasaṃjñāvidhānadvāreṇa
tatkāryaṃ dvitīyākhyameva vihitaṃ bhavati | tathā ca punardvitīyā
vidhīyamānā bādhakabādhanārthā jāyate, yena nāprāptinyāyena |
saṃbandhe hi ṣaṣṭhī prāptā, tasyā bādhikā | atra vacanasāmarthyaṃ
nibandhanam | anyathā punaḥ saṃjñāvidhānameva dvitīyāvidhānārthaṃ
kathamupapadyate | itthaṃ ca kṛtvā kalpanāntaramapyupapadyate | guṇabhedena
pradhānabheda iti vacanena nyāyasya bādhanāt | tathā ca bhāṣyam---
'dṛṣṭāntasyāpi puruṣāraṃbho nivartako bhavati ' (g. eṇa. od gh. 1.1 85)
iti | yadvā saṃjñābhedādanuśabdaḥ saṃjñī bhidyate | na tu dvitīyāvidhiḥ
pradhānabhūtaḥ, yena nyāyyo guṇabhedāt pradhānabhedo bhavet |
saṃjñibhedācca labdhabalātiśayo dvitīyāvidhistṛtīyāvidhiṃ bādhate |
yathoddeśapakṣe ca guṇadeśaṃ gaccchat pradhānamekena guṇena kṛtārthaṃ na
bhavati | tatprakaraṇagatena sarveṇārthitvāt, saṃbandhasyāviśeṣāt | ata
eva pradhānāntaraṃ guṇo'kṛtārthaḥ kalpayatu rājāntaraṃ yathā prakṛtayaḥ
| na ca vaisādṛśyaṃ prakṛtīnāṃ rājakṛtopakārāpekṣitve'pi sarvathā
guṇatvāt tadaṅgabhāvaparipoṣārthameva hi
tatkṛtopakārāpekṣaṇam' na tu svapradhānyārtham | kāryakālapakṣe'pi
kāryavākye saṃjñādvayaṃ bhedenaiva saṃjñidvayamupapasthāpayati | tasmāt
punaḥ saṃjñāvidhānalakṣaṇādvacanasāmarthyāt prāptātiśayaḥ
kakṣyāntaraprāpto dvitīyāvidhiḥ paramapi
tṛtīyāvidhimapasārayatītyeva yuktam | tathā ca vakṣyati |

\textbf{hetuhetumator yogaparicchede 'nunā kṛte |
āraṃbhād bādhyate prāptā tṛtīyā hetulakṣaṇā ||}

iti | tadittham anurlakṣaṇa ityatra hetureva lakṣaṇavyāpakatvaṃ kathayituṃ
lakṣamatayopātaḥ satyapyavāntarabhede'nayoḥ | tathā hi 
'lakṣaṇahetvoḥ kriyāyāḥ' (gh. 3.2.126)
ityatra lakṣaṇādbhedena janakatvavivakṣayā heturupātaḥ | tadevaṃ
nimitasāmānyaṃ tridhā vibhaktam | svarūpabhedena kriyānirvartakaṃ
kārakam, sāmānyena janako hetuḥ, jñāpake lakṣaṇamiti || 24 ||

janakatvasāmye'pi hetusādhanayorviṣayabhedāt bhedamāha---

\textbf{dravyādiviṣayo hetuḥ kārakaṃ niyatakriyam |}

(23) dravyaguṇakriyāsādhanasamarthaṃ vastu dravyādīnāṃ hetuḥ | tadyathā
bījenāṅkuraḥ, dhanena kulam, kanyayā śokaḥ, vidyayā yaśaḥ,
śilpābhyāsena naipuṇyam, agninā pākaḥ |
kriyāsādhanasamarthamapyanāśritavyāpāraṃ hetureveti pākādayo yadyapi
kriyāḥ tathāpyatroparatavyāpārāṇāṃ sannidhinātropakāritayā
tejaḥprabhṛtīnāṃ vivakṣitatvādavidyamānakaraṇabhāvāste hetutṛtīyayā
saṃbadhyante | dhanena saha saṃpatikriyāprabhāvitaḥ kulasya saṃbandhaḥ |
kulasya hi dhanena prasiddhirupajanyate | tatra coparatavyāpāraṃ dhanaṃ
yogyatāmātrātkulasya heturiti śeṣaṣaṣṭhībādhikayā hetutṛtīyayā
saṃyujyate kanyāpi janayituḥ śokasya jananakriyādvāreṇa | vidyāpi
yaśasvinā prāpyamāṇaiva yaśaso heturiti prāptikiyājanito'tra
śeṣasaṃbandhaḥ | evamanyatrāpi yathāyogamabhyūhyam | śilpābhyāsena
naipuṇyamadhyayanena vasatīti guṇakriyāviṣayatā hetoḥ |
kriyāsādhananiyataṃ tu kārakam | tatra hi kriyaiva viṣayabhūtā
niyatā, vyāpārāviṣṭatvaṃ ca svarūpabhedaḥ | kasyacitu
vyāpāraviṣṭasyāpi kriyāviṣayasya śāstrīyaṃ hetutvamuktamityāha |

\textbf{kartā kartrantarāpekṣaḥ kriyāyāṃ hetur iṣyate || 25 ||}

kartā prayojakaḥ prayojyakartṛśaktivyavadhānena kriyāyām upayujyamānaḥ
prayojyakartāraṃ preṣayan tatprayojako hetuḥ iti śāstre
hetutveneṣyate | vacanāt kārakaviśeṣo'pi na tvayaṃ rūḍho
heturityarthaḥ || 25 ||

idānīṃ kriyāviṣayatve samāne'pi hetukāraṇayorviśeṣamāha |
kriyāyai karaṇantasya dṛṣṭaḥ pratinidhistathā |
hetvarthā tu kriyā tasmānna sa pratinidhīyate || 26 ||
(24) kriyāsādhanāya prakṛṣṭopakārakaṃ karaṇamapradhānam, kriyā tu
sādhya svabhāvā pradhānam | ata eva pradhānānurodhitvād
guṇānāmasati śrute karaṇe śiṣṭaiḥ pratinidhirācaritaḥ | pradhānabhūtā
hi kriyā sādhyatvāt yogyasādhanākṣepasamartheti
sādhanasāmānyasyākṣepe'dhyāvāpārthaṃ śrutaḥ sādhanaviniyoga iti
tadasaṃbhave'pi pradhānena kriyāsvarūpeṇa yadākṣiptaṃ sādhanasāmānyaṃ
tadviśeṣāntareṇāpi paripoṣyamāṇaṃ na śrautārthatyāgāya prabhavati |
sāmānyasyāpi śrutatvāt tasya ca sarvaviśeṣasvīkārayogyatvāditi
pratinidhyupapatiḥ pūrvameva carcitā vistarataḥ | aṅgabhreṣe'pi
pradhānānucchedāt pradānakriyāsvarūpasaṃrakṣaṇāya śiṣṭaiḥ
saṃpradāyapāraṃparyāvagataṃ sadṛśamaṅgaṃ mukhyabuddhyaivopādīyata
iti kriyāyāḥ prādhānyasyaitat phalam | ata eva kecit karaṇaśabdo'tra
sarvakārakavacano jātau caikavacanamiti sarvāṇi kārakāṇi
guṇabhūtānīti guṇasya pratinidhirupapadyata iti vyācakṣate | adhyayanena
vasatīti tu vasatikriyā''khyātāt prādhānyenāpi pratīyamānā
vastusāmarthyādadhyayanākhyahetuparatantrā | tathā hyadhyayanārtho mamātra
vāsa iti vaktāro bhavanti | na ca guṇānurodhi
pradhānamityadhyayanābhāve vāsārthamanyat kiṃcidatra pratinidhātuṃ
yuktam | anyasya hyupādāne tādarthyaṃ kriyayāḥ pratīyeteti tadānīṃ sa
eva mukhyo hetuḥ syāt | tasmādadhyayanena vasatīti neyaṃ
karaṇatṛtīyā, api tu hetutṛtīyaiveti | na hyadhyayanena
vyāpārāviṣṭena vāso nirvartyate api tu vāsasyādhyayanameva
saṃpādyaṃ pradhānamiti nirvyāpāraṃ yogyatāmātreṇoddeśyamadhyayanaṃ
vāsasya hetuḥ | vyāpārāviṣṭatve'dhyayanasya vāsaṃ
pratyānukūlyavivakṣāyāṃ bhikṣādivat tatsamarthācaraṇāt
prayojakakartṛtvam , adhyayane vāsayatīti | tadevaṃ kārakaviṣaye
kriyāpradhānaṃ kārakaṃ guṇabhūtam | hetuviṣaye tu viparyaya
ityanenāpi viśeṣeṇa kārakāddhetoryatireka uktaḥ || 26 ||
idānīṃ caturthīviṣayaṃ tādarthyaṃ
tṛtīyāviṣayāddhetoryatirecayitumāha |
pratilomyānulomyābhyāṃ heturarthasya sādhakaḥ | 
tādarthyamānulomyena hetutvānugataṃ tu tat || 27 ||
(25) apacīyamānāvastho heturapatikameva kāryaṃ janayan
prātilomyopalakṣitaḥ | tadyathā
jalamātapasaṃsargaviśeṣopajātāpacitarūpamutarotarāpacitatarāpacitatamalakṣaṇāvasthāparaṃparāsādanānuguṇam | arthāntarasaṃsargopacīyamānarūpaviśeṣaṃ tu kāryamupacitatarameva janayan kāraṇamānukūlyopalakṣito hetuḥ | tadyathā bījamudakātapasaṃyogāhitaviśeṣamucchūanatarāvasthamaṅkuraprasavasamartham | itthaṃ dviprakāro hetuḥ | tadviśeṣastu tādarthyam | ānukūlyaikaniyatamarthasiddhinimitamānukūlyena yaddhetutvaṃ janakatvaṃ tadanugataṃ hetuviśeṣarūpam | tādarthyaṃ kāryakāraṇasaṃbandhasyodbhūtarūpamityarthaḥ | tatra caturthī tadanugataṃ hetuviśeṣarūpam | tādarthyaṃ kāryakāraṇasaṃbandhasyodbhūtarūpamityarthaḥ | tatra caturthī kuṇḍalāya hiraṇyamiti | 'tasmā idaṃ tadartham' iti idamopakārakasya nirdeaśaḥ, taccchabdenopakāryasya | samāsādbhāvapratyayena saṃbandhasyodbhūtarūpābhidhānam | tatra cotarapadārthasya kāraṇarūpasya prajñāpanīyatvena prādhānyātaccchabdavācye guṇe saṃbandhaḥ padaṃ badhnāti | tatra hyasāvudbhūta iti tādarthye kāraṇasyopakāritve gamyamāne kāryakāraṇasaṃbandhodbhave caturthī vidhīyamānā kāryavācina eva bhavati | tathaiva kāraṇagatasyāpi saṃbandhasya nirbhogāt | kāraṇavacanātṛtīyā na bhavati | kāryameva prayojakatayā pariṇataṃ tadarthamityucyata ityayuktamabhidhānam | utarapadārthapradhānatvātadarthaśabdasya kāraṇavacanatā nyāyyā yataḥ | ata eva hetutvānugataṃ tādarthyam | kāraṇaviśeṣo na kāryam | yadarthamasya gamanaṃ tadanena sādhitamityatra tūpasarjanasyaiva yaccchabdenoddiṣṭasya samānādhikaraṇena taccchabdena nirdeaśāt sarvanāmarthaḥ kāryayogī yuktaḥ | na tvetāvateha sarvanāmārthasya prādhānyamutarapadārthe guṇatvāt || 27 ||
tadevaṃ kārakaṃ heturlakṣaṇaṃ tādarthyamiti nimitatvasāmye'pi
pravibhaktasvarūpaviṣayāḥ padārthāḥ iti na tatra lakṣamasāṅkaryaprasaṅga
ityānuṣaṅgike'rthe nirṇīte prastutameva kārakaviṣayaṃ vicāraṃ
prastāvayitumāha |
sarvatra sahajā śaktiryāvaddravyamasthitā |
kriyākāle tvabhivyakterāśrayāduparakāriṇī || 28 ||
(26) anityānāṃ padārthānāṃ svahetoreva śaktānāmudbhava iti
sahajātā śaktiḥ | na hyanādheyātiśayatvād
bhāvānāmaśaktasyotpatāvanantaramādhātuṃ śakyate śaktiḥ | nityānāṃ tu
svābhāvikī śaktiḥ | āśrayasamavetāstu śaktayaḥ | tadapāye nirādhārā
nāvasthātuṃ pārayantīti yāvaddravyabhāvinyaḥ | evamapi
sahakārisannidhāne svakāryodyauktāḥ śaktyābhivyaktimāśrayante bhāvā
ityabhivyaktikāla eva kāryopayogitvāt sādhanamityucyante nānyadā
satve'pi | tathā hi |
kuḍaḷsyāvaraṇe śaktirasyādīnāṃ vidāraṇe | 
sarvadā sa tu san dharmaḥ kriyākāle nirūpyate || 29 ||
(27) āvarakaṃ kuḍaḷm, pāṭakaṃ ca śastram ,
chedyāvarītavyavastusannidhāne jñāyata iti vidyamānā
śaktirviṣayāyatā'bhivyaktiḥ | pariccchidyate trikālā'pyarvāgdarśanaiḥ |
upādānaniyamāddhi kāryāt pūrvamapi śaktirasti | punarapi
kāryakāraṇādutarakālamapi |
āgamenāpyetat saṃvādayati |
svāṅgasaṃyoginaḥ pāśā daityānāṃ vāruṇā yathā |
vyajyante vijigīṣūṇāṃ dravyāṇāṃ śaktayastathā || 30 ||
(28) daityānāṃ svakarmanirmāṇā vāruṇāḥ pāśāḥ svahastatalāvalīnāḥ
sadā sannihitāḥ śaktirūpeṇetyāgamaḥ | te tu
parasainyāvajayavelāyāmabhivyajyanta iti śrūyate | evaṃ dravyāṇāṃ sadā
sannihitāḥ śaktayaḥ svakāryaniṣpādanakāle prakāśanta ityapi yuktam ||
30 ||
yaduktamastrādīnāṃ vidāraṇe śaktiriti tadeva pratanyate |
taikṣṇyagauravakāṭhinyasaṃsthānaiḥ svairasiryadā |
chedyaṃ prati vyāpriyate śaktimān gṛhyate tadā || 31 ||
(29) nibiḍatarasaṃhananānāmavayavānāṃ krameṇāgre sūkṣpībhavatāṃ
tanutvaprakarṣastaikṣṇyam, chedanimitaṃ dharmaḥ |
bhinnajātīyāvayavasaṃbhedavicchede
viccchinnāntarālānāmavayavānāmupacayād gauravaṃ
nipatanaheturdharmaḥ | tāpanotejitānāṃ lohāvayavānāṃ nibiḍatvaṃ
kāṭhinyaṃ nāma dharmo yataḥ prahāraviśeṣo niṣpadyate |
saṃniveśaviśeṣo
maṇḍalāgrādivyapadeśanibandhanamasyantarādvibhāgahetuḥ saṃsthānam |
yataḥ prahāravaicitryamājāyate |
tadetairdharmairasiyaṣṭiśchedyavipāṭanamārabhamāṇā śaktimatīti gṛhyate |
vyāpāraśūnyā tu satsvapyeteṣu dharmeṣu na tathāvasīyate |
evamanyeṣāmapi padārthānāṃ yathāyogaṃ vācyam | aserupalakṣaṇatvāt
|| 31 ||
pakṣāntarāṇyapyatra sādhane saṃbhavantītyāha |
prāṅ nimitāntarodbhūtaṃ kriyāyāḥ kaiścidiṣyate |
sādhanaṃ sahajaṃ kaiścit kriyānyaiḥ pūrvamiṣyate || 32 ||
(30) siddhe satve'bhivyaktipratīkṣā śobhate | satvameva tu śaktīnāṃ
kāryānumeyatvāt kriyātaḥ pūrvamasiddhamiti kriyāyāḥ
pūrvamapekṣaṇīyānnimitāccchaktayo dravyāṇāṃ kriyānuguṇā jāyanta eva |
satve'pyabhivyaktinimitamapekṣaṇīyamiti tadeva janakam | tathā hi
sahakārisavyapekṣāṇāṃ bhāvānāṃ janakatvāt parasparahitaśaktayaste
svakāryahetava ityunnīyante, kevalānāmajanakatvāt na tadānīṃ śaktā
iti na svādhārajanakādeva śaktijanma, api tu nimitāntarādeva |
pratyabhijñāyamānatvācca śaktāśaktatve'pi na vastubheda iti kecit | ata
evopādānaniyamo'pi yogyatāvadhāraṇat siddhaḥ | anye tu
śaktervastuno'nanyatvāt svabhāvabheda eva śaktāśaktatve manyante |
sahajaṃ kaiścit sādanaṃ śaktirdravyāṇāmiṣyata iti
pūrvapratipāditapakṣānuvādaḥ | kecitu na yenaiva kāraṇena kriyā
janyate tenaiya sādhanam, api tvanyenaiveti kriyāpekṣayā
nimitāntaratvaṃ manyamānāḥ sahajatvamapi kriyāpekṣayaiva vyācakṣate |
tathā hi itaretarasya śaktimarpayantaḥ kriyāṃ nirvartayanti bhāvāḥ
sahakāriṇaḥ iti sādhanaśaktibhiḥ sahaiva kriyāṇāṃ vyāpārarūpāṇāṃ janma
manyate | tathā ca yebhya eva kriyā jāyante tebhya eva śaktaya iti
sahajatvam | upajātaśaktayastu kṣaṇāntare kriyānirvartakā iti
sādhyasādhanabhāve nāsti kṣatiḥ na hi sādhyasādhanayoḥ sahabhāvaḥ
pramāṇopapannaḥ || 32 ||
anye tu sādhanāt pūrvabhāvinīṃ kriyāṃ manyante | tat kathaṃ yujyata
ityāha | pravṛtireva prathamaṃ kvacidapyanapāśritā |
śaktirekādhikaraṇe srotovadapakarṣati || 33 ||
(31) anapekṣitahetuvyāpārā nityā kriyā anapāśritā kvacidapi
bhaṇyate | svāśraye tu samavetaiva | ata eva tatrāśraye
sādhanaśaktīrāvirbhāvayati pratiniyatasvakāryasaṃpādikāḥ | anye
tvanāśritaivetyāhuḥ | satatavyāpārapravahadrūpā nadīpravāha iva
tṛṇaparṇalatādīn sādhanaśaktiḥ pravṛtilakṣaṇā kriyā karṣati vahatīti
keṣāṃcijjaranmīmāṃsakānāmāgamaḥ | rajolakṣaṇā vā pravṛtirnityā
sarvabhāveṣvanuyāyinī svakāryaprasavasamarthā kāryāṇi janayatīti
sāṃkhyanayaḥ | vyāpārāviṣṭasya sarvasya kāryajanakatvād vyāpāraḥ
pūrvabhāvī samavasthitaḥ | tena ca śaktyākṣepa iti yuktam || 33 ||
keyaṃ pravṛtiranapāśrayā nityetyāha |
[ito granthapātasandhānāya phullarājakṛtirlikhyate | ṭ
idānīṃ matāntarānusāreṇa sādhanasvarūpaṃ varṇayitumāha |
apūrvaṃ kālaśaktiṃ vā kriyāṃ vā kālameva vā |
tamevaṃlakṣaṇaṃ bhāvaṃ kecidāhuḥ kathaṃcana || 34 ||
(32) apūrvaṃ dharmādharmākhyamadṛṣṭasaṃjñakaṃ kecidevaṃrūpaṃ taṃ
sāmarthyalakṣaṇaṃ bhāvamāhuḥ | adṛṣṭavaśāddhi sarvaṃ bhavati vā na
vetyadṛṣṭameva sarvakāryāṇāṃ nimitaṃ boddhavyam | kecitu vādinaḥ
kālaśaktim eva bhagavato brahmaṇaḥ saṃbandhinīṃ sādhanamāhuḥ |
kālaśaktyanuprāṇitaṃ hi sarvaṃ kriyāyai prabhavati | kriyāṃ vā
rājasīmanantaropavarṇitasvarūpā sādhanamāhuḥ | na tūtarakālabhāvi
kiṃcana sādhanaṃ nāma śakyaṃ nirūpayitum | pūrvaṃ kriyāmūlaṃ sādanaṃ
vyākhyātam | adhunā kriyaiva sādhanamiti viśeṣaḥ | saiva hi svayaṃ
pravartamānā viśeṣībhavatīti kālamevā vā
nityamekamityādilakṣaṇalakṣitaṃ dravyakālavādinaḥ sādhanamāhuḥ |
kālavaśāddhi sarve bhāvāḥ prabhavanti kāryasaṃpataye iti kāla eva
sādhanam | yathā vakṣyati
'yadi na pratibadhnayāt...' (jat.kṣkṣkṣ kā. dh.5)
iti || 34 ||
atha śakteḥ sāmarthyalakṣaṇāyāḥ sādhanatvena pūrvaṃ nirūpitāyāḥ
saṃkhyāvicāraṃ kartumāha |
nityāḥ ṣaṭ śaktayo'nyeṣāṃ bhedābhedasamanvitāḥ |
kriyāsaṃsiddhaye'rtheṣu jātivat samavasthitāḥ || 35 ||
(33) anyeṣāṃ matenārtheṣu nityāḥ paṭ śaktayaḥ pṛthaglakṣaṇalakṣitāḥ
karmādyā vyavasthitā iti sthitam | kīdṛśyaḥ | bhedābhedasamanvitāḥ,
ekāntenārthebhyaścedbhedasamanvitā abhidhīyerannarthasya
śaktivaikalyānnīrūpatā syāt | athābhinnāḥ kathyante tarhi
vibhinnakāryodayo nāvatiṣṭhate | tasmāt kathaṃcid bhedena,
kathaṃcidabhedeneti bhedābhedābhyāṃ samanvitāḥ
śaktayo'rthajātivadavasthitāḥ yathā
satvadravyatvagotvaśābaleyatvādijātayaḥ satataṃ
bhedābhedasamanvayenārtheṣvityeke manyante || 35 ||
tāsāṃ tu dravyādibhedenānantye'pi ṣaṭtvameva ṣaḍvidhatvameva
tatvaṃ nātivartata ityāha |
dravyākārādibhedena tāścāparimitā iva |
dṛśyante tatvasāyāṃ tu ṣaṭ śaktīrnātivartate || 36 ||
(34) dravyabhedena bhinnā śaktiḥ | yathā dātramanyathā cchinati,
khaṅgo'nyatheti | ākārabhedena khaṅgo'nyathā khaṅgalatā
tvanyatheti | ādigrahaṇāddeśakālabhedena saiva
dahanaśaktiragnivaicitryamupayāti, ṣaṭtvaṃ punastatsvarūpaṃ
nātikrāmati || 36 ||
athaikaiva śaktirnimitabhedādviśeṣarūpatayā ṣoḍhātvamāpannā vā
sādhanamityāha |
nimitabhedādekaiva bhinnā śaktiḥ pratīyate |
ṣoḍhā kartṛtvamebāhustatpravṛternibandhanam || 37 ||
(35) yasmāt kartṛtvameva nimitabhedenepsitatamatvādinā
jvalanatvādinā vā ṣoḍhāvasthitaḥ san tatpravṛteḥ
śaktibhedapravṛteḥ kārakavyavahārabhedasya nibandhanaṃ nimitabhūtaṃ
boddhavyam | yathoktam-edhāḥ pakṣyantyā vikliteḥ jvaliṣyantīti
jvalanakriyākaraṇasya pāka ityādinyāyena
kartṛtvamevāvāntaravyāpāravivakṣayā karaṇādivyapadeśarūpatāṃ bhajate
|| 37 ||
tadevamekānekā vā śaktirupapāditā | athāsau vyatiriktā vā
syādavyatiriktā vetyāśaṅkyāha---
tatve vā vyatireke vā vyatiriktaṃ taducyate |
śabdapramāṇako lokaḥ sa śāstreṇānugamyate || 38 ||
(36) bhedābhedābhyāṃ tāvadanirvācyasau kevalaṃ
bhedābhedasamanvitetyuktam | athātra niṣkoṣāṇikāṃ karoṣi tatvayā
sahāsmākaṃ kiṃ prayojanam | sarvathā śaktiśaktimatorekāntena
tatvamavyatireko vābheda eva vā |
vaiyākaraṇanayānusāribhistvasmābhiḥ sāmarthyaṃ vyatiriktamevocyate |
yasmāt śabdapramāṇako lokaḥ | śāstrema cānena sa evānugamyata
ityāha 'sa śāstreṇānugamyate | lokapratītyanusāreṇa hi śabdasyārtho
vitanyate | lokaśca śabdakriyānimitāyāḥ
śaktervyatirekamevānugaccchati | avyatireke hi satataṃ
kriyāniṣpatiprasaṅga ityuktam || 38 ||
atha paramārthaparyālocanāṃ vinā na nirvṛtimupalabhase tadatrocyate
kiṃcidityāha |
paramārthe tu naikatvaṃ pṛthaktvādbhinnalakṣaṇam |
pṛthaktvaikatvarūpeṇa tatvameva prakāśate || 39 ||
(37) pṛthaktvādbhinnalakṣaṇaṃ pṛthaktvavyatiriktaṃ naikatvamasti,
pṛthaktvaikatvarūpeṇa hi tatvameva prakāśate | taddhi vyatahāradaśāyāṃ
pṛthaktvena prakāśate | avidyāvilaye tvekatvena prakāśate iti
boddhavyam || 39 ||
etadeva vyākhyātumāha |
yat pṛthaktvamasaṃdigdhaṃ tadekatvānna bhidyate |
yadekatvamasaṃdigdhaṃ tat pṛthaktvānna bhidyate || 40 ||
(38) asaṃdigdhaṃ yāthātmyāvagamāt sandehaṃ vinā kṛtaṃ yat
pṛthaktvaṃ tadekatvānna bhidyate | yaccaikatvamasaṃdigdhaṃ vigatabhramaṃ
tat pṛthaktvān nānātvān na bhidyate || 40 ||
tathā ca yadaikatvaṃ kāṣṭhāmanuprāptaṃ tadā sarvatra tadeva prakāśata
ityāha |
dyauḥ kṣamā vāyurādityaḥ sāgarāḥ sarito diśaḥ |
antaḥ karaṇatatvasya bhāgā bahiravasthitāḥ || 41 ||
(39) athavāstāṃ katipayavastuviṣayo nānātvaikatvavicāraḥ | ya ime
dyauścākāśaṃ ca pṛthivī ca dyāvāpṛthivyau mahatyau mahābhūtasaṃjñike
vastunī, tadantare ca vāyurapi tṛtīyaḥ, tathādityalakṣaṇamapi
sakalatejasāṃ pradhānaṃ divyaṃ tejaḥ, āpaḥ saritsamudrā vā vikārāḥ,
sarvāṇyetāni mahābhūtāni sakalajagajjīvitabhūtāni, diśo vā
lokavyavahāranimitabhūtāḥ, kālaśca vakṣyamāṇaḥ, tadetat sarvam
'antaḥkaraṇatatvasya' iti antaḥkaraṇasaṃjñakaṃ antaḥkaraṇatvenāntararūpatayā
pratibhāsamānaṃ yat tatvaṃ tasyaivaite bhāgāḥ pratibiṃbakāḥ ābhāsā
bahiravasthitāḥ | paramārthe tu kīdṛśo'ntarbahirbhāvaḥ | ekameva
saṃvinmayaṃ paraṃ śabdabrahma tathā tathāvasthitamiti kārikārthaḥ || 41 ||
mahābhūtarūpatvaṃ digrūpatāṃ cābhidhāya kālarūpatāṃ tasyaiva
pratipādayannāha |
kālavicchedarūpeṇa tadevaikamavasthitam |
sa hyapūrvāparo bhāgaḥ pararūpeṇa lakṣyate || 42 ||
(40) sa hyapūrvāparaḥ paramārthena | athavā kālaśaktivaśāt
cirakṣiprādikrameṇa lakṣyate | tadevaṃ padārthasāmarthyena
lokaprasiddhyā vaiyākaraṇamatena sādhanam | atha vā
buddhiprakalpanārūpaṃ vijñānavādābhiprāyeṇa, saṃsargirūpaṃ vā
padārthāntarabhūtaṃ saṃsargavādānusāreṇa, adṛṣṭalakṣaṇapūrvaśabdavācyaṃ
vā mīmāṃsakadṛṣṭaḷa, brahmasaṃbandhinī lā kālaśaktiradvaitadarśanena, kriyā
rājasī pravṛtirūpā vā sāṃkhyadarśanānusāreṇa, nityamekaṃ
dravyalakṣaṇaṃ vā kālarūpaṃ dravyakālavādināṃ matena vijñāyam |
sādhanaprastāvena ca kārakaśabdavicāraḥ tat prasaṅgena ca
hetulakṣaṇakārakakaraṇatādarthyānāṃ bhedanirūpaṇapūrvakaṃ leśataḥ
svarūpamuktam | tataḥ śakteḥ kiṃ saha prāgutarakālaṃ vā bhāva iti
kālavikalpaḥ | sā ca kiṃ karmadibhedena bhinnaivāhosvidekaiva tathā
tathā parikalpyata iti saṃkhyāvikalpaḥ | sā ca kiṃ
padārthādvyatiriktaivāthāvyatirikteti vyatirekāvyatirekacintā
tatprastāvena ca brahmasvarūpanirūpaṇamiti prakaraṇasya samudāyārthaḥ || 42
||
idānīṃ prakṛtaṃ vaiyākaraṇamatamevānubadhnan
śaktervyatirekamevānvayavyatirekamukhena parighaṭayan
sadbhāvamupapādayitumāha |
dṛṣṭo hyavyatireke'pi vyatireko'nvaye'sati |
vṛkṣādyarthānlayastasmādvibhaktyartho'nya iṣyate || 43 ||
(41) avyatireke abhede'pi prakṛtyarthasya vṛkṣo vṛkṣaṃ vṛkṣeṇa
vṛkṣāyetyādau vibhaktyarthasya sādhanalakṣaṇasya vyatirekaḥ tathā
tatraiva vibhaktyarthasyāsatyanvaye prātipadikārthasyānvayo'nugamo
dṛṣṭaḥ | tato manyāmahe asti kaścid vyatiriktaḥ
pratyayārthabhūto'napahnavanīyaḥ sādhanalakṣaṇaḥ śaktiśabdavācyo'rtha iti
sādhanasiddhiḥ || 43 || 
idānīṃ vyākhyātasyopasaṃhārāya vyākhyeyasyopakramāya kārikāmāha |
sāmānyaṃ kārakaṃ tasya saptādyā bhedayonayaḥ |
ṣaṭ karmākhyādibhedena śeṣabhedastu saptamī || 44 ||
(42) sāmānyaṃ kārakamiti vyākhyātārthopasaṃhāraḥ | tasya
saptetyādinā vyākhyeyārthopoddhātaḥ | tatra ādyāḥ pradhānabhūtāḥ
sāmānyasādhanāpekṣayā bhedarūpāśca tā yonayaḥ svakānāṃ
bhedānāmiti bhedayonayaḥ | atha vādyāḥ sapta bhedānāṃ ca svakānāṃ
yonayaḥ kāraṇāni | kathamityāha 'ṣaṭ karmākhyādibhedena' ityādi |
karmādivyapadeśabhedena sapta kārakaśaktayo lakṣyante'dhuneti
sādhanasamuddeśasaṅgatiḥ || 44 ||
karmādibhedena saptabhedabhinnā tāvat kārakamuddiṣṭam | atha
tadanukrameṇa karma tāvat saṃkhyābhedapradarśanapūrvakaṃ vyākhyātumāha |
nirvartyaṃ ca vikāryaṃ ca prāpyaṃ ceti tridhā matam |
tatrepsitatamaṃ karma caturdhānyatu kalpitam || 45 ||
(43) kārakaviśeṣanirdeaśaśca vibhaktyanukramānusāreṇeha pradarśitaḥ |
sūtreṣu tu vipratiṣedhaparibhāṣāmanurudhyāpādānādikrameṇoktam |
tatra vibhaktayaḥ svaujasamādyāḥ | kārakamabhihitamanabhihitaṃ ca bhavati |
tatra prathamābhihitakārakavibhaktiḥ prātipadikārthamātra eva vidhīyate |
dvitīyādayaśca sarvā anabhihite kārakavibhaktaya ityekaprakamatvād
dvitīyādyarthānusāreṇaivopakrame | dvitīyādyarthaśca karmādiḥ |
yaduktam 
'karmaṇi dvitīyā' (gh. 2.3.2)
ityādi | ataḥ karmaṇa evādau vyākhyāvasaraprāptā | tatra kartuḥ kriyayā
yadīpsitatamamāptumiṣyamāṇatamaṃ tadeva lakṣaṇaṃ prathamasūtranirdiṣṭam
| karma tridhā tribhiḥ prakāraiḥ
nirvartyavikāryaprāpyarūpairbhinnamanekāntarnirdiṣṭaṃ boddhavyam | athā
anyat sūtrāntaranirdiṣṭamapi bhedāyitumāha caturdhānyatu kalpitam
iti || 45 ||
kathamityāha |
āṬdāsīnyena yat prāpyaṃ yacca karturanīpsitam |
saṃjñāntarairanākhyātaṃ yadyaccāpyanyapūrvakam || 46 ||
(44) āṬdasīnyena tāṭasthyena yat prāpyam | yathā grāmaṃ
ganturvakṣamūlādi | tathā yata karturinīpsitam coraviṣādi |
etat 
'tathāyuktaṃ ca nīpsitam' (gh. 1.4.50)
iti dvitīyasūtranirdiṣṭaṃ tāvad dvividham | tathā ca yenaiva
prakāreṇa karturīpsitatamaṃ tenaiva cet anīpsitam īpsitādanyad
dveṣyaṃ viṣādi, itaracca yadaudāsīnyena prāpyaṃ vṛkṣamūlādyauktam |
tathā saṃjñāntaraiḥ apādānādisaṃjñābhiranākhyātamapi kriyānimitam |
etad 
'akathitam ca' (gh. 1.4.51)
iti tṛtīyasūtranirdiṣṭaṃ veditavyam | tathā 'yad
yaccāpyanyapūrvakam' iti | anyat kārakaṃ pūrvaṃ yasya | yathā
'devadatamabhikrudhyati', 'devadatamabhidruhyati' iti | atra hi 
'krudhadruhorupasṛṣṭayoḥ karma' gh. 1.4.38
ityanena kudhadruhorupasṛṣṭayoḥ upasargasaṃbandhayoḥ yaṃ prati kopaḥ
tat kārakaṃ pūrvasūtreṇa saṃpradānasaṃjñamapi karmasaṃjñameva bhavati |
tathākṣān dīvyatītyatra
'divaḥ karma ca '(gh. 1.4.43)
ityanena divaḥ sādhakatamaṃ yat pūrvasūtreṇa karaṇasaṃjñameva karmasaṃjñaṃ
bhavati || 46 ||
atha nirvartyādi trividhamuddiṣṭaṃ vyākhyātumāha |
satī vāvidyamānā vā prakṛtiḥ pariṇāminī |
yasya nāśrīyate tasya nirvartyatvaṃ pracakṣate || 47 ||
(45) yasya nirvartyamānasya ghaṭādeḥ prakṛtiḥ satī vā yathā mudādikā,
avidyamānā vā yathā śabdaḥ saṃyogādeḥ pariṇāminī abhedena
nāśrīyate mṛdaṃ ghaṭaṃ karotītyādyevaṃrūpatayā na vivakṣyate, kiṃ tarhi,
mṛdā ghaṭaṃ karotītyādibhedenaiva | yadā prakṛtyā saha vivakṣā tadā
tannirvartyaṃ karma pracakṣate karmavidaḥ |
vikāryaṃ vyākhyātumāha---
prakṛtestu vivakṣāyāṃ vikāryam 
(46) prakṛteḥ kāramasya pariṇābhitvena yadā vivakṣā kriyate mṛdaṃ
ghaṭaṃ karoti, kāśān kaṭṃa karoti, aṅgārān bhasma karoti
ityādi tadā vikāryaṃ karma pracakṣate | athāparaya
matāntaropanyāsaccchalena prakārabhedakathanavāñcchayā nirvartyavikārye
nirūpayitumāha---
kaiścidanyathā 
nirvartyaṃ ca vikāryaṃ ca karma śāstre pradarśitam || 48 ||
kathamityāha
yadasajjāyate sadvā janmanā yatprakāśyate |
tannirvartyaṃ vikāryaṃ ca karma dvedhā vyavasthitam || 49 ||
(47) yadasad vaiśeṣikādyanusāreṇa jāyate sāṃkhyamatena sadeva vā
jāyate tadubhayamapi janmanā prakāsyamānatvānnirvartyaṃ kathyate |
etaduktaṃ bhavati | satkāryavādināmasmākaṃ nirvartyaṃ karmāstīti vaktuaṃ
pāryate | satī tasya prakṛtirbhavatvasatī vā | sarvathā janmanā
prakāsyamānatvena nirvartyatvasya na kācit na kṣatiḥ | sadasadvā
janmanā yat prakāśyate tannirvartyam | asajjāyate
ityavidyamānaprakṛteḥ karmaṇā nirvartyamānatā pūrvoktaivānūditā,
asajjanmanā prakāśyata iti | ayamatra viśeṣaḥ | tadyathā putraṃ prasūte,
śabdaṃ prayuṅkte ityatra kukṣisthasya sata eva putrasya vivakṣitasya ca
śabdasya prasavaprayogābhyāṃ prakāśanam | sataḥ prakāśanaṃ hi janmātra
vivakṣitasya ca śabdasya prasavaprayogābhyāṃ prakāśanam | sataḥ prakāśanaṃ
hi janmātra vivakṣitam | janmagrahaṇena pravṛtivivakṣāyāṃ
vikāryatāmāha | anyathābhivyajyamānasya nirvartyakarmatābhāvamapi
janmagrahaṇenāha | tena vedamadhīte ityādau vedāderadhyayanena
prakāśyamānasya nirvartyakarmatābhāvāt 
"karmaṇi nirvartyamānavikriyamāṇe iti ced
vedādhyāyādīnāmupasaṃkhyānam |"
(ja. od gh. 3.2.1)
ityucte | itthaṃ ca prakṛtyarthavivakṣāyamevaitannirvatyaṃ karpoktamiti 
kaiścidanyathā
ityasaṅgam | tasmāt sadasatkāryavādimatabhedenaitad
vyākhyeyam | pūrvaṃ lokapratītyanusāreṇoktam | iha tu
tīrthikamatāśrayeṇāpīti viśeṣaḥ || 49 ||
"vikāryaṃ ca karma dvedhā vyavasthitam" | prativyakti lakṣaṇaṃ vaktuaṃ
vyaktibhedaḥ pūrvamuktaḥ | lakṣaṇamāha---
prakṛtyucchedasaṃbhūtaṃ kiṃcit kāṣṭhādibhasmavat |
kiṃcid guṇāntarotpatyā suvarṇādivikāravat || 50 ||
(48) niranvayavināśāt prakṛterucchedaḥ | tadyathā kāṣṭhāderbhasmādi |
kāṣṭhāni bhasma karoti | pūrvavat prakṛtivikārayoḥ kriyāsaṃbandho
yojyaḥ | pūrveṇa tu lakṣaṇena nirvartyametat karma
prakṛteravivakṣāyām | vivakṣāyāṃ tu vikāryam | iha
tvavivakṣāyāmapi prakṛtau bhasmādikāryaṃ rūpaviśeṣādeva
kāṣṭhādimupamṛdyopajātamiti pratitervikāryam | pūrvatra tu
prakṛtiḥ sākṣādvikāryaṃ karma, tadabhedena vikāro'pi | iha tu
sākṣādvikāra eveti viśeṣaḥ | kāṣṭhāni dahatīti tu prakṛtireva
śabdavatī, sāmarthyagamyastu vikāro bhasm | evaṃ kāṇḍāni lunāti,
carma karotītyādau veditavyam | kācitu prakṛtiratyajantī svarūpaṃ
saṃstānāntarāpatyā vikṛtetyucyate iti guṇāntarasya
sanniveśāderdharmasyotpatyopalakṣitamaparaṃ vikāryaṃ karma | tadyathā
suvarṇī kuṇḍale bhavata iti | asatkāryanaye yadyapyatrāpūrvotpatiḥ
tathāpi pratyabhijñānālloke
tatvādhyavasāyādguṇāntaramātrāpatirucyate | etacca
prakṛtivivakṣāyāṃ pūrvalakṣaṇenāpi saṅgṛhītam || 50 ||
idānīṃ prāpyakarmalakṣaṇamāha---
kriyāgataviśeṣaṇāṃ siddhiryatra na gamyate |
darśanādanumānādvā tatprāpyamiti kathyate || 51 ||
(49) nirvartye karmaṇi nirvṛtarātmalābha eva kriyākṛto viśeṣo
darśanāt pratyakṣādevāvadhāryate | vikārye tu bhasmakuṇḍalādau
vikāraḥ kriyākṛto viśeṣo'vadhāryate pratyakṣeṇa | kvacit
punaranumānādviśeṣāvasāyo yathā paratra janitānāṃ
mukhaprasakādādinā liṅgenāvasāyaḥ | jalāvasekādikriyāveśitaśca
vanaspatīnāṃ viśeṣaḥ pratikalamavāptajanmā
sūkṣmatvādutpatyanantaramanavasīyamānaścaramāvasthāyāṃ
pracitataraviśeṣadarśanādavadhāryate | na hyaṃ pracitataro
viśeṣo'napekṣitakramo jhaṭityaśaṅkita evodetuṃ prabhavatīti
pratikṣaṇamanumīyamānaḥ sūkṣmarūpaḥ, tasya hi sūkṣmasya rūpasya
duravadhāratvam | yathā ṣaḍjādisvaraviśeṣayostārātitārayoḥ |
tadevaṃvidho viśeṣo yatra kriyākṛto na saṃdṛśyate tatkartuḥ
kriyāyā viṣayabhāvamātreṇepsitatamatvāt prāpyaṃ karma | tadyathā
ādityaṃ paśyati, nagaramupasarpati vedamadhīta ityādi |
ādityādīnāṃ hi saṃdarśanādikriyayā kaścidāhito viśeṣo
nāvadhāryate | pramāṇābhyāṃ kriyāsaṃbandhamātraṃ tu lakṣyate, na ca sa eva
viśeṣaḥ | vṛte kriyāsaṃbandhe vastugatasya bhedasya
nirvartyavikāryayordṛṣṭatvāt | nagare tu gamanakriyājanitaḥ
saṃyogo dviṣṭho viśeṣo bhavannapi nagarasya
mahāparimāṇatvādalpaparimāṇena devadatena
himavadaṅgulisaṃbandhavadalakṣyaḥ | pṛthivyekadeśaśca svāvayavāpekṣayā
kalpito viśiṣṭasvādheyādhārarūpo nagaraśabdavācyaḥ prasiddha eveti na
kācidanupapatiḥ || 51 || nāstyeva prāpyaṃ karma, sarvatra
kriyākṛtaviśeṣāvadhāraṇāditi kecit | tathā hi---
viśeṣalābhaḥ sarvatra vidyate darśanādibhiḥ |
keṣāṃcitadabhivyaktisiddhirdṛṣṭiviṣādiṣu || 52 ||
(50) darśanādikriyāhitaviśeṣalābho vikāraḥ sarvatra vidyate,
saukṣmthātu nāvadhāryate sarveṇeti keṣāṃcinmatam | tasya
viśeṣasya dṛṣṭau viṣaṃ yeṣāṃ teṣu tadviṣaye'bhivyakterniścayasya
siddhiḥ | dṛṣṭiviṣairahibhirnirīkṣito hi viṣayo jvalanajvālālīḍho
lakṣyate | tatsādharmyādanyatrāpi darśanādināhitaḥ kaścidviśeṣo
viṣaye samunneyaḥ | taijasaṃ hi cakṣuḥ prāpyakārīti tena yukto
viṣayo'vaśyaṃ vikriyate yathā dṛṣṭiviṣadarśanena | iṃyāstu viśeṣo
dṛṣṭiviṣe teja utkaṭṃa viṣasaṃparkādviṣayaṃ dahati, anyatra
tanutarasthiti | ādiśabdenāyaskāntādīnāṃ grahaṇam | tatsannidhau
lohaṃ bhramati | katakaphalasannidhau hi jalaṃ prasīdatītyādāvastyeva
kriyākṛto vikāraḥ | etacca na śāstrakārasya matam |
keṣāṃcidityanyamatatvenopanyāsāt | kvacidviśeṣadarśane'pi na
sarvatra tathā bhāvo'dṛśyamānaviśeṣe'pi yuktaḥ | kiṃ ca kriyāphale'tra
vikāro'bhipretaḥ | paridṛśyamānastu viṣayo kriyāyā heturna
phalam | dṛṣṭiviṣādāvapi tejaḥsaṃyogādviṣaye vikāro na
kriyābalāt | evamayaskāntādervastusvabhāvo'yaṃ na kriyākṛto
vikāraḥ kaścit | tasmādastyeva prāpyaṃ karma
kriyākṛtopakārānapekṣam || 52 ||
na cedvikārayogaḥ prāpyakarmaṇaḥ kathamasya kriyāsiddhāvaṅgabhāvaḥ
sādhanatāsampatyarthaḥ | ucyate |
ābhāsopagamo vyaktiḥ soḍhatvamiti karmaṇaḥ |
viśeṣāḥ prāpyamāṇasya kriyāsiddhau vyavasthitāḥ || 53 ||
(51) yogyadeśāvasthānena darśanapathamavatarati viṣaya iti
yogyadeśāpatiḥ ābhāsopagamaḥ svabhāvadasya darśane upakāraḥ |
yogyadeśasthitasyāpi
nīhārādiprakāśapratibandhakabhāvābhāvadipradīpādinimitā vyaktiḥ
aṅgam | viṣayasya
prakāśayogyadeśasthitasyāsatyāvarake'dṛṣyasvabhāvaḥ piśācādiriti
soḍhatvam, bodhakṣamatā, dṛśyasvabhāvatāṅgam | etairatiśayairdarśane
sādhanabhāvamanubhavati prāpyaṃ karma | ete ca darśanakriyāhetavo na
tatkṛtāḥ | grāmaṃ gaccchatītiyādāvapi yathāyogaṃ soḍhatvādayo
grāmādervācyāḥ | samīhāviṣayīkṛtā hi grāmādayo'vabhāsamānā
gamanādisahāḥ sādhayanti gamanādikriyām | evamadhīyamānaṃ vedādi
adhyamanasaham | mātaraṃ smaratītyapi mātā kṛtopakārā
krūrahṛdayasyāpi smṛtipathamanupatantī sādhayati smṛtim |
ipsitatamatvānapekṣāyāṃ tu saṃbandhitvasāmānyavivakṣāyāṃ śeṣaṣaṣṭhī
mātu smaratīti || 53 ||
evaṃ tāvād yathāvibhāgaṃ karma lakṣitam | idaṃ tu
vaktavyam--pūrvamuktaṃ guṇakriyāṇāṃ kartāraḥ sarve sādhanaviśeṣā iti
| tatkathaṃ karmatāsaṃbhava iti karmakartṛvicārapātanikāṃ karoti |
nirvartyādiṣu tatpūrvamanubhūya svatantratām | 
kartrantarāṇāṃ vyāpāre karma saṃpadyate tataḥ || 54 ||
(52) nirvṛtivikriyābhāsāpatikriyāsu yathāsaṃkhyaṃ
nirvartyavikāryaprāpyalakṣaṇaṃ karma svātantryaṃ kartṛtvamāśritya
tadapekṣayānyeṣāṃ nirvartyādikarmaviṣayatvād bahūnāṃ kartṛÃṇāṃ
nirvartanādivyāpāre praiṣalakṣaṇe karma jāyate tenāpyamānatvāt |
tathā hi nirvartate vikurute ābhāsamupaiti prakāśate iti kartāraḥ
svavyāpāre karmabhedāt pradhānakartṛvyāpāre vivakṣite
nirvartayati, vikārayati, ābhāsādiviṣayabhāvamāpādayatīti
kartṛpraiṣe svāmisannidhāviva bhṛtyā nyagbhavantaḥ karmabhāvamanubhavanti
|| 54 ||
evaṃ tarhi kartā karma bhavatīti karma bhūtvā kartā kathaṃ bhavati yena
karmakartṛtvasaṃbhava ityata āha---
tadvyāpāraviveke'pi svavyāpāre vyavasthitam |
karmāpadiṣṭān labhate kvacicchāstrāśrayān vidhīn || 55 ||
(53) bhidyate kusūla ityādau yadā kartṛpraiṣasya nirvṛtiḥ
kusūlāderviśarārurūpatayā svayameva bhidānubhavāt, lūyate kedāra
ityādavapi śuṣkataratvāt kartṛprayatnānapekṣaṇāt, pacyate odanaḥ
svayametevetyādāvapi saukaryātiśayavaśāt karturanapekṣaṇāt
tadā svavyāpāre svātantryāśraye kartṛtve śāstreṇa yatnena
karmakāryaprāptyarthaḥ karmavadbhāvo vidhīyata iti karmaṇi apadiṣṭān
vihitān vidhīn kāryāṇi kartā'pi san prāpnoti |
karmakartrantakavyāpārapātanena hi svavyāpāre kartṛtvāt
karmakāryāṇi na syuriti śāstrāraṃbhaḥ | kvacid iti |
karmasthabhāvānāṃ karmasthakriyāṇāṃ ca dhātūnāṃ viṣaya ityarthaḥ || 55 ||
nanu ca kartṛpraiṣe nivṛte tadāyataṃ karmatvamatra mā bhūt kartṛtvaṃ
tu kathaṃ yena karmakarteti vyapadeśaḥ | tathā hi | sati pradhānakartari
karmaṇaḥ svakriyāyāṃ vyāpāro dṛṣṭa iti tadabhāve karmatvavat
kartṛtvamapi kuta ityāśaṅkyāha |
nivṛtapreṣaṇaṃ karma svakriyāvayave sthitam |
nivartamāne karmatve sve kartṛtve'vatiṣṭhate || 56 ||
(54) dhātvarthānukūlyena ceṣṭanaṃ kartuḥ preṣaṇaṃ svavyāpāraḥ
kārakāntaviniyogalakṣaṇam | tadanabhidhīyamāne kartari pacyate
odanaḥ svayamevetyādau nivartate | kartrāśrayasya vyāpārasya kartari
pratyayenābhidhīyamāne dhātunā'nabhidhīyamānatvāt | pratīyamānatvaṃ
tu tasya dvayarthaḥ paciriti bhāṣyavirodhādasaṅgatamityākhyātam |
tāni dhātvantarāṇi 
iti ca vakṣyamāṇatvād viṣayavibhāgena dhātvarthatā kartṛvyāpārasya
granthakṛto'bhipretā | tasminnivṛte svasmin kriyāvayave
pradhānakriyāpekṣayā guṇabhūte svavyāpārekarma sthitam,kartṛtayā
bhūtapūrvagatyā tvasya karmavyapadeśaḥ | yadyapi ca
kartṛvyāpārevivakṣite svakriyāyāmevāvasthānaṃ taddvāreṇa
pradhānakriyānirvartanāt, kārakaṃ karmeti sāmānādhikaraṇyācca,
tathāpi sāmarthyādatra prādhānyenāvasthānaṃ boddhavyam | ata eva
kartṛvyāpārāpekṣayā karmatvasya nivṛtāvātmīye sarvatrabhavati
svakriyāviṣaye kartṛtve'vatiṣṭhate iti kartṛvyāpārāpekṣayā pūrvaṃ
karma bhūtvā saukaryātiśayapratipādanaparatayā tadavivakṣāyāṃ kartā
sampadyata iti bhavati karmakartā | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ | na kevalaṃ
kartṛvyāpārasyāvivakṣāmātre karmakartṛtā, api tu svavyāpāre
svātantryavivakṣāyāmapi satyām | tataścāprāptāni yagādīni
karmakāryāṇi 
"karmavat karmaṇā tulyakriyaḥ" (gh. 3.1.87)
iti śāstreṇa prāpyante | taduktaṃ vārtikakṛtā 
"karmakartari kartṛtvaṃ svātantryasya vivakṣitatvāt " (ja.od gh. 3.1.87)
iti | tatra parāyatopajane'pi bhidādau kusūlādeḥ parānapekṣā
prātipāditā bhāṣye 
"yastu khalu nivāte nirabhivarṣe acirakālakṛtaḥ kusūlaḥ svayameva
bhidyata iti |"
(g.eṇa. od dyaṇe ja.dddvodyaed aḍodhe)
nivāta iti samīrasampātamanavāpyeti kartṛvyāpāro vyudastaḥ |
nirabhivarṣa iti salilakalāsaṃsparśo vyudastaḥ | acirakṛta iti
kālāntaparivāsādīyamānaviseṣavyudāsaḥ | ete hi pātahetavaḥ
kusūlasya saṃbhāvyeran iti vyudastāḥ | evaṃ cādṛḍhasaṃniveśitvāt
svayameva viśīryata iti kartṛtvopapatiḥ | yatrāpi dātrahasto
devadataḥ paripatan dṛśyate lūyate kedāraḥ svayameveti atrāpi
tīvrakarakiraṇasamparkasamutsāritasnehaḥ parijarjaritanālaḥ
viśīryamāṇaḥ kedāraḥ tātsthyāt vrīhirūpo lavane sukaratāmeti
| sāmagrīsādhyatvācca kriyāṇāṃ pratyekaṃ vyāpārotkarṣaṇārthaṃ
svātantryādhyāropeṇāpyupapannaḥ prayogaḥ devadata cchinati, asiḥ
cchinati, sthālī pacatītyādiḥ | tatra ca karmaṇa eva kartuḥ
karmavadbhāvo nānyasya, cchidyate rajjuḥ, pacyate odanaḥ
svayameveti tadetaduktaṃ bhāṣye---
"tatrāpi yā'sau sukaratā nāma tasyā nānyaḥ kartā..."
(g.eṇa.od gh. 3.1.87)
iti | saukaryātiśayavadvyāpāre kedāre samīhite na
devadataśchedasya kartṛvyapadeśaṃ pratipatu yogyo'pi tu kedāra
evetyarthaḥ | odanaviklitau tu chedanavat kartṛvyāpāro'pi
nāvaśyamapekṣyaḥ | anapekṣitakartṛprayatnā eva hi taṇḍulā viklidyanti |
karaṇādikārakaviniyoga eva hyatra mukhyaḥ kartṛvyāpāro lakṣyate || 56
||
nanu caika eva pacyādirdhātuḥ kvacit kartṛvyāpāre
guṇabhūtamarthamāha pacatyodanaṃ bhinati kusūlaṃ lunāti kedāraṃ
devadata iti | kvacitu kartṛvyāpāre'pratipannaguṇabhāvaṃ
svapratiṣṭhamarthamāha pacyata odanaḥ svayamevetyādāvityeṣo'sati
rūpabhede dhātūnāṃ viveko durlabha iti kartṛpratyayavācyaiva bhāvanā
bhavatvityāśaṅkyāha---
tāni dhātvantarāṇyeva pacisiddhyativad viduḥ |
bhede'pi tulyarūpatvādekatvaparikalpanā || 57 ||
(55) karmakartṛviṣayāṇi pacyādīni viklityādināmātravacanāni
tadupasarjanavikledanādivyāpāravācipacyādidhātubhyo
dhātvantarāṇi boddhavyāni | arthabhedena śabdabhedāt | pradhānaṃ hi
śabdānāṃ pratipādyo'rthaḥ | pradhānānuyāyī ca guṇa iti nyāyāt
pratyarthaṃ bhidyate śabdaḥ | tadyathā pacervikledanavacanāt sidhyatiranyo
dhāturviklitivacanaḥ | dhātvantaratve'pi caikatvena
rūpābhedādupadeśo dhātupāṭhe ḍupacaṣ pāka ityādi | abhinnarūpaṃ
hyaekatvakalpanāsahaṃ vastu | yatra tūpadeśabhede phalabhedo'sti tatra
dvistrirupadeśaḥ, tadyathā kṣiyatyādīnāṃ vikaraṇabhedāt kṣi kṣaye
bhvavādau, kṣi nivāsagatyoriti tudādau divādau ca, kṣīñ
hiṃsāyāmiti ca krayādau || 57 ||
idānīm 
ekamāhuranekārthaṃ śabdamanye parīkṣakāḥ 
iti nayenāha---
ekadeśe samūhe ca vyāpārāṇāṃ pacādayaḥ |
svabhāvataḥ pravartante tulyarūpasamanvitāḥ || 58 ||
(56) tulyenābhinnena rūpeṇa svabhāvena yuktā ete dhātavo'bhinnāḥ ,
abāṣyamānasyaikākārapratyayasyābhedāvedakatvāt | anekārthatvameva
vādhakamiti cenna | śaktibhedenatadupapateḥ |
anekārthapratipādanaśaktirhyeko'pi śabdo dṛṣṭaḥ | yathā grāmaśabdaḥ
śālāsamudāyavāṭakaparikṣiptadeśajanasamudāyavacanaḥ | yathā
vākṣādiśabdāḥ | tatra karmakartṛviṣaye viklitimātravacanaḥ paciḥ,
pacyate odanaḥ svayameveti | devadatasya kartuḥ
pratyayenānābhidānātadvyāpārasyātra dhātuvācyatvābhāvaḥ |
pradhānabhūto hi pacyartho viklitiḥ, sarvatra saṃbhavāt | apekṣite'pi
hi kartṛvyāpāre devadataḥ pacatītyatra viklityarthatvādvikledanasya
kartṛvyāpārasyārthena rūpeṇa viklitereva prādhānyāt, atra ca
dvayasyābhidhānāt samūhavṛtitvam | atraiva dvayarthatopapatiruktā |
karmakartṛviṣaye tu kartṛpratyayasyābhāvāt tadvyāpāro bhāvanā
nābhidhīyate | karmaiva hyatra kartā, tadvyāpāraśca viklitireveti
saivābhidhīyate | tadyathā viklidyanti siddhyanti taṇḍulā iti
viklitereva kartṛvyāpārasyābhidhānam| evaṃ bhidyate kusūlaḥ
svayameveti dvidhābhavanaṃ dhātvarthaḥ | bhinati kusūlaṃ devadata iti
tadupasarjanaṃ dvidhābhavanamityudāhāryam || 58 ||
tadevaṃ vicitraśaktitvāccchabdānāṃ viṣayabhedenārthavivekaḥ samuditaḥ
| idānīṃ karmakartṛprasaṅgena 
"ṇeraṇau yatkarma"(gh. 1.3.67)
ityatra
"karmakartṛtvāt siddham"(ja.od gh. 1.3.67)
ityuktatvāt vicārayati---
nyagbhāvanānyagbhavanaṃ rūhau śuddhe pratīyate |
nyagbhāvanā nyagbhavanaṃ ṇyante'pi pratipadyate || 59 ||
avasthāṃ paṃcamīmāhurṇyante tāṃ karmakartari |
nivṛtapreṣaṇāddhātoḥ prākṛte'rthe ṇijucyate || 60 ||
(57) ihārohayate hastī svayamevetyayaṃ hastivyāpārābhidhāyī
prayogo'sti karmakartṛviṣayaḥ | tasya cānvākhyānopāyaparikalpanā
kartavyā | tatra rūpasāmānyānugamapravartitena
rekhāgavayavadavayavāpoddhāreṇa tadarthāśrayaṇena cānvākhyānaṃ
pravartyate | tathā ca yāvatīṣu sopānasthānīyāsu arthavibhāgabhūmiṣu
padaṃ vinyasyeyaṃ prāyogikī paryantabhūmiḥ prāpyeta tā
antarālabhāvinyo gaṇyamānā bhūmayo'vasthāśabdavācyāḥ | tatrādye bhāge
ruhidhātuḥ pratyabhijñāyate | tasya cānyatrāvasthādvayarūpo'rthaḥ prasiddhaḥ,
sa evehāṅgīkriyate | tadetadāha---
nyagbhāvanā nyagbhavanaṃ rūhau śuddhe pratīyate |
iti śuddhe kevale ṇijrahita ityarthaḥ | ārohanti hastinaṃ
hastipakā ityatra nyagbhavantaṃ nyagbhāvayantītyarthaḥ | atra ca
dhātvarthamātrapradarśane tātaparyam | vikaraṇapratyayādyaupādānaṃ tu
nāntarīyakam | nānyathā prayogārhaḥ śabdo labhyata ityārohayate
hastī svayamevetyasyaiva dhātorayamamarthaḥ pradarśito bhavati | evaṃ ca
kṛtvaikena śabdena dhātunā
nāntarīyakatayopātapratyayasahāyenāvasthādvayaṃ
nyagbhāvanānyagbhavanalakṣaṇamupātam | dhātubhāgādūrdhvaṃ cātra
guṇāyādeśalakṣaṇakṛtavikāro ṇic pratyabhijñāyate | sa yadi
yathāsthitaprakṛtyarthaniṣṭhe prayojakavyāpāre'nvākhyāyate tadā
nyagbhāvaniṣṭhāparaprayojakavyāpārapratītiprasaṅgāt prakṛtavaiguṇyaṃ
syāt | tena ruhidhātvarthasaṅkocalabhyaṃ madhye'vasthāntaraṃ
prakṛtyarthatvena ṇica āśrīyate hastivyāpāramākṣarūpam | tatparyante
pūrvaśaṅkāparihārāya darśitam |
nivṛtapreṣaṇād 
ityādinā | tatastanniṣṭhe prayojakavyāpāre prāgavasthopacayakāriṇi
ṇijutpāditaḥ kevalaruhitulyārthatāṃ sampādayati | tadāha
nyagbhāvanānyagbhavanaṃ ṇyante'pi pratipadyate |
iti | ārudyate hastī svayameva, ārohayanti hastinaṃ hastipakā
ityudāharaṇadvayamātra | ekena prayojyavyāpāro ṇic prakṛtyartharūpo
darśitaḥ | sa ca nānyathā śakyadarśana iti yagātmanepage
karmavadbhavāt prāpte nāntarīyakatayopāte | dvitīyena ṇijartho
hastipakavyāpāraḥ pradarśitaḥ | sa ca prayojyavyāpārapṛṣṭhapātitvena
śakyadarśana iti ruhirupātaḥ | pratyayādyaupādānaṃ tu nāntarīyakatayā
pūrvavat | tadetāścatastro'vasthāḥ | tata urdhvamātmane padasarūpo
bhāgo dṛśyate hastivyāpāramātrapratītiśca | etacca
karmakartravasthāmantareṇāśakyamiti pūrvāvasthāsaṃkocalabhyā sāśrīyate
| tadāha---
avasthāṃ paṃcamīm 
ityādi | tadevaṃ karmakartṛtvād ārohayate ityātmanepade siddhe 
ṇeraṇau 
ityatra
"karmakartṛtvāt siddhamiti ced yakciṇornivṛtyarthaṃ vacanam"
(ja.od 1 3 67) iti vārtikakāreṇoktam | tatra
"na vā yakciṇo pratiṣedhāt" (ja. od gh. 1.3.67)
ityādinā karmavadbhāvenaivātrātmanepadamiti siddhāntitam |
gaṇayati gaṇaḥ svayamevetyatra tu ātmanepadanivṛtyarthaṃ niyamārthatayā 
'ṇeraṇau'
iti sūtraṃ vārtikakāreṇa sthāpitam | nyagbhavanaṃ ca viklitivat
karmasthamiti ruhiḥ karmasthakriyo vākyakārasya karmavadbhāvaviṣayaḥ ||
59,60 ||
dṛśyate ca dhātūnāṃ prakṛtyantaṇyantānāṃ keṣāṃcit samāno'rtha
ityatra dṛṣṭāntamāha--
bravīti pacaterarthaṃ siddhyatirna vinā ṇicam |
sa ṇyantaḥ pacaterartho prakṛte vyavatiṣṭhate || 61 ||
(58) siddhyatyodana iti viklitimātravacano dhātuḥ |
pacatyodanamiti viklityupasarjanaṃ vikledanalakṣaṇaṃ pacatyarthaṃ
ṇicsahito'bhidhate sādhayatyodanamiti | evamārūhirṇyanto'pi
kacitadarthe vartata iti | idamatra tātparyam | bhinnāvevāruhī
prakṛtyantaṇyantūrapau pacatisādhayativadekārthau, ṇyante tu praiṣasya
nivṛtāvārohayate hastī svayamevetyapi nyagbhavanavacano'nya eva
dhāturevamupāyaparikalpanayā vyutpādyate | na tvayamatra paramārthato
ṇic yenārthanivṛtau kathamavatiṣṭhateti codyeta | anvākhyeyaṃ hi
laukikaṃ śabdamuddiśya pratipādakaśabdaniṣṭhamupāyaparikalpanayā
prakṛtipratyayavibhāgenāsatyena sarvatrānuśāsanaṃ
laukikārthānusāreṇaivaṃ kriyate |
nanvevamapyasatyasyāpoddhārapadārthasyāsaṃbhave kathaṃ śāsanaṃ
laukikārthānusāreṇaivaṃ kriyate |
nanvevamapyasatyasyāpoddhārapadārthasyāsaṃbhave kathaṃ taccchabdaḥ | evaṃ
tarhi karmavyāpārātiśayasya ṇicpratyāyyasyāsti saṃbhava ityadoṣaḥ ||
61 ||
evaṃ praiṣasya nivṛtyā karmaṇaḥ kartṛtvamupapādya
praiṣadhyāropeṇāpyupapādayitumāha---
keṣāṃciddevadatādervyāpāro yaḥ sakarmake |
sa vinā devadatādeḥ kaṭādiṣu vivakṣyate || 62 ||
(59) kaṭṃa karoti ityādau sakarmake dhātvarthe
devadatādipradhānakartṛviṣaye pradhānakartuḥ prayojakasya yaḥ
praiṣalakṣaṇo vyāpāraḥ asau yadā saukaryātiśayavivakṣā karmaṇaḥ tadā
kartāramantareṇa tatra karmaṇi vivakṣyate | yathā hi devadataḥ prayuṅkte
evaṃ kaṭo'pi sukaro devadatamiti bhavati vivakṣā || 62 ||
tadetat sāmānyenoktaṃ prakṛte yojayati---
nivṛtapreṣaṇaṃ karma svasya kartuḥ prayojakam |
preṣaṇāntarasaṃbandhe ṇyante lenābhidhīyate || 63 ||
(60) kartṛvyāpāraḥ praiṣaḥ karmaṇodhyāropita iti nivṛtapreṣaṇaṃ
tadavivakṣitaprayojakavyāpāraṃ svātantryavivakṣāyāmātmīyasya
prayojakasya prayojakatāṃ saukaryātiśayavaśāt pratipadyamānaṃ tatra
kaṭādikarmasaṃbandhini devadatādikartṛviṣaye preṣaṇāntare
prayojakavyāpārāntare ṇici sati kārayate kaṭaḥ svayameveti
