ṇyantāllakāreṇa karmakartṛviṣayeṇābhidhīyata iti ṇijabhidheyaḥ
praiṣo'stīti bhāvaḥ | atra copāyaparikalpanā praiṣau ṇico na tu
vastutastatsaṃbhava iti prayojyāpekṣābhāvaḥ | tathā ca
prayojyakarmasaṃbhavādavidyamānakarmāntarasaṃbandhāddhātorvidhīyamānaṃ
ṇeraṇāvityātmanepadaṃ kathamiti na codanīyam | tathā
hi-saukaryātiśayapratipādanāya karmaṇo vāstavaḥ praiṣo'dhyāropita
iti pratīte tasmin prayokatvasyāvivakṣā,
karmakartṛpratītisāmarthyāt tu bhūtapūrvapreṣaṇāvasthāvagamyata iti
tadviṣayo ṇiñ na nivartate | evaṃ karmakartṛprasaṅgena
ṇeraṇāvityatra vicāritam |
"tyadādiṣu dṛśo'nālocane kañ ca"(gh. kṣkṣkṣ.2.60)
ityatra vārtike 
"kṛdarthānupapatistu" (ja.odgh. 3.2.60)
iti coditam | kartari kṛditi vacanāt kartrā pratyayārthena
bhāvyam | sa cātra nopapadyate | sadṛśalakṣaṇaṃ hyarthaṃ tādṛśādayaḥ śabdāḥ
prāhuḥ, na tu darśanakriyāyāḥ kartāramiti vārtikārthaḥ | tatroktam |
"ivārthe cāyaṃ taddhitaḥ"(ja.od 3.2.60)
iti | ive pratikṛtāvityatra prakaraṇe'yaṃ taddhitasaṃjñaḥ pratyayo vaktavyaḥ
| tathā ca sa iva tādṛśa ityādyarthasiddhirityarthaḥ | evaṃ vārtikakāreṇa
siddhantitam | bhāṣyakārastu kṛdarthānupapatiriti codyaṃ
yathānyāsameva parihartuṃ 
"karmakartṛtvāt siddham"
iti | tatra dṛśaḥ kartṛsthabhāvakatvāt
kartṛvyāpāradarśanamātravācyatayā praiṣasyābhāvāt kathaṃ
karmakartṛtvasaṃbhavaḥ | preṣaṇādhyārope tu ṇic syāt | aṇyantaśca
sūtre dṛśirupātaḥ || 63 ||
atra
nivṛtapreṣaṇaṃ karma svakriyāvayave sthitam |
nivartamāne karmatve sve kartṛtve'vatiṣṭhate || 
ityanena nyāyena karmakartṛtvaṃ kecit samarthayamānāḥ prāhuḥ |
sadṛśādiṣu yatkarma kartṛtvaṃ pratipadyate |
āpatyāpādane tatra viṣayatvaṃ pratikriye || 64 ||
(61) sadṛśatādṛśādiṣu śabdeṣu vyutpādyeṣu yatkarmakartṛtvaṃ
pratipadyate yatkarmaṇaḥ kartṛtvamuktaṃ tatra dṛśerviṣayabhāvaviṣaye hi
āpatyāpādane kriye vācye iti tadāśrayeṇa karmakartṛtvasamarthatā |
tathā hi paśyatīti ko'rthaḥ | viṣayaṃ ghaṭādikaṃ
darśanaviṣayabhāvamāpadyamānamāpādayatīti | tatra viṣayasya
paricchedyatvena vyāpāra āpatiḥ, karmabhūtasya tadviṣayamāpādanaṃ
kartṛvyāpāro dṛśyartho na kevalaḥ | tatra kartṛvyāpāre āpatilakṣaṇo
viṣayavyāpāro nyagbhavatīti tatsannidhau svavyāpāre svātantryaṃ
viṣayasya tirobhavati | yadā tu yogyadeśavasthitaḥ
spaṣṭatarāvokamadhyaparivartī viṣayaḥ tadā sudarśano bhavan
svayamabhivyaktadarśanaḥ kartṛviniyogamapākarotīti svavyāpāre
svātantryavivakṣāyāṃ karma bhūtvā kartā sampadyata iti bhavati
karmakartṛviṣayaḥ | 
etatu nopapadyata ityāha |
kutaścidāhṛtya padamevaṃ ca parikalpane |
karmasthabhāvakatvaṃ spāddarśanādyabhidhāyinām || 65 ||
(62) iha dṛśeḥ kartṛsthaṃ darśanamātraṃ vācyaṃ paśyatīti | na hyato
viṣayabhāvāpatiravagamyate | kiṃ tu viṣayajñānameva dhātvarthaḥ |
viṣayabhāvāpatirhi sarvasya dhātvarthasyāstīti viśiṣṭameva darśanaṃ
dṛśerabhideyaṃ nyāyyam | tacca darśanaṃ viṣayapratiṣṭhamityasmāt
kutaścit sāmarthyāt viṣayākṣepadvāreṇa
viṣayabhāvāpatyādivācipadādhyāhāre
viṣayagatavyāpārapratyāyanātadupasarjanakartṛvyāpāracitvakalpane
darśanāsanādyabhidhāyināmapi karmasthabhāvakatvameva syāditi na
kaściddhātuḥ kartṛsthabhāvakriyo nāma syāt |
ataitadayuktamiti pratipādayitumāha
āpadyamānamāpādayatītyevaṃ vicakṣaṇatayā yataḥ kutaścit
padamadhyāhṛtya bhavatedamupakalpyate | tadevaṃ sati darśanādyabhidhāyināṃ
dṛśyādīnāṃ dhātūnāṃ karmasthabhāvakatvaṃ syāt |
ādigrahaṇādāsanādyabhidhāyināmāsiprabhṛtīnāmapi | na caitadupapannaṃ
kartṛsthabhāvakatvādṛśyādīnāmiti | atra karmasthabhāvakatvaṃ syāt
ityabhidadhatā kartṛsthabhāvakatvaṃ yadupapannameṣāṃ tadevaṃ
vighaṭetetyuktaṃ bhavati || 65 ||
atha kathaṃ kartṛsthabhāvakatvaṃ kathaṃ vā karmasthabhāvakatvaṃ
vijñāyetevyāśaṅkya prasaṅgād vyākhyātumāha |
viśeṣadarśanaṃ yatra kriyā tatra vyavasthitā | 
kriyāvyavasthā tvanyeṣāṃ śabdaireva prakāśyate || 66 ||
(63) yatra kartari karmaṇi vā viśeṣaḥ kaścit paridṛśyate tatraiva
kriyā vyavasthiteti boddhavyam | saparispandasādhanasādhyā kriyā,
aparispandasādhanasādhyo bhāva itiviśeṣamanapekṣya sūtrakārābhiprāyeṇa
kriyāpi bhāvo'bhidhīyata iti ṭīkākārasyābhiprāyaḥ | tathā ca 
lakṣaṇahetvoḥ kriyāyāḥ (gh. 3.2.126)
yasya ca bhāvena bhāvalakṣaṇam (gh. 2.3.37)
ityādāvameda eva sūtrakārasya kriyābhāvayoścetasi vartate |
anye tvetanneccchantītyāha |
anyeṣāṃ mate kriyāvyavasthā śabdaireva prakāśyate | etaduktaṃ bhavati |
ye'pi karmasthabhāvakāḥ pacyādayastatrāpi kartaryapi pariśramādiko
viśeṣaḥ paridṛśyate | tato naitadvayavasthāpakam, viśeṣadarśanaṃ yatra
tatra kriyeti | tasmāccchabdaireva ca yatra viśeṣaḥ prakāśyate prātipādyate
tatraiva kriyā sthiteti vaktu yuktam | śabdapramāṇakānāṃ hi śabda eva
yathā yathārthamabhidhate tathaiva tasyābhidānamupapannam | na tu
vastumukhaprekṣitayā | śabdaśca paśyati ghaṭamityādau
dṛśikriyāviṣayaviśiṣṭameva pratyāyayati | kāṣṭhṃa bhinatītyādau tu
bhidikriyāviṣayaṃ saviśeṣamabhidhata iti tadvaśenaiva kartṛsthabhāvakatvaṃ
karmasthabhāvakatvaṃ cābhidhānīyam | evaṃ ca dṛśyate svayameveti na
bhavatyeveti mantavyam | yatvaparispandasādhanasādhyo bhāva ityādi
lakṣaṇamabhidadhāya karmasthaḥ pacaterbhāva ityādikamabhidadhati
tadapyavyavasthitamiva lakṣyate | tathā ca pīlupāke ghaṭṃa pacatīti
ghaṭasyānalasaṃparkādavayavakriyādvāreṇāvayavasaṃyogavināśapūrvakaṃ
pūrvapūrvakāryavināśe utarotarakāryadravyavināśāt
saparispandaparamāṇuviṣayapākakriyāpi | tataścāvyavasthitamevaitat
|| 66 ||
eva tāvat prathamasūtralakṣitaṃ nirvartyavikāryaprāpyabhedena tridhā
bhinnaṃ karma vyākhyāya tatprasaṅgena ca nirvartyavikāryaviṣayaṃ
matabhedena nivṛtapreṣaṇaṇyantāṇyantadhātuviṣayamadhyāropitapreṣaṇaṃ
ca karmakartāraṃ pradarśya prāpyaviṣayasya pradarśanapūrvakaṃ
pratyākhyānamuktvā tatprasaṅgenaiva
karmasthabhāvakatvakartṛsthabhāvakatve matabhedenaiva vyavasthāpyedānīṃ
sūtrāntaranirdiṣṭānyapi karmāṇi vyākhyātumupakramate |
tatrākathitasūtre |
"kālabhāvādhvagantavyāḥ karmasaṃjñā hyakarmaṇām deśaśca |"(ja. od gh. 1.4.51)
ityuktaṃ tāvadanāraṃbhaṇīyamiti pratipādayitumāha |
kālabhāvādhbadeśānāmantarbhūtakriyāntaraiḥ |
sarvairakarmakairyoge karmatvamupajāyate || 67 ||
(64) akarmakāṇāṃ kathaṃ karmaṇā yogaḥ syāditi kālādīnāṃ
tatsaṃprayoge karmatvaṃ vaktavyamupajāyate | atrocyate | nātra
karmasaṃjñā vaktavyā yasmāt kālīdīnāmakarmakairdhātubhiryoge
karmatvamupajāyata eva | kathamiti viśeṣayitumāha
antarbhūtākriyāntaraiḥ iti | antarbhūtaṃ pradhānakriyāpekṣayā kriyāntaraṃ
yeṣāmiti te tathā | taiḥ yoge saṃbandhe sati | ataśca
kriyāntarāpekṣayā teṣāṃ karmatvaṃ siddhameveti kimupasaṃkhyānena |
tathā ca māsamāste, godohamāste, kośaṃ svapiti, kurūn
svapitītthādau māsādīn vyāpyāsta iti
vyāptyaṅgāyāmāsanādikriyāyāṃ dhāturvṛtaḥ |
vyāptyadikriyayāptumiṣṇamāṇatvāt kālādīnāṃ sphuṭameva
karmatvemitita yatnāntareṇa nārthaḥ | yadyevanamidānīmāsiprabhṛtayaḥ
kālabāvādhvakarmabhiḥ sakarmakāḥ sampannāḥ, tataśca tebhyaḥ kālarādiśveva
karmasu lakṛtyaktakhalarthāḥ prāpnuvanti na bhāve | tatra māsamāsyate
devadateneti bhāve lakāro na syāta | māsamāsitavyam,
māsamāsitam, svāsaṃ māsam iti ca kṛtyaktakhalarthā bhāve na syuḥ |
"bhāve cākarmakebhyaḥ" iti vacanātatrāpīṣyate |
tasmātkālādikarmasadbhāvādakarmakatvaṃ katham | sakarmakatve bhāve
lakāraḥ kathamiti | ucyate | dvividhaṃ karma, antaraṅgaṃ bahiraṅgaṃ ca
| tatra dravyakarmāntaraṅgam, tenaiva ca pūrvaṃ saṃbandhaḥ | bahiraṅgaṃ
kālādilakṣaṇaṃ karma | yad vakṣyate |
śaktipramāṇa.......
ityādiślokena | tatra sakarmakatvākarmakatvavyapadeśo'ntaraṅgeṇaina
nyāyyaḥ | tathā coktamakarmakāṇāmityucyata ityādinā bhāṣyeṇa |
evaṃ ca lādibhirapyantaraṅge bhāvyamityantaraṅgabahiraṅgatāmevāha
'ādhāra' iti |
godohaṃ svapiti | yāvatā kālena gavāṃ dohaḥ samāpyata iti
kośādiḥ punaḥ svakālaparimāṇena kriyāntaraṃ pariccchinati |
deśo'pi kutaścidavadheḥ prāpyamāṇatayā
pariccchinnakālasvāpādiviśeṣaheturiti bhinnāḥ kālādayaḥ | eteṣāṃ
kālādīnāṃ sarvairevākarmakaiḥ sakarmakaiścāpi dhātubhiryoge
prākṛtameva karmatvamupajāyata iti naiṣāṃ karmasaṃjñopasaṃkhyeyetyarthaḥ
| tathā hi | na dvitīyāvidhānārthameva karmasaṃjñātra, api tu lādyarthā
api | tathā ca 
"kālādhvanoratyantasaṃyoge"(gh. 2.3.5)
ityatra vārtikam 
"atyantasaṃyoge karmavallādyartham"(ja.odgh. 2.3.5)
iti | lakṛtyakhalarthā api saṃjñāphalabhiṣyanta ityarthaḥ |
atra kriyāntarasya vyāptilakṣaṇasyāpyāsyādidhātubhiraṅgīkṛtatvād
vyāptikriyāviṣayabhāvopagamanena māsādi prāpyaṃ
karmepsitatamameveti prākṛtamevaitat karma yathā ghaṭṃa karoti
śakaṭṃa karotīti bhāṣya uktam | tathāhi, māsamāste, godohamāste,
krośaṃ svapiti, kurun svapitīti vyāpanāṅgamāsanādi
dhātubhirabhidhate māsaṃ vyāpyāste, māsamāsanayā vyāpnotītyarthaḥ |
evamanyatra yathāyogaṃ yojyam | tadyathā valāhakādvidyotate vidyauditi
niḥsaraṇāṅge vidyotane dhāturvartate tathātra prāptyaṅge'rthe
dhātorvṛtiḥ | kriyāntaramevedamantarbhūta vyāptikaṃ
kālādiviṣayam | devadataḥ ki karoti, āsta ityatra tu
vyāptirahite dhāturāsanamātre vartate | tadetade yathāha
ekadeśe samūhe vā vyāpārāṇāṃ pacādayaḥ |
svabhāvataḥ pravartante tulyarūpasamanvitāḥ || 
pacati, pacyate svayameveti tathātra bodhyam | evaṃ copasaṃkhyānasya
pratyākhyānādakarmakagrahaṇamanarthakam | sakarmakāṇāmapi māsamodanaṃ
pacatītyādau māsādervyāptiviṣayatvena karmatāsiddhiḥ |
māsamodanapākena vyāpnotītyatrārthaḥ | etadarthaṃ cātra sarvagrahaṇaṃ
vyākhyātam | kālādikarmaṇā pacatyādīnāṃ dvikarmakatve'pi pradhāne
dravyakarmaṇi lādividhiruktaḥ
pradhānakarmaṇyākhyeye lādīnāhurdikarmaṇām |
iti | satyapi vāsyādīnāṃ vyāptikriyāntarbhāve kaṭa āsta ityādau
kaṭāderāsanayā vyāptirnāsti, api tu
māsādirevāsanādikriyāparicchedāyopādīyamāno vyāpyata iti
tasyaiva karmatā na tu kaṭāderupaśliṣṭamātrasyādhikaraṇasyeti sarvaṃ
sustham || 67 ||
(65) kartṛkarmavyavadhānenādhāraḥ kriyāṃ dhārayati yathā, evaṃ
māsādirapītyādhāracchāyāṃ bhajate | tathā hi udite juhoti, rātrau
bhuṅkte iti prayogaḥ | krośaṃ śete, 'kurūn svapiti' iti ca
sphuṭamevādhikaraṇatvamadhvadeśayoḥ kartṛdhāraṇāt | māsamodanaṃ
pacatītyādau dravyakarmaviṣaye ca dravyakarmadhāraṇaṃ sphuṭameva |
vyāptivivakṣāyāṃ tu karmatvamādhārabhāvasyāpagamanāditi iva śabdaḥ |
yadvā kartṛkarmavyavadhānena yathādhāraḥ kriyāyogaṃ bhajate tathā
dravyakarmavyavadhānena kālādītyupamārthaḥ | dravyakarmaṇā ca pūrvaṃ
kriyāyogaḥ | taddvāreṇa tu kālādikarmaṇeti bhinnā kakṣyā avasthāsya
karmatvasya | tathā hi
śaktipramāṇasaṃkhyāderdravyadharmādviśiṣyate |
kriyāsu kālayogo'taḥ prāgyogo dravyakarmaṇā ||
ityuktam | kaṭhinasya hi pākyadravyasya cireṇa niṣpatiḥ,
mahāparimāṇasya bahusaṃkhyākasya ca viparītasya tvacireṇeti
dravyakarmāpekṣaḥ kālādiviśeṣaḥ | evaṃ godohaṃ svapiti, iṣupātaṃ
pacatītyatrāpi vācyam | kośamadhīte svādhyāyam, kurūn
pañcālānadhīte
ityatrādhyayanapracayāpacayāyatāvadhvadeśayorvṛddhihrāsāviti
viprakṛṣyate kālādikarma kriyāsaṃbandhaṃ prati dravyakarmāpekṣayā | ata
evotarakālabhavameteṣāṃ karmatvam | samastasādhanasaṃbandhe hi vṛte
kriyāyāḥ paricchedahetavaḥ kālādayo'pekṣyante iti bhinnakakṣyāste 
'akathitaṃ ca' (gh. 1.4.51)
ityutareṇa yogena karmasaṃjñāṃ labhante, nepsitatamatvena,
īpsāprakarṣābhāvāt | duhyādiparigaṇane cākathitasūtre 
"kālabhāvādhvagantavyāḥ karmasaṃjñā hyakarmaṇām deśaśca | (ja.odgh. 1.4.51)
ityatraivoktam | guṇapradhānasannidhau ca pradhānameva kāryeṇa yujyata iti
dravyakarmaṇi lādividhiḥ | māsamodanaḥ pacyate, pakvaḥ, paktavyaḥ,
supacaḥ krośamadhīto'nuvāka ityādyaudāhāryam | dvitīyā tu
bhinnaśabdopādānā kālādikarmaṇyapi bhavati | eko hi
lādirguṇapradhānabhūtaṃ karmadvayamaśakto'bhidhātumiti pradhāne
kāryasampratyayaḥ | bhinnarūpaprātipadikadvayātu vibhaktirutpadyamānā
karmadvayamapi pratyāyayati |
yataśca kālādīnāṃ sarvadhātuviṣayaṃ bhinnakakṣyaṃ ca karmatvam,
atastaiḥ karmabhirdhāturyukto'dravyairakarmakaḥ |
lasya karmaṇi bhāve ca nimitatvāya kalpate || 69 ||
(66) yatrāsti dravyakarma tatra tadapekṣayā
kālādikarmaṇo'pradhānyāllādistatra na bhavati | yatra tu dravyakarmaṃ
nāsti tatra kālādāveva karmaṇi lādiḥ | lagrahaṇasyopalakṣaṇārthatvācca
| lakṛtyaktakhalarthāḥ āsyate māsaḥ, āsitavyaḥ , āsitaḥ svāsa ityādi |
akarmakavyāpadeśaśca dhātūnā dravyakarmābhāvanimitaḥ, kālādikarmaṇaḥ
sarvatra saṃbhavāt, saṃbhavyabhicārābhyāṃ viśeṣaṇayogāt, iti
kālādikarmasaṃbhave'pyakarmakavyapadeśasya
dravyakarmābhāvanimitasyāvyāvṛterbhāve lakāraḥ siddhyati,
māsamāsyate bhavatetyādi | māsamāsito devadata iti
cākarmakatvāt kartari ktaḥ | gatyādisūtre cākarmakatvāśrayā
prayojyasya karmasaṃjñā siddhayati māsamāsayati devadatamityādau | ata
eva tatra
"akarmakagrahaṇe kālakarmaṇāmupasaṃkhyānam"(ja.odgh. 1.4.52)
"siddhaṃ tu kālakarmaṇāmakarmakavadvacanāt"(ja.odgh. 1.4.52)
iti vārtikārtho nyāyasiddhaḥ | tathā ca bhāṣyam |
"na ca kecit kālabhāvādhvabhirakarmakāḥ | ta evaṃ vijñāsyāmaḥ kvacidye
akarmakā iti | athavā yena karmaṇa sakarmakāścākarmakāśca bhavanti
tenākarmakāṇām | na caitena karmaṇā kaścidapyakarmakaḥ | atha vā
yatkarma bhavati na ca bhavati tenākarmakāṇām | na caitat karma
kvacidapi na bhavati |" (g.eṇa.od gh. 1.4.52)
iti | parihāratraye ko viśeṣaḥ | ucyate |
śabdakarmākarmakāṇāmityatra avivakṣitakarmakā akarmakā āśriyante | evaṃ
hyāste śethe iti kriyāmātravivakṣāyāṃ kālādibhirapyakarmakatvaṃ
saṃbhavati | tathaiva pacādīnāmapyavivakṣayā karmasaṃbandhe'pākṛte aṇi
karturṇau karmasaṃjñāprasaṅgaḥ | tasmādyasyātyantamasaṃbandhaḥ karmaṇā
so'karmakaḥ | sa ca yadi dhātvartha āśrīyate tadā tasya kriyātmano
vivakṣāvaśena karmasaṃbandhāsaṃbandhayorbhede kālādibhirapyakarmakatve
vacanasya caritārthatvaṃ bhavet | tasmādavidyamāne karmāsyeti
dhāturanyapadārthatvenāśrīyate | gatyādyarthatvenāṇau ṇāviti ca
tasyaiva viśeṣaṇatvena prakramāt | rthadvārake ca dhātoḥ
karmayoge'yoge ca rūpasyāviśeṣāt akarmakatvaṃ nopapadyate ,
kālādikarmabhiḥ sarveṣāṃ dhātūnāmaviyogāt | evaṃ ca
viśeṣaṇasāmarthyād viśiṣṭā eva dhātavo dravyakarmasamanvayarahitāḥ
samāśrīyante | dravyakarmaviṣaye'karmakā iti pūrvaparihārārthaḥ |
dvitīye tu parihāre vartipadārthasya karmaṇaḥ sākṣāt
kriyopakārakatvenāntaraṅgatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ samāśritaḥ | tṛtīye tu
parihāre bhavati na bhavatītyabhidhānānniṣidhyamānasya saṃbandhasya
viśeṣa āśrīyate | yena karmaṇā kadācit saṃbandho bhavati tenaiva
dravyakarmaṇā akarmakāṇām, na nityasaṃbaddhena kālādikarmaṇetyarthaḥ |
dravyakarmāpekṣatvācca sakarmakākarmakavyapadeśasya |
"udaścaraḥ sakarmakāt "(gh. 1.3.53)
ityanena māsamuccarati dhūma ityātmanepadaṃ na bhavatītyāhuḥ || 69 ||
na ca kālādikarmaiva bhinnakakṣyam ,api tu
sarvaṃ vākathitaṃ karma bhinnakakṣyaṃ pratīyate |
dhātvarthoddeśabhedena tannepsitatamaṃ kila || 70 ||
(67) gāṃ dogvi payaḥ, pauravaṃ gāṃ yācate, gāmavarumaddhi vrajam ,
māṇavakaṃ panthānaṃ pṛccchati, vṛkṣamavacinoti phalāni, ityādi
sarvamevāpādānādiviśeṣakathābhirakathitam | dravyakarmāpi gavādi
payaḥprabhṛtipradhānakarmāpekṣayopāyabhūtaṃ nāntarīyakatvād
bhinnakakṣyam | dhātvarthasya hi dohādeḥ uddeśa eva bhedo viśeṣaḥ tena
hetunā gavādīpsāprakarṣarahitam | payaḥprabhṛti kiloddiṣya
dhātvarthaḥ pravṛtaḥ | tatra tūpāyabhūtā gavādayo nāntarīyakāḥ |
dhātvarthena voddeśo viṣayīkaraṇaṃ tasya bhedaḥ pūrvāparakālajam |
pūrvaṃ hi dhātvarthena pradhānaṃ payaḥprabhṛti viṣayīkṛtaṃ
paścānnimitabhūtaṃ gavādi | dhātvarthaṃ vā dohādikamuddiśya yasmād
gavādayo bhedenopāyatvena pravartante, ataste bhinnakakṣyāḥ | 'kila'
ityasmin pakṣe'ruciṃ sūcayati | tathā cābhinnameva karmeti
siddhantayiṣyati | nanu kālādīnāṃ dravyakarmāyatatvād bhavatu
bhinnakakṣyatvam, gavādau tu viparyayāt kathametat | tathā hi
gavādyāyatāni payaḥprabhṛtīni karmāṇi | tathā coktam---
"guṇakarmaṇi lādividhiḥ pūrvaṃ guṇakarmaṇā bhavati yogaḥ |
mukhyaṃ karma prepsuryasmādgavyeva yatate prāk |
tasmācchuddhasya duherbhavati gavā pūrvameva saṃbandhaḥ ||
gorduhinā payasastu prāk, tasmāllādayastasmin "
(g.eṇa. od gh. 1.4.51)
iti | satyametat | pradhānāpradhānakṛtastavatra kakṣyābhedaḥ || 70 ||
tathā hi 
pradhānakarma kathitaṃ yat kriyāyāḥ prayojakam |
tatsiddhaye kriyāyuktamanyatvakathitaṃ smṛtam || 71 ||
(68) yadudviśya phalabhūtaṃ kriyāpravartane sādhanāni sajjībhavanti
yadavāptāvudāsate tadīpsāprakarṣagṛhītaṃ pradhānaṃ
karturīpsitatamamiti karmasaṃjñayā prathamameva kathitam | tacca
payaḥprabhṛti tadarthaṃ tu yadanīpsitamapi nāntarīyakaṃ kriyayā
samanveti tadanavārtaviśeṣamakathitakarmāpradhānatvād
bhinnakakṣyamityarthaḥ | tadyathā gavādi | evamaprādhānye'pi copāyatvāt
prathamaṃ kriyāyoge lādividhau nimitatvāya kalpate| anapekṣite tu
payasi gaureva pradhānam, kṣīravicchedastu tasya mā
bhūdityevamanarthyapi kṣīreṇa samprati gosaṃskārārthaṃ dohe pravartata
iti |
apādānamutārāṇi (ja.od gh. 1.41)
gāṃ dogdhi' (g.eṇa. od dyaṇe aḍodhe )
ityudāharaṇamupapadyate vipratiṣedhena |
anyathākathitakarmatve'pādānādisaṃjñā prāptyabhāvāt kathaṃ
vipratiṣedhodāharaṇametadbhavet |
apādānādiviśeṣakathābhirasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ hyakathitaṃ karma | tadevaṃ
nimitanimitibhāvenāvasthitatvād bhinnakakṣyatve kathitākathitayoḥ
karmaṇorbhedādaprāptā gavādīnāṃ karmasaṃjñā 
akathitaṃ ca |
ityanenārabhyata iti sthitam || 71 ||
idānīm 
"nīvahyaerharateścāpi gatyarthānāṃ tathaiva ca |
dvikarmakeṣu grahaṇaṃ draṣṭavyamiti niścayaḥ |"(g.eṇa. od gh. 1.4.51)iti
bhāṣyoktaṃ vyācaṣṭe |
duhyādivannayatyādau karmatvamakathāśrayam |
ākhyātānupayoge tu niyamāccheva iṣyate || 72 ||
(69) ajāṃ nayati grāmam, edhān vahati nagaram, harati bhāraṃ
saṃvāham | gatyarthagrahaṇena gatibuddhītyādinā yeṣāṃ dvikarmakatvaṃ
te lakṣyante | 'tathaiva ca ityanenānuktasamuccayārthena jayatyādayo
gṛhyante, jayati śataṃ śatānīkam sa gargān daṇḍayati śatamiti |
atrepsāprakarṣayuktamajādikarma kathitaṃ pradhānaṃ payovat | grāmādi
tvavadhisthānīyatvāt guṇakarma gavādivadeva
nāntarīyakatvādakathitakarmaiva draṣṭavyam parigaṇanalakṣaṇena tu
niyamenākhyātānupayoge karmasaṃjñayā vyāvartyate | naṭasya
śṛṇotītyādau naṭādirupayoge hyakhyātāpādānamityanupayoge
viśeṣakathānākrāntatvād bhavedakathitakarmeti
parigaṇanalakṣaṇāniyamāccheṣo'ḍayaṃ ṣaṣṭhīviṣayaḥ || 72 ||
tadevaṃ pradhānetarabhāvena karmabhāva uktaḥ | kecitu pradhānamekamevedaṃ
karmetyāhuḥ | tathāhi
antarbhūtaṇijarthānāṃ duhyādīnāṃ ṇijantavat |
siddhaṃ pūrveṇa karmatvaṃ ṇijantaṃ niyamastathā || 73 ||
(70) antarbhūto vācyatayā ṇico'rthaḥ prayojakavyāpāraḥ praiṣalakṣaṇo
yeṣāṃ duhiyāciprabhṛtīnāṃ teṣāṃ prayoge gāṃ dogvi paya ityādau
"karturīpsitatamam" ityanenaiva gavādīnāmapi siddhā karmasaṃjñā |
yathā "gamayati grāmaṃ devadataṃ yajñadataḥ" ityatra ṇijantaviṣaye
prayojyasya prayojakavyāpāreṇāpyamānatvāditi 'gatibuddhi' iti ca
niyamārthaṃ sūtraṃ gamyādīnāmeva ṇyantānāṃ prayojyaḥ karma, nānyeṣāṃ
pacyādīnāmityaṇyantaviṣaye'tra na pravartate | tathā hi gauḥ payaḥ
kṣarati, devadataḥ tāṃ kṣarantīṃ kṣārayati,iti duherarthaḥ | tatra
kṣaraṇenāpyamānatvāt payaḥ karma, kṣāraṇenāpyamānatvād gauḥ |
yadyapi cārthena rūpeṇa payaḥ pradhānabhūtam , upāyabhūtā tu gauḥ
tathāpyābhidheyakena kṣaramopasarjanasya kṣāraṇasya śabdārthatvād
gaureva pradhānaṃ karmeti tatraiva lādayaḥ, duhyate gauḥ, payaḥ dogdhavyā,
dugdhā, sudohā, iti | "apradhāne duhādīnām" ityārthena
rūpeṇāprādhānyamucyate yadyapyapāyavivakṣāṃ vinā kṣāraṇaṃ nopapadyate
tathāpi
"apādānamutarāṇi"(ja.od gh. 1.4.1)
iti karmasaṃjñaivātra bhavati paratvāt | yadi hyakathitasūtreṇa
karmasaṃjñātrocyeta tadāpādānatvāt kathitatvenāsau na syāt |
pūrvasūtreṇaiva tu karmasaṃjñāyāmubhayasaṃbhave paratvād
'apādānamutarāṇi, "gāṃ dodhi" ityuktam | tathā
cātrāparādānavivakṣāvarṇanamasamīcīnam | evaṃ pauravaṃ gāṃ yācate,
dāpayatītyarthaḥ | pauravo gāṃ dadāti, taṃ prayuṅkte yācakaḥ
śraddhotpādanena iti yācerṇyarthaḥ praiṣo vācyatvenāntarbhūtaḥ |
yācanasya gauḥ karma, praiṣasya tu gauḥ | yadā tu sthāpayatītyarthaḥ tadā
vrajasyādhikaraṇatā, ubhayasaṃjñāprasaṅge tu paratvāt karmasaṃjñoktā,
adhikaraṇaṃ karma gehaṃ praviśatīti praveśopasarjanamatra sthānamarthaḥ
gehaṃ praveśanena vyāpya tiṣṭhatīti | nimitanimitibhāvena
praveśasthāne pratīyete | tatra praveśāpekṣayā karma
sthānāpekṣayādhikaraṇamityubhayaprāptau paratvātkarmodāhṛtam |
māṇavakaṃ panthānaṃ pṛccchati iti ācaṣṭe panthānaṃ māṇavakaḥ, taṃ
prayuṅkte ākhyāpayatītyarthaḥ | ācikhyāsati vā māṇavakaḥ, taṃ
prayuṅkte ācikyāpayiṣatītyarthaḥ | evaṃ pauravo gāṃ ditsati, taṃ
prayuṅkte yācaka ityapyartho'vagamyayate | evaṃ bhikṣerapyartho
veditavyaḥ | vṛkṣamavacinoti phalāni, muñcati vṛkṣaḥ phalāni , taṃ
mocayatītyarthaḥ | apāyavivakṣāyāmapi 'apādānamutarāṇi' iti
karmasaṃjñaivātra | māṇavakaṃ dharmaṃ brūte, pratipādayatītyarthaḥ | tatra
pratipaterdharmaḥ karma, praiṣasya tu māṇavakaḥ | 'māṇavakamanuśāsti
dharmam' iti | śikṣate dharmaṃ māṇavakaḥ, taṃ śikṣayatadītyarthaḥ |
karmaṇābhipreyamāṇasya tu māṇavarasyātra saṃpradānasaṃjñāyāṃ
pradhānavyāpāraviṣayayā karmasaṃjñayātra saṃpradānasaṃjñā bādhyata iti
vaktavyam | yadvā karturyadīpsitatamaṃ karma tenābhiprayemāṇasya
saṃpradānasya saṃpradānatvam | yathopādhyāyāya gāṃ dadātīti | gaurhi
dāturvallabhā | tata eva guṇavate dīyate | dharmastu karturnābhimataḥ ,
pūrvameva siddheḥ | tena tu māṇavako'nugrahītumabhipreta iti
prayojyasyātropsitatamaṃ karma, na prayojakasyeti na saṃpradānasaṃjñāyā
viṣayo'yamityanye | evaṃ nayatyādāvapi pūrvasūtreṇaiva karmatvam |
ajāṃ nayati grāmam, prāpayatītyarthaḥ | prāpaṇena pradhānena saṃbaddhājā
pradhānaṃ karmeti 
"pradhānakarmaṇyākhyeye lādīnāhurdvikarmaṇām"
(ja. od gh. 1.4.51)
iti nyāyasiddham, ajā nīyate grāmam, netavyā nītā, sunayeti |
evamanyatrāpi yatāyogamudāhāryam | satyapi cātra ṇyarthāntarbhāve
gatyādiniyamena prayojyasya karmasaṃjñā na vyāvartyate
sajātīyāpekṣatvānniyamasya, anyatra ṇyanta eva tena nivāraṇāt | atra ca
praiṣo ṇyartho dhātunaiva svīkṛta iti ṇic na bhavati || 73 ||
nanu ca vāstavena rūpeṇa payaādeḥ prādhānyātasyaivepsitatamatvaṃ
nyāyyam, na tu gavādeḥ
pratīyamānopasarjanabhūtakriyāpekṣamīpsitatamatvaṃ yujyate
pradānasannidhāne guṇasyepsāprakarṣaviṣayatvābhāvāt | na ca
kārakaprakaraṇe 
'sādhakatamaṃ karaṇam' (gh. 1.4.42)
iti tamabgrahaṇāt prakarṣo na vivakṣyate iti śakyate tatra vaktum,
'īpsitatamam' iti sākṣāt prakarṣavivakṣārthaṃ
tamapśruterupādānāt | liṅgasya tvanyatra kṛtārthatvam |
gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa ityādāvadhikaraṇasaṃjñā yathā syāditi | śrutyā hi
liṅgabāṃdhā | 'śrutiliṅgavākyaprakaraṇasthānasamākhyānāṃ pūrvaṃ
pūrvaṃ balīyo'rthaviprakarṣāditi nyāyavidbhiruktatvāt kataṃ
gavādeḥ pūrveṇa karmasaṃjñetyāśaṅkyāha---
karaṇasya svakakṣyāyāṃ na prakarṣāśrayo yathā |
karmaṇo'pi svakakṣyāyāṃ na syādatiśayastathā || 74 ||
(71) yathā kārakāntarāpekṣayā labdhātiśayasya karaṇasaṃjñāyāṃ
sajātīyakaraṇāntarāpekṣayā prakarṣo nāśrīyate, yena sarvasyaiva
karaṇakāryaṃ pravartate, aśvena pathā dīpikayā yāti, padābhyāṃ pathā
vrajati, balena pādenādata iti | anyathā aśvādereva prakṛṣṭatamasya
bhavediti | tathā karmaṇo'pi | karaṇādīnāmapi
kriyāyāmupayogādīpsitatamatvamiti iti siddhā
nimitanimitibhāvavasthitasyāpi sarvasya karmaṇaḥ pūrveṇova yogena
kamasaṃjñeti nāstyakathitakarmetyekīyamatenopanyāsaḥ || 74 ||
etatu na yuktamityāha |
karmaṇastvāptumiṣṭatva āśrite'tiśayo yataḥ |
āśrīyate tato'tyantaṃ bhedaḥ pūrveṇa karmaṇā || 75 ||
(72) iha sādhanabhāvasya sarvakārakaviṣayatvād yuktaṃ
yatkārakāntarāpekṣayā labdhātiśayasādhanabhāvaṃ karaṇaṃ svakakṣyāgataṃ
prakarṣaṃ nādriyeta | karma tvīpsāprakarṣabhāvaṃ kārakāntarāpekṣayā
pratipatumamasartham | na hi kārakāntarāṇīpsitāni | kartrā kriyayā
yadāptavyaṃ tadīpsitam | kārakāntarāṇi tu kriyāyai
karaṇīdīnyāptavyāni, na tu kriyayā |
tasmātulyajātīyāpekṣatvādatiśayasya karmaṇa āptumiṣṭatamatvaṃ
īpsitamityevamāptumabhipretatve āśrīyate | tamapa
upādānādatiśayo yasmādaṅgīkriyate tasmādīpsitārthāntarāpekṣa
evāsau prakarṣo'bhyupagantavya iti svakakṣyāgata eva saḥ | tathā ca
payaḥprabhṛti gavādīpsitāpekṣayā prakaṣṭamīpsitaṃ pūrvayogasya
viṣayo gavādestu pūrveṇāsiddhāyāṃ karmasaṃjñāyāmakathitamityanena
siddhirityeṣa eva pakṣo nyāyyaḥ | tataścabhinnakakṣyaṃ karmadvayameva tat |
svakakṣyāpekṣe'nāśrite tu karmaṇaḥ prakarṣe tamapaśrutiranarthikā syāt |
tathā cepsitamātrasya karmasaṃjñāyāṃ 
'vāraṇārthānāmīpsitaḥ'(gh. 1.4.27)
ityatra yavebhyo gāṃ vārayati, kūpādandhaṃ vārayati, agnermāṇakkaṃ
vārayatītyudāharaṇaṃ na syāt | yavodarapi karmasaṃjñāprasaṅgāt |
tataścaitat sūtraṃ nirviṣayaṃ bhavediti viṣayavibhāgārthaṃ
karmāpādānayoḥ svakakṣyāgataḥ karmaṇo'tiśayaḥ siddhāntitaḥ | tathā ca
vārtikam---
"vāraṇārthānāṃ karmānarthakyaṃ karturīpatitatamaṃ karmeti vacanāt"
(ja. od gh. 1.4.27)
bhāṣyamapi | yatu kathitaṃ purastād īpsitayuktaṃ ca tasya
siddhyarthamīpsitatamagrahaṇam iti vāraṇārthānāmityanena
kathitamiti 'akathitaṃ ca' ityanenāndhāderīpsitena kūpādinā
yuktasya karmasaṃjñā na siddhyatīti 'īpsitatamam' ityanena
siddhyatītyarthaḥ | yatpuranīpsitamātramapādānādisaṃjñābhirakathitaṃ
tasyākathitasūtreṇa gavādeḥ karmasaṃjñeti bhāṣye'bhihitam |
īpsitameva tu yat syāt tasya bhaviṣyatyakathiteneti || 75 ||
yat punaretaduktaṃ duhyādīnāṃ ṇijantavat siddhaṃ pūrveṇa
karmatvamiti tatrāpyāha |
ṇijante ca yathā kartā sakriyaḥ san prayujyate |
nayatyādau tathā kartā niṣkiyo'pi prayujyate || 76 ||
(73) gamayati grāmaṃ devadatamityādau ṇijante prakṛtipratyayavācye dve
kriye bhinne pratīyate iti tadapekṣaṃ dvayoḥ karmatvaṃ yujyate | gāṃ
dogdhi paya ityādau tu prakṛtivācyaikaiva kriyeti kathaṃ
karmadvayayogaḥ | na cāpyantarbhūtaṇijarthatvena tadupapatiḥ |
ṇijarthasyātrāyogāt | praiṣo hi ṇijarthaḥ | sa ca pravṛtakriyasya
savyāpārasya karturupapadyate | tathā hi tasya svatantrasya prayojako yo
hetuḥ tadvayāpāro ṇijuktaḥ | ata evāpravṛtikriyāṇāṃ karaṇādīnāṃ
prayojakasya svatantrasya karturhetusaṃjñā na bhavati | tadevaṃ
gavāderniṣkriyatvānna tadvayāpāreṇāpyamānatvāt payaādeḥ karmatā,
api tu dohena devadata vyāpāreṇaiva gopayasordvayorapi
āpyamānatvamityatiśayayogāt payaādoreva pūrveṇa karmatvam,
gavādestvakathitamityanenārabhyate || 76 ||
nanu ca yathā gaccchantaṃ saṃprayukte gamayatīti prayogaḥ tathā kṣarantīṃ
kṣārayati dogdhi ityartha ityāśaṅkyāha |
bhedavākyaṃ tu yat ṇyante nīduhitaprakṛtau ca yat |
śabdāntaratvānnaivāsti saṃsparśastasya dhātunā || 77 ||
(74) gaccchantaṃ prayuṅkte ityādinā gamayatītyādau ṇyante
prakṛtipratyayārthayorvibhāgapradarśanāya yad vākyam, grāmaṃ
prāpnuvantīmajāṃ prayuṅkate , kṣarantīṃ gāṃ prayuṅkte ityādi ca
nayati dogdhi ityādau prayogāccchabdāntaraṃ bhinnārthameva | na hyaeṣo'tra
dhātvarthaḥ paramārthataḥ, adūraviprakarṣeṇārthamātrakathanametat | yathā
rājapuruṣādau rājñaḥ puruṣa ityādi | tathā hi gaccchantaṃ prayuṅkta
ityādau vākye prayojyakarturnidarśanaṃ gamayatītyādau ṇyante
prayojyakriyāmātraṃ pratīyate | prayojyastu svaśabdopādāno
devadatamiti | yatra caitad bhedavākyaṃ vidūraṃ ṇyante tatra
nayatyādīnāṃ sutarāṃ vidūratā | na hyatra vākyārtho dhātūpādānaḥ
kaścit | atastasya vākyasya vākyārthasya vā duhādidhātunā nāsti
saṃbandhaḥ | tasmānnātra ṇyarthāntarbhāva iti kriyādvayāśrayeṇāpi
karmaviśeṣasamarthanamasamīcīnam | tadevamekaiveyaṃ vilakṣaṇā
karmadvayaviṣayā kriyeti 
sarvaṃ cākathitaṃ karma bhinnakakṣyaṃ pratīyate |
dhātvarthoddeśabhedena tannepsitatamaṃ kila ||
ityeṣa pakṣo'vasthāpitaḥ | aprādhānye'pi
cākathitakarmaṇo'ntaraṅgatvāt prathamaṃ kriyāyogāllādividhiruktaḥ |
nayatyādau tu prādhānyamajāderiti tatraiva lādayaḥ | ṇyante tu
ṇyarthasya prādhānyātenāpyamānatvāt kartaiva pradhānaṃ karmeti tatraiva
lādayaḥ | tataśca 
pradānakarmaṇyākhyeye lādīnāhurdvikarmaṇām |
apradhāne duhādīnāṃ ṇyante kartuśca karmaṇaḥ ||
iti nyāyasiddhamidamudāhṛtam || 77 ||
idānīṃ siddhāntamudghāṭayati |
yathaivaikamapādānaṃ śāstre bhedena darśatam |
tathaikameva karmāpi bhedena pratipāditam || 78 ||
(75) dhruvamapāye (gh. 1.4.24)
ityanenaiva sarvatrāpādānasaṃjñā bhāṣye'pi pratipāditā |
bhītrārthādiviṣaye'pi bauddhāpāyasya bhāvāt | abudhabodhanārthaṃ hi 
'bhītrārthānām' (gh. 1.4.25)
ityādinā bhedena kathanam | tathā ca vakṣyati |
nirdhāraṇe vibhakte yo bhītrāryānāṃ ca yo vidhiḥ |
upātāpekṣitāpāyaḥ so'budhapratipatye ||
iti | evaṃ karmāpīpsitatamamekameva, kartuḥ kriyayāpyamānatvasya
sarvatra bhāvāt | mandabuddhayanugrahāya tūtarasūtraprapañca iti
kārikārthaḥ | yadyapi ca payaḥprabhṛtyarthaṃ gavāderupādānaṃ tathāpyastyeva
dhātvarthena tasyāpyamānatvam | tadyathā taṇḍulānodanaṃ pacatīti | akṣa
taṇḍulānāṃ viklitiviśiṣṭā hi vikledanā pacerarthaḥ | tatra
viklityākhyaguṇadvāreṇa taṇḍulā api karma vikledanasya,
sākṣātvodanam | taṇḍulan vikledayan#edanaṃ nirvartayatītyarthaḥ |
itthaṃ ca guṇapradhānabhūtaikadhātuvācyakriyābhedāśrayeṇātra yathā
karmabhedaḥ tatā kṣaraṇopasarjanakṣāraṇārthatāyāṃ duhergopayasī
samameva karmabhāvamanubhavataḥ | tathā ca ṇyarthāntarbhāvo'pi yuktaḥ |
ṇyante'pi prakṛtyarthaviśiṣṭasya pratyayārthasya prādhānyānniṣkriyatvaṃ
svatantrasya | na hyatra ṇic kriyate yena 
sakriyasya prayogastu yadā sa viṣayo ṇicaḥ |
iti nyāyo'nuvarṇyeta | dhātuvācyastu ṇyarthaḥ | tadyathā sādhayatīti
ṇyarthaḥ pacativācyaḥ, bhedavākyaṃ tu bhavatyadūraviprakṛṣṭaṃ
bhinnārthamatyantaviśeṣāpekṣayā | sāmānyapekṣayā tvekārthameva
rājapuruṣavṛtivākyavat | tathā ca pratīyata evaitadvākyārtho'tra
prayoge | na cāpi gavādīnāṃ niṣkriyatvaṃ nyāyyaṃ kriyāsamavāyabhāve
kārakatvābhāvāt | ārabhyamāṇe'pyakathitayoge karmasaṃjñā na syāt |
prāk prayogānniṣkriyatvamiti cet ṇyante'pi samānametat | yathā
ca tatra saṃbhāvitakriyasya kartuḥ prayojaka #iti tadvyāpāropapatiḥ
tathātreti na kiṃcidavadyam | tamabgrahaṇasya ca
pratyākhyānādīpsāprakarṣānāśrayaṇam | yatu tamabgrahaṇasyodāharaṇaṃ
'dadhnā bhuñjīya' iti tatra samakakṣyatayā dadhyodanayorīpsitatve
tamabgrahaṇamakiṃcitkaram | saṃskārakatve hi
dadhnastatsaṃskṛtasyaudanasyaivepsitatamatvamiti
nāstyetadudrāharaṇam | tathā ca bhāṣyam |
"tasyāpyodana evepsitaḥ, na tu guṇeṣvasyādaraḥ"
(g.eṇa. od gh. 1.4.49)
iti | yadapi karmāpādānayorviṣayavibhāgapradarśanārthaṃ
tamabgrahaṇamityuktaṃ tatrāpi vāraṇārthānāṃ vārayitavyānāṃ
vāraṇamarthayanta iti vāraṇayogyānāṃ yadīpsitaṃ tadapādānamityevaṃ
vyākhyānādadoṣaḥ | tathā hi vārayitavyāḥ pratiṣeddhavyāḥ gavādayaḥ;
teṣāmīpsitā yavādaya evāpādānam | tadevamīpsitameva sarvaṃ karma
| itthaṃ ca duhyādyaudāharaṇārthaṃ na parigaṇanametat | naṭasya
śṛṇotītyādau naṭādi śṛṇotikriyayā nimitamātraṃ na tu
tayepsitatvena vivakṣyata iti karmasaṃjñābhāvaḥ | śṛṇotikriyāyāṃ ca
savyāpāro naṭaḥ kārakamupayogagrahaṇādapādānasaṃjñāyā vyudasyate |
na cānyā saṃjñāsya vihiteti śeṣo'yaṃ ṣaṣṭhīviṣayaḥ |
hetutṛtīyāyāstvayamaviṣayaḥ 
anāśrite tu vyāpāre nimite heturiṣyate |
dravyādiviṣayo hetuḥ kārakaṃ niyatakriyam || 
iti vacanāt || 78 ||
nanu nirvartyādibhedaḥ karmaṇo'bhihita iti kathamekameva
karmetyāśaṅkyāha |
nirvartyo vā vikāryo vā prāpyo vā sādhanāśrayaḥ |
kriyāṇāmeva sādhyatvāt siddharūpo'bhidhīyate || 79 || 
(76) iha sādhanāśrayaḥ śaktyādhāro niṣpannarūpo nāmapadairucyate |
ākhyātapadaistu sādhyasvabhāvaḥ kriyātmārtho'bhidhīyata iti
kriyādravyayoḥ siddhasādhyāsvabhāvarūpaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ śabdapratipādyena tadrūpeṇa
kathyate | yad bhāṣyam---
"kriyā caiva hi bhāvyate, svabhāvasiddhaṃ dravyam"
(g.eṇa.od gh. 1.3.1)
iti | tathā ca kaṭṃa karoti, taṇḍulānodanaṃ pacati, sūryaṃ paśyati
iti nāmapadaiḥ karma śaktyādhāramavilakṣaṇaṃ siddhatayā pratyāyyata iti
nāsti tasya svarūpabhedaḥ svagataḥ | kriyārthatvācca vastutaḥ sādhānānāṃ
kriyādharmeṇa vyapadeśaḥ | nirvṛtivipariṇatiprāptilakṣaṇā hi kriyāḥ
svasāmarthyāyatamupakāraṃ karmaṇi darśayantyo
vibhinnavyapadeśanimitatāmāsādayanti | evaṃ ca kṛtvā śābdena rūpeṇa
siddhatvāt karmaṇaḥ sādhanamāvo'pyupapadyate |
kriyāviśeṣāveśitavyāpadeśabhedānāśraye tu
kriyāviṣayakārakanimitatayepsitamityeva sāmānyākhyayā sarvaṃ karma
kathyata iti siddham || 79 ||
gatamadaḥ | iha kathamīpsitatvam, 'viṣaṃ bhakṣayati', 'corān paśyati '
ityāśaṅkyāha |
ahiteṣu yatā laulyāt karturicchāepajāyate |
viṣādiṣu bhayādibhyastathaivāsau pravartate || 80 ||
(77) vāyvādidhātuvaiṣamyanimiteṣu yatā''turasya hitamiti
bhojananiyamāsahiṣṇorabhilāṣo bhavati tathā
svāmyādibhayaparipīḍitasya kṣetriyavyādhiduḥkhārtasya vā paraṃ
viṣabhakṣaṭhameva jyāya iti manyamānasya bhavati viṣaṃ
bhakṣaṇenepsitamiti 
tathāyuktaṃ cānīpsitam | (gh. 1.4.50)
ityetadapi karma pūrveṇaiva siddham | tadāha bhāṣyakāraḥ |
"yaca eṣa manuṣyo duḥkhārto bhavati so'nyāni duḥkhānyanuniśamya
viṣabhakṣa
ṇameva jyāyo manyate |" (g.eṇa.od gh. 1.4.50)
iti | 'laulyāt ' iti | na prekṣāpūrvakāritāghaṭitā sarvatrepsā,
api tu prakārāntareṇāpi sambhavatītyarthaḥ |
bhakṣaṇakriyāviṣayabhāvāpatirevātrepsitatvaṃ tathāyuktagraha
ṇādevāvasīyate | sūtrāraṃbhe'pi paropahitaṃ cāvijñātaviṣabhāvaḥ
bhakṣyamāṇīpsitameva viṣam | evaṃ dṛśikriyayā corādīpsitameva |
yadīpsitādanyannaivepsitaṃ nānīpsitaṃ vṛkṣamūlādi tadapi
pūrvamanabhisaṃhitamapi tadānīmanupasarpaṇavelāyāmīpsitameva,
anyathopasarpaṇakriyaviṣayabhāvameva nopeyāt | tamabgrahaṇaṃ ca
pratyākhyātamiti śramāpanodanārthaṃ grāmagamane'pyantarā
vṛkṣamūlopasarpaṇamapi saṃhitamiti siddhyatīpsitamātrasya
karmasaṃjñā pūrveṇaiva | saṃjñāntarapūrva kamapi karmepsitamityeva
sāmānyalakṣamena siddham | grāmamadhiśete, nagaramupatiṣṭhati,
ityādāvupasargasaṃbandhe sakarmakatvāddhātūnāṃ paratvāt
karmasaṃjñāyāmadhikaraṇaṃ karma gehaṃ praviśatītyuktam |
anupasargatve'dhikaraṇasaṃjñā bhavati | akṣān dīvyati' iti
vijigīṣopasarjanapātanavacane dīvyatāvīpsitatvamakṣāṇām,
krīḍārthatve tu 'akṣairdīvyati' iti karaṇatākṣāṇāmityubhayasiddhau
samāveśārthaṃ sūtram---
'divaḥ karma ca ' (gh. 1.4.43)
iti | taduktaṃ vārtike 
'gatidivihetumatsu ca grahaṇam'
iti | asati samāveśārthe sūtre karmasaṃjñāyā avakāśo 'manasā
dīvyati' iti | manasādeva iti karmaṇyaṇpratyayaḥ | karaṇasaṃjñāyāstu
devano'kṣa iti karaṇe lyuḍiti paratvād dvitīyāṃ bādhitvā
tṛtīyaiva syāt |
'manasastṛtīyāyāḥ'(gh.  6.3.4)
ityaluki, karaṇasaṃjñāyā apyavakāśo manasādeva
ityetadevodāharaṇam | sūtrāraṃbhe tu cakārasahite
saṃjñādvayasamāveśānyathānupapatyā dvitīyāpi siddhayati | evaṃ
kudhadruhorapyanupasargayoḥ sampradānatā upasṛṣṭayostu paratvāt
karmatetyevaṃjātīyameva karma prāgeva pratipāditamasmābhiḥ punarapi
smāritam || 80 ||
gatametat | idamidānīṃ cintyate | iha karmadvayayoge
prādhānyamārthamāśrityapradhāne lādiruktaḥ | dvitīyā tu
prātipādikadvayādapyutpadyata iti tatra guṇapradhānabhūtakriyādvaya
viṣayāyāmapi karmaśaktāvekasyā lādinoktatve'paraśaktyabhidhānāya
dvitīyā kasmānnotpadyata ityāśaṅkyāha |
pradhānetakayoryatra dravyasya kriyayoḥ pṛthak |
śaktirguṇāśrayā tatra pradhānamanurudhyate || 81 ||
(78) ihārthānāmekavākyopātānāṃ samanvayo bhavati | tatra dvayoḥ
pradhānayoḥ samatvān mitho'ṅgāṅgibhāvo nopapadyate, nāpi
dvayorguṇayoḥ 
"guṇānāṃ ca parārthatvādasaṃbandhaḥ samatvāt syāt" (gt.cdv.3.1.12.22)
iti | guṇapradhānabhāvena tu samanvayopapatāvekavākyatā
bhavatītyāha---
'pradhānetarayoryatra'
iti || 81 ||
tatra pradhānānumāyitvād
guṇānāmapradhānakriyāviṣayakarmaśaktyabhidhāne dravyasya
guṇakriyāviṣayāpyabhihitavat prakāśyata iti pradhānānurodhaṃ
sāmānyenoktaṃ vyākhyātumāha |
pradhānaviṣayā śaktiḥ pratyayenabhidhīyate |
yadā guṇe tadā tadvadanuktāpi prakāśate || 82 ||
(79) pradhānakriyāvācino dhātorutpannena pratyayena lādinā yadā
pradhānakriyāviṣayā karmaśaktiḥ pratyāyyate tadā guṇakriyāviṣayāpi
sākṣād anuktāpi tadvat prakāśate itīyamanuvṛtirvyākhyātā |
atrodāharaṇamāha |
pacāvanuktaṃ yatkarma ktvānte bhāvābhidhāyini |
bhujau śaktyantare'pyukte tataddharmaḥ prakāśate || 83 ||
(80) 'paktvaudano bhujyate' ityudāharaṇam | atra ktvāpratyayasya
tumarthaghaṇānuvṛtyā bhāṣyakāramatena bhāve vidhānam |
vārtikakāro hi 
'ā ca tumunaḥ samānādhikaraṇe' (ja. od gh. 3.4.26)
iti papāṭha | yathāvakāśaṃ 
'śakadhṛṣaḥ.....'(gh. 3.4.65)
ityādisūtravidhīyamānāj ā tumunpratyayādanuprayogadhātunā
samānābhidheyā ṇamulādayaḥ pratyayā vaktavyā iti vārtikārthaḥ | tena
'svāduaṃkāraṃ bhujyate yavāgūrdeavadatena iti ṇamulā
karmābhidhānādyavāgūśabdād dvitīyā na bhavati | devadataśabdācca
tṛtīyā bhavati | anyathā kṛtatvāt kartari ṇamulutpatau
devadataśabdātṛtīyā na syāt | anenaiva nyāyena 'pakūtvau dano
bhujyate' ityatrāpi ktvāpratyayo 'bhujyate' ityanuprayogadhātu nā
samānābhidheye karmaṇyevotpadyata iti nāsti kasyāścidapi
sakteranabhidhānamitita vārtikakāramatenedamucyate | bhāṣyakārastu
'paktvaudanaṃ gamyate grāmaḥ " ityatrānena nyāyena dvitīyā na
syāditi vārtikaṃ pratyācaṣṭe | tumarthagrahaṇānuvṛtyā ṇamulādīnāṃ
bhāve vidhānaṃ manyate | tumartho hi bhāvaḥ | kṛttvātkartari siddhe
sektvāprabhṛtīnāṃ tumartha iti vacanāt karturapakarṣaḥ | na
cānyastumuno'rtho nirdiṣṭa ityanirdiṣṭārthāḥ pratyayāḥ svārthe
bhavantīti svasyāḥ prakṛterartho bhāvaḥ siddhaḥ | evaṃ ktvānte 'paktvā'
ityasmin bhāvavācini pacikriyāviṣayaudanasya karmaśaktiranuktā |
bhujikriyāviṣayāyāṃ karmaśaktau lenābhihita#ayāṃ tathaiva taddharmaḥ
tasya pradhanasyaiva dharmaḥ pratyāyitatvalakṣaṇo yasya taddharmaḥ prakāśate iti
dvitīyā na bhavatyodanāt | 'paktvaudanaṃ bhuṅkte ityatra
pacikriyāpekṣādeva devadatāt kartulatṛtīyāpyasmānnyāyānna bhavati
| pakvaudanaṃ gamyate grāma ityatra tu gamipratyayenaudanagatā
karmaśaktirnābhihiteti bhavati dvitīyā | yadyapi ca
sādhanaikārthasamavetāyāṃ saṃkhyāyāṃ bhujyata iti vibhaktirutpadyate
tathāpi paciviṣayakarmopādhisaṃkhyānabhidhānād bhaved dvitīyeti
nyāyo'yamuddhoṣyate | ekatvādiṣvapi hi vibhaktayartheṣyavaśyaṃ
karmādayo'ṅgatvenāpekṣaṇīyāḥ || 83 ||
udāharaṇāntaramāha |
iṣeśca gamisaṃsparśād grāme yo lo vidhīyate | 
tatroṣiṇaiva nirbhogaḥ kriyate gatikarmaṇaḥ || 84 ||
(81) 'iṣyate grāmo gantum' ityudāharaṇam | atreṣerubhe karmaṇī,
gamergrāma eveti grāmastheṣiviṣayāyāṃ karmaśartāvibhihitāyāṃ
grāmaviṣayāpi tadvat prakāśate | tatra gamanārthameṣaṇām | tathā ca
kriyāyāṃ kriyārthāyāmupapade tumunnati phalabhūtātra gamikriyā
pradhānaṃ neṣiriti śaṅkyāmudāharaṇāntaramidam | ākhyātapadavācye
hyarthe nirvartyatvāt pradhānatā vartate | paktvā bhujyata ityatra
tvārthamapi prādhānyamasti | pākasya bhojanārthatvāt |
'gāmisaṃsparśād' iti | iṣerdravyāntarasaṃbandhaḥ kriyāntaramukhenaiva

tvekavākyopātagamidvāreṇetyarthaḥ | nirbhogaḥ carvaṇam, ākhyānam,
iti yāvat | 'iṣiṇaiva' iti | iṣipratyayena lakāreṇa karmaṇi
vihitena | tantreṇa hi śaktidvayamapyabhidadhāti pratyaya iti
vākyapadīye nirṇītam || 84 ||
matāntareṇāpyatropapatimāha |

odanaṃ pacatiḥ so'sāvanumānāt pratīyate || 85 ||

kiṃcidodano bhujyata ityarthaḥ syāt | tathā ca

'so'sāvanumānāt pratīyate' iti | sa iti parāmṛṣṭasyaudanasya

| etacca satyeva guṇapradhānabhāve nānyatheti pūrvokta eva

pūrvakālasyeti guṇapradhānabhāvopapatāvekavākyatopapatiḥ || 85 ||
na kevalaṃ karmākhyakārakaviṣayo'yaṃ nyāyaḥ, yāvat

(83) 'bhuktvā nagaro'bhiniviśyate' ityatrāminiviśaśceti

karmaśaktirna pratīyeteti pradhānoparodho guṇena kṛtaḥ syāt | na
caiṣa nyāyyaḥ | pradhānānuyāpitvād guṇānāṃ pradhānavirodhini

gatametat | iha kathaṃ karmatvaṃ 'guḍaṃ bhakṣayati' iti | guḍo hi
nirvṛto bhakṣaṇakriyāgataṃ na kaṃcana viśeṣamākāṅkṣati tathepsitam
| api tu bhakṣaṇakriyāyā āśrayaviśeṣapradarśanārthamupādīyata iti

dantāvacūrṇanādi na bhakṣaṇakriyāphalam, api

phalamiti nāsti kriyāṃkṛto'tra kaścanotpatyādiviśeṣaḥ | taduktaṃ

"īpsitasya karmasaṃjñāyāṃ nirvṛtasya kārakatve karmasaṃjñāprasaṅgaḥ,

(ja.od gh. 1.4.49)

tatra kathaṃ guḍāderīpsitatvamityetad vyākhyātumāha |
yannirvṛtāśrayaṃ karma prāpterapracitaṃ punaḥ |
bhakṣyādiviṣayāpatyā bhidyamānaṃ tadīpsitam || 87 ||
(84) nirvṛto niṣpanno'ta eva nirākāṅkṣaḥ āśrayo guḍādi dravyaṃ
yasya karmamaḥ karmaśakteriti nirvatyakarmatāvyudāsaḥ |
vikārānākāṅkṣaṇācca vikāryatāpi vyadastā | 'prāpterapracitam'
iti | prāpterbhakṣaṇakriyāsaṃbandhādyo'pi
pracāyāpacayayorviśeṣastenāprayuktamapracitamiti prāpyakarmatāpi
vyudastā pūrvapakṣe | ādyena hi kārikārdhena pūrvapakṣavārtikārthaḥ
saṃgṛhītaḥ | dvitīyenārdhenotaravārtikavyākhyānam |
bhakṣibhujyādikriyāviṣayabhāvāpatyā bhidyamānaṃ viśiṣyamāṇaṃ
guḍādīpsitaṃ prāpyametat karmetyarthaḥ | tathā hi, na
bhakṣaṇādikriyaivātrepsitā | evaṃ hi loṣṭhamapi bhayakṣitvā kṛtī
bhavet | nāpi guḍaḥ kriyāntarāśrayatvenepsitaḥ, api tu
bhakṣaṇāśrayatvenetyubhayamāśrayāśrayibhāvena samuditamīpsitam | yasya
hi guḍabhakṣaṇamīpsitaṃ tasya guḍo'pīpsitaḥ | bhakṣaṇakriyāsaṃbandha eva
cātra kriyākṛto viśeṣaḥ prāpyakarmatāyāṃ
viśeṣāntarasyānākāṃkṣaṇāt | anyathā nirvatyavikāryābhyāṃ ko
viśeṣaḥ syāt | bhakṣaṇasya prītyatiśayo'tra phalam | sa ca guḍasya
tatsaṃbandhe sati ghaṭata ityayameva viśeṣaḥ | kriyākṛtaḥ
svānurūpaphalapralavayogyatā hi saṃskāraḥ | nanu ca tṛptilakṣaṇāyāṃ
prītau guḍo'ṅgamiti kathaṃ pradhānakārakarūpaṃ karmatvamasyāḥ | ucyate
| tṛptyaṅgatāyāṃ guḍasyāyamitikartavyātaviśeṣo
yadbhakṣikriyāviṣayīkaraṇe tāṃ prati prādhānyam, nānyathā tṛptiḥ
sampādayituṃ śakyā | tadasya tṛptyaṅgabhāve'yaṃ prāthamakalpiko
vyāpāraḥ | tadyathā guruḥ sevyamāno dharmotpatāvaṅgatāmeti,
taccāsya paricaraṇakriyāviṣayabhāvagamanamantareṇa na ghaṭanāmetīti
guruṃ śuśrūṣata iti prādhānyopapatiḥ | tadevamatra pūrvapakṣe
dravyepsāpahnaveva kriyāmātrasyepsā darśitā | atastadānuguṇyena
parihāre dravyasyāpīpsā pradarśitā | yadi tarhi
kriyāviṣayabhāvamātragamanena karmatāsiddhiḥ tathā sati sarvatra
kriyāmātre'pi sakarmakatā syāt | tataścākarmakavyapadeśāḥ śāstre
virudhyeran | na ca virudhyante | na hyavaśyaṃ kriyāmātre
bāhyaviṣayasaṃbhavaḥ | kāciddhi kriyā kartaryeva viśrāmyati | na tu
bāhyabhāvamapekṣate | tadyathā āste, śete ityādi | dvayarthaḥ paciriti
vacanāt sarvadhātuviṣaye'pi bhāvanālakṣaṇe vyāpāre dhātvartha evātra
bhāvanāparyavasānāt sa eva bhāvyaḥ | 'āsanaṃ karoti' śayanaṃ karoti
iti | ata evātra bāhyabhāvaniṣṭhā bhāvanā tatra tena bāhyaena karmaṇā
bhāvyena sakarmakatvam | tatra ca kimityanuyujyate | pacati
kimodanamityādi | dhātvarthasya hi sarvatrāvyabhicārād bhāvyatve'pi
na tena sakarmakākarmakavibhāgaḥ śakyakriyaḥ [kālādikarmaṇeva bāhyaṃ tu
karma vyabhicaratīti tadapekṣo'yaṃ vibhāgaḥ | ataśca śuddhāyā bhāvanāyā
dhātvarthaḥ karma, dhātvarthaviśiṣṭāyāṃ tu bāhyaṃ karma | ata eva
bāhyaniṣṭhāyāṃ bhāvanāyāṃ dhātvarthaḥ karaṇamiti nyāyavidaḥ |
tenodanaṃ pacati, pākenaudanaṃ bhāvayatīti vākyārthaḥ | yadyapīha
darśane bhāvanā dhātvartha eva tathāpi phalaparyantāsau kartṛvyāpārarūpā
dīrghatarāvayavakriyāmātrāt pṛthagvyavahārasahā | evaṃ sarvatra
yojyam || 87 ||
kvacit punarvastuto bāhyakarmasadbhāve'pyakarmakavyapadeśo
bhavatītyāha |
dhātorarthāntare vṛterdhātvarthenopasaṃgrahāt |
prasiddheravivakṣātaḥ karmaṇo'karmikā kriyā || 88 ||
(85) prasiddhādarthādanyatrārthe yadā dhāturvartate tadā tatrāsya
karmasamanyavayarahitakriyā vācyā pratīyate | tadyathā
vahatirdeaśāntaraprāpaṇavacanaḥ sakarmakaḥ prasiddho bhāraṃ vahatīti | sa
yadā nadī vahatītyatra syandanakriyāmabhidhāyī tadā syandanasya
jalasrutirūpasya nadyātmabhūtatvāt kimityanuyogābhāvādakarmaka iti
dhātvarthamātrāpekṣayeyaṃ bhāvanā vahati syandanaṃ karotīti | yadāpi
dhātvarthakriyāyāṃ karmāntarbhavati tadāpyakarmakatvam | tadyathā 'jīvati'
iti | 'prāṇān dhārayati' iti hyatra dhātvartha iti prāṇalakṣaṇasya
karmaṇo'ntarbhāvaḥ evaṃ 'mriyate' 'prāṇān jahāti' iti | tathā 'asti'
'ātmānaṃ vibharti' iti | yatrāvyabhicāreṇa pratiniyatasya karmaṇaḥ
pratītistatrāpyarkārmikā kriyeti vyavahāraḥ | tadyathā 'varṣati' iti |
atra hi yathā varṣaṇakriyāyāmapyabhicārāddevaḥ kartā pratīyate tathā
karmāpi jalalakṣaṇamiti prasidderakarmakatvāśrayaḥ kartari kta udāhṛtaḥ
'vṛṣṭo devaḥ' iti | yatvaprasiddhaṃ karma tena sakarmakatvamevāsyāḥ
kriyāyāḥ, 'rudhiraṃ varṣati' 'śaran varṣati' iti | 'pāṃsavo vṛṣṭāḥ'
iti karmaṇi ktaḥ | vidyamāno'pi vā yatra karmasaṃbandhaḥ
kriyāmātrapratipādanatātparyānna vivakṣyate tatrāpyakarmakatvaṃ
saṃbhavati | tadyathā neha pacyate, neha bhujyate iti |
kriyāpratiṣedhatātparyādbhāve lakāraḥ | dīkṣito na dadāti, na
pacati, na juhoti iti karmaṇo'vivakṣā | atra kimiti nāpekṣyate |
kriyāpratiṣedhatātparyāt || 88 || 
tadevaṃ caturbhiḥ prakārairakarmakavyapadeśāḥ śāstre bhavanti |
evamanye'pi prakārāḥ saṃbhavantītyāha |
bhedā ya ete catvāraḥ sāmānyena pradarśitāḥ |
te nimitādibhedena bhidyante bahudhā punaḥ || 89 ||
(86) ya ete sakarmakāṇāmapi dhātūnāmakarmakatvanibandhanabhūtā
bhedāścatvāro'bhihitāste nimitādibhedenānekaprakārā jāyante |
nimitaṃ sahāya upasargādiḥ | ādiśabdāddeśakālādayaḥ | tadyathā caratiḥ
deśāntaraprayogasya sāmarthyādbhavati | 'bāṣpa uccarati', 'dhūma
uccarati' iti | uccchabdavaśāddhāturayamūrdhvapravṛtāvakarmakaḥ |

'akarmakācca' (gh. 1.3.45)
ityātmanepadam vākyasāmarthyāt kvacidakarmakatvābhivyaktiḥ | tadyathā
'nadī vahati' iti | nadīlakṣaṇakartṛviśeṣasāmarthyāt
syandanavacanatāyāmakarmakatvam | evaṃ 'vāyurvahati' iti | tathā

kṛtvākarmakavyapadeśanimitaṃ śāstramatra viṣaye pravartate 
'muco'karmakasya guṇo vā' (gh. 7.4.57)
iti | 'mokṣyate vatsaḥ svayameva' iti | evamarthāntare
vṛtirdhātorakarmakatvanibandhanaṃ nimitādibhedena bhidyate |

kriyā svarūpasāmarthyalakṣaṇānnimitāt tirohitabhedatve
karmaṇo'ntarbhāve'karmakavyapadeśo jīvatyādau |
kvacidanvākhyānavyavasthayā nirdhāryamāṇarūpabhedasyāpyantarbhāvaḥ |
tadyathā putrīyatau putrakarmaṇaḥ | paramārthato'tra putralakṣaṇaṃ karma
kriyāśarīrānupraviṣṭaṃ karma kriyāśarīrānupraviṣṭaṃ bhedenāsatyena
prakriyāyāmanvākhyānanimitamāśrīyate tathā ca vakṣyati 
putrīyatau na putro'sti viśeṣocchā tu tādṛśī |
vinaiva putrānugamādyā putre vyavatiṣṭhate || 
iti | ācārakyaci tvatropamānakarmāntarbhūtamupameyakarmaṇā tu
sakarmakatvam | 'putrīyati chātram ' iti | 'muṇḍayati māṇavakam '
iti tu sāmānyakarmāntarbhūtaṃ viśeṣakarmaṇā tu sakarmakatvam | evaṃ
'miśrayati tilān' ityādau | bāṣpāyate, ūṣmāyate, romanthāyate
ityādau viśeṣakarmaṇa eva nirdhāritarūpasyāntarbhāvaḥ | paceḥ pārthivaṃ
bhideśca sāvayavaṃ karma prasiddham, na tvantarbhūtam | tasya
svaśabdenāpi vacanāt, pacati pākyam, 'bhinati bhedyam' iti |
antarbhāve tu svaśabdena nirdeaśo na syāt | na hi bhavati 'jīvati
prāṇān' iti | 'jalaṃ varṣati' iti tu bhavati | antarbhāvācca karmaṇo
'namasyati devān ' ityatra namaḥśabdayoge caturthī na bhavati | tathā ca
vārtike 
'namasaḥ kyaci dvitīyānupapatiḥ'(ja.od gh. 3.1 19)
iti codayitvā
'prakṛtyantāratvāt siddham' (ja. od gh. 3.1.19)
iti parihṛtam | anenaivāśayena dhātvantārameva namasyatiśabdaḥ
pūjārtho na saṃbhavatyevātra namaḥśabdo'vayavakalpanādvāreṇa tvevaṃ
vyutpādyata ityarthaḥ | bāṣyakārastvanvākhyānavelāyāṃ namasaḥ
prakṛtitvena parigrahāt saṃbhavaṃ manyamānaḥ punaścodayati |
'nanu namasyatiśabde namaḥ śabbdo'sti, tena yoge caturthī
prāpnoti'
(g.eṇa. od ja. aḍodhe )
iti | pariharati ca 
'arthavato namaḥśabdasya grahaṇam, na ca namasyatiśabde
namaḥśabdo'rthavān'
(g.eṇa. od ja. aḍodhe )
iti | subdhātuvṛtiriyam | atra copasarjanārthaḥ pratyayārthe
guṇībhūta iti namo devebhya ityatra pradhānabhūtanamaḥśabdayoge
sāvakāśatvādatra caturthī na bhavatīti bhāṣyārthaḥ | prasiddherapi
yatrākarmakatvaṃ tatrāpyavāntaro bhedo deśakālavaśājjāyate | tadyathā
dakṣiṇāpathe pūrvāhṇe 'pacyatām' ityukte yavāgūkarmakaḥ
pāko'vagamyate | aparāhṇe punarodanasādhanaḥ | māṃsabhūyiṣṭhe tu
deśe māsasādhanaḥ pākaḥ prasiddhaḥ | kriyāsvarūpasāmarthyāt kvacit
prasiddhaṃ niyataṃ karma, 'varṣati' iti jalaṃ pratīyate |
kartṛsāmarthyādapi karma prasiddham, yathā 'durjanaḥ karoti' ityukte
upaghātalakṣaṇaṃ karma gamyate | sujanaḥ karoti' iti
tūpakāro'vagamyate | goṣṭhībhedena deśabhedena cāparimitā
prasiddhiranugantavyā, kiyadudāhriyate | avivakṣāpi kvacit
sādṛśyāmātrapratipādanaparatayā vākyasya karmaṇo dṛśyate | tadyathā
"anuvadate kaṭhaḥ kalāpasya" ityatra kaṭhakalāpayorbhāṣaṇasādṛśyaṃ
pratipādyamiti karma na vivakṣitam | tathā karmaśaktiviśeṣapratipādane
'devadataḥ kiṃ karoti' iti praśne 'pacati' 'bravīti' iti
karmasaṃbandho na vivakṣyate | kriyāprabandhāvyuparamapratipādane'pi na
vivakṣyate karma 'pacatyeva dadātyeva' iti yatāyogamanusaraṇīyaṃ
prayogajātamiṣṭam || 89 ||
iti bhūtirājatanayahelārājakṛtau prakīrṇaprakāśe karmādhikāraḥ ||

iha svādividhivākye yayā''nupūrvyā vibhaktipratyayāḥ

(89) pradhānasya kriyālakṣaṇasyārthasya prasiddhaye prakṛṣṭopakārakasya

kartrantaraprayogābhāvāt | pāratantryayoge'pi sādhanāntarāṇāṃ

dūrādeva sādhayanti | karaṇavyāpārasamanantarameva tu kriyāsiddhiḥ ||
94 ||

siddhau satyāṃ hi sāmānyaṃ sādhakatvaṃ prakṛṣyate || 95 ||
(92) iha kriyāsiddhiviṣaye sādhanāntarebhyo'tiśayaḥ karaṇasya
vivakṣitaḥ | yadā ca pravṛtāni gṛhītavyāpārāṇi kārakāṇi tadā

nyagbhavanti | sādhanānyanāviṣṭasvavyāpārāṇi svātantryanibandhanaṃ
sāmānyabhūtaṃ sādhanavyapadeśameva nāsādayantīti kutaḥ tadapekṣayā
karturatiśayaḥ syāt | tadāha
'siddhau satyāṃ hi sāmānyaṃ sādhakatvaṃ prakṛṣyate |'
iti | yatsāmānyaṃ sādhanatvaṃ tatsiddhau niṣpatau satyāṃ
prakarṣamāsādayati | niṣpatiśca tasya kartṛviniyogādutarakālaṃ
svavyāpārāveśe sati | tathā ca tasyāṃ
daśāyāmatiśayaścintyamāno'dhikaraṇādestulyakakṣyāt
karaṇasyaivāvatiṣṭhate na tu kartuḥ | sa hi svāmī prākpravṛti
svatantro na mṛtyaiḥ saha spardhate | sajātīyāpekṣo hi prakarṣaḥ |
kartā tu vijātīya eva, kārakāntarāṇāṃ svatantraparatantrarūpatvāt |
evaṃ ca kṛtvā kartari parādhīnasyādhikaraṇasya sādhakatvātiśayaḥ
kārakāntarāpekṣo yujyate, bhinnaviṣayatvāditi kathamasvādhīnaṃ
prakarṣavat sādhanaṃ syāditi na codanīyam || 95 ||
yadi tarhi kartuḥ karaṇasya ca rūpaviprakarṣaḥ kathamidānīṃ karaṇasyāpi
kartṛtvamasiśavcchanatītyāśaṅkyāha |
asyādīnāṃ tu kartṛtve taikṣṇyādi karaṇaṃ viduḥ |
taikṣṇyādīnāṃ svatantratve dvaidhātmā vyavatiṣṭhate || 96 ||
(93) iha yadā cchidiṃ prati sutīkṣṇadhāratvāt
kartṛviniyogatiraskāreṇāsiḥ svātantryeṇa vivakṣyate tadāsau
karaṇameva na bhavati, api tu yadanantarabhāvinī kriyā tasyaiva
karaṇatvaṃ taikṣṇyagauravakāṭhinyasaṃsthānādeḥ | tasyāpi
kartṛtvavivakṣāyāṃ vivakṣāprāpitarūpabhedāyata karaṇabhāvaḥ | tathā hi
taikṣṇyaṃ cchinati svasāmarthyeneti
śabdabhedopahitarūpabhedādekamapi taikṣṇyaṃ bhedenaiva
kartṛkaraṇate pratipadyate | svādhīnatvenāśrita ātmā kartā
sādhakatamatvenāśritaḥ karaṇamiti dvedhā vyavasthānam | kriyāyāḥ
karaṇāvinābhāvāt karaṇāntaravyāvṛtipareyaṃ codanā | taikṣṇyameva
cchidau sādhakatamaṃ nānyaditi tasyātiśayaparipoṣe tātparyam,
tadevaṃ kartṛkaraṇe bhinne eva || 96 ||
yadyevamekatrāprāptau saṃjñayoḥ 
'karaṇaṃ parāṇi.......'(ja. od gh. 1.4.1)
iti vipratiṣedhaḥ kathamupanyasta ityāha |
ātmabhede'pi satyevameko'rthaḥ sa tathā sthitaḥ |
tadāśrayatvād bhede'pi kartṛtvaṃ bādhakaṃ tataḥ || 97 ||
(94) vivakṣāprāpite satyapi kartṛkaraṇatayā rūpabhede sa eko'rthaḥ
śatayādhārasthito na pracyavate rūpādityarthaḥ | atropapatiḥ
'tadāśrayatvāt' iti | kalpanāśrayatvāt | kālpaniko hi bhedo na
vāstavikamekatvaṃ vihantītyarthaḥ | yadvā tadāśrayatvaditi
tadekārthādhāratvāt kartṛkaraṇasaṃjñayoriti kecid vyācakṣate |
śaktibhede'pi tadādāradravyaikatvādekaḥ saṃjñayorvipratiṣedhaviṣaya
upadyate | saṃjñā hi śaktiviśiṣṭasya dravyasyaiva | tadevaṃ vastuni
vidyamānamekatvaṃ yadā vivakṣopādate tadaikaviṣayatve vipratiṣedhe
paratvāt kartṛsaṃjñā | karaṇasaṃjñāṃ bādhata ityudāhṛtam 'asiścchanati'
iti | atra hi sādhakatamatvāvivakṣāvinābhāvinī
svātantryavivakṣāserityekaviṣayatve ubhayasaṃjñāprasaṅge paratvāt
kartṛsaṃjñā | sādhakatamatve hi sati svātantryamāropyate | yathā
dhanuṣā vidhyatītyapāyabhāve satyeva sādhakatamatvaṃ dhanuṣaḥ | tadevaṃ
kriyāṃ prati prakṛṣṭopakārakaṃ karaṇamiti sthitam || 96 ||
upakāraśca bhavatā'rthena kriyate, na
tvavidyamānenetyetāmāśaṅkāmapyapākaroti | yathā ca sannidhānena
karaṇatvaṃ pratīyate |
tathaivāsannidhāne'pi kriyāsiddheḥ pratīyate || 98 ||
(95) dātrādayaḥ sannihitāḥ kriyopākāratiśayamāsādayantaḥ karaṇatayā
yathā prajñāyante tathaiva dhanābhāvena mukta #tyādāvisannihitamapi dhanaṃ
muktiṃ sādhayat karaṇameva prasiddham || 98 ||
atraiva sūtrakāramataṃ saṃvādakamāha |
satokasya vābhinirvṛteranivṛteśca tasya vā |
prasiddhiṃ karaṇatvasya stokādīnā pracakṣate || 99 ||
(96) 'karaṇe ca stokālpa....' (gh.  2.3.33)
ityatrodāharaṇaṃ stokānmuktaḥ stokena mukta iti | atra
satvavacanānāmapi stokādīnāṃ dharmamātrābhidānatātparyeṇa
prayogādasatvavacanatā | tacca dharmamātraṃ stokalakṣaṇaṃ sadasadvā
muktikriyāyāmatiśayenopakārakaṃ karaṇamityuktam | yasmāt stokaṃ
deyavastuno niṣpannaṃ na cānyat saṃbhavati niṣkiṃcanatvāt ato
muktaḥ ; yadi vā stokaṃ deyavastuno'vaśiṣṭamavasitaṃ tato muktaḥ |
yadi tu bahu deyaṃ syāt na mucyeteti stokenābhavatā muktaḥ |
evamekena na viṃśatirekānna viṃśatiriti ekābhāvo viṃśatiniṣedhasya
karaṇam || 99 ||
tadevaṃ vivakṣādhīnatvāt sādhanavyavahārasya yadyat sādhakatamatvena
vivakṣyate tatatkaraṇam | tathā ca 
dharmāṇāṃ tadvatā bhedādabhedācca viśiṣyate |
kriyāvadhepavacchedaviśeṣād bhidyate yathā || 100 ||
(97) āśritamātraṃ dharmaśabdenāha | 'tadvatā' ityāśrayeṇa | tathā hi
'devadataḥ kāṣṭhṬaḥ pacati' itīndhanagatasya tejasastadabhedavivakṣayā
prayogaḥ | tathā ca devadatakartṛkendhanakaraṇikā ceyaṃ pacikriyā |
yadā tu tejo bhedenandhanād vivakṣyate tadā "edhāḥ pakṣyanti
tejasā" iti tejaḥkaraṇikendhanakartṛkā ca pacikriyā |
yadāpyauṣṇyaṃ tejaso bhedena sādhanaviśeṣāt kriyā |
tadyatā'vadhergrāmāderavacchedaviśeṣād bhedaviśeṣāt kriyā bhidyate
viśiṣyate 'grāmādāgaccchati', 'grāmasya samīpādāgaccchati' ityādau
tadvadatrāpi boddhavyamityarthaḥ | sāraṇyake sasīmake sasthaṇḍilake hi
grāmaśabdābhideye'bhedena 'grāmādāhaccchati' iti prayogaḥ |
vāṭaparikṣipte tu grāmaśabdābhidheye'bhedena 'grāmādāgaccchati' iti
bhidyate'vadhiḥ | tadbhedācca kriyā viśiṣyate | evaṃ
vaivakṣikakaraṇabhedādapi yathāvivakṣaṃ ca tadeva pradhānakaraṇaṃ
tṛtīyāvaddhātupratyayena lyuṭā ghañā vābhidhīyate, pacanamedhaḥ, pacanaṃ
tejaḥ pacanī sthālī, pacanamauṣṇyam ityādi yathāvivakṣamudāhāryaṃ
prayogajātamiṣṭam || 100 ||
iti bhūtirājatanayahelārājakṛte prakīrṇaprakāśe sādhanasamuddeśe
karaṇādhikāraḥ ||
---------------
(98) tṛtīyārthe karaṇe vicārite samprati tadarthaḥ kartā vicāryate |
tasya lakṣaṇam 
'svatantraḥ kartā' (gh. 1.4.54)
iti | sva ātmā tantraṃ pradhānamasya | karaṇādīnāṃ hi paraḥ kartaiva tantraṃ
pradhānam | kriyāsiddhau tena prayojyatvāt | kartā tvananyaprayojyaḥ
svatantra ityucyate | tatra karaṇādīnāmapi svavyāpāre svātantryāt
ko'tiśayaḥ karturityāśaṅkyopapādayati |
prāganyataḥ śaktilābhānnyagbhāvāpādanādapi | 
tadadhīnapravṛtitvāt pravṛtānāṃ nivartanāt || 101 ||
adṛṣṭatvāt pratinidheḥ praviveke ca darśanāt | 
ārādapyupakāritve svātantryaṃ karturucyate || 102 ||
karaṇādipravṛteḥ prāk evārthitāderaparasmānnimitāt, na tu
sahakāribhya eva sāmarthyamāsādayati | phalakāmo hi kartā
karaṇādīnyupārjayate | tathā ca pūrvameva śaktimān svatantraḥ |
karaṇādīnāṃ tu kartṛviniyogādeva svavyāpāre svātantryam, na
tvanyataḥ | ata eva kartrā nyagbhāvamātmādhīnatāmāpādyante |
tadāyatavyāpārāśca | kartā tu phalārthamīhamānaḥ svayaṃ vyāpāravān
| atipravṛtāni ca karaṇādīni nivartyante kartrā | sa tu phalaprāptau
svayameva nivartate | coditāsaṃbhave karaṇādīnāṃ pratinidhiruktaḥ |
kartuḥ sa nāsti | kartrantaraṃ hi kriyāṃ nirvartayat pratinihitaṃ
nocyate | tasyāpyarthinaḥ samarthasyāparyudastasyādhikārāt |
praviveke ca kārakāntarāṇāmabhāve dṛśyate kartā, tadyathā asti,
bhavati, vidyata ityādau | yadyapyatrādhikaraṇādayaḥ saṃbhavanti tathāpi
nāntarīyakāste śabdavyāpārādapratīyamānāḥ | tataścākartṛkāyāḥ
kriyāyā abhāvāt saṃbhūyakāribhyo'pi viśiṣyate kartā | etena
hetukalāpena kartuḥ karaṇāpekṣayā kriyāsiddhau
viprakṛṣṭopakārakatve'pi svātantryaṃ prādhānyanibandhanamucyata iti
tasyaiva kartṛsaṃjñā, na tu karaṇādeḥ svavyāpāre svatantrasya#apītyarthaḥ
| etena cedaṃ bhāṣyaṃ vyākhyātam---
"yat sarveṣu sādhaneṣu sannihiteṣu kartā pravartayitā bhavati"
(g.eṇa.od gh. 1.4.23)
iti | etacca bhāṣyaṃ garbhīkṛtanāntarīyakārthaṃ saṃkṣipyoktamiti
garbhīkṛtārthavyaktīkāreṇātra vyākhyātam | tathā hi
'tadadhīnapravṛtitvāt' ityanena sākṣāt pravartakatvaṃ vyākhyātam |
sāmarthyākṣiptaṃ ca nyagbhāvanādi nirdiṣṭam | tathā hi
prayoktradhīnatvāt pravṛternyagbhāvaḥ karaṇādīnāṃ tatrārthoktaḥ | sā
pravartanā ca phalārthamiti tatprāptāvatipravṛtau nivartanāpyākṣiptā |
pravartayitā cāvaśyaṃ pravartyādanyataḥ śaktiṃ prāg labhata iti tadapi
vyākhyātam | asaṃbhave ca pravartayiturevābhāva iti pratinidhyabhāvaḥ
sāmarthyokto vivṛtaḥ | sākṣātsamarthasya kvacidanyānapekṣaṇāt
praviveke ca darśanaṃ sāmarthyalabhyaṃ vyākhyātamiti samasyābhihitaṃ
bhāṣyaṃ vyasya vyākhyātam || 101,102 ||
acetaneṣu tarhyevaṃvidhadharmakalāpabhāvāt kartṛtā na
prāpnotītyāśaṅkyāha |
dharmairabhyuditaiḥ śabde niyamo na tu vastuni |
karturdharmavivakṣāyāṃ śabdāt kartā pratīyate || 103 ||
(99) abhyuditaiḥ uktairetaiḥ prāganyataḥ śaktilābhādibhiḥ
pravartayitṛtvādilakṣaṇaiḥ dharmaiḥ svabhāvairūpalakṣitaḥ svatantraḥ kartā
ityevaṃlakṣaṇo niyamo'vaśyabhāvaḥ śabdaviṣaye samāśrayaṇīyaḥ | śabdena
yasyaite dharmāḥ pratyāyyante sa kartā boddhavyaḥ, na tu vastuviṣayatayā
dharmāṇāmeṣāṃ sambhavo'nveṣaṇīyaḥ yenācetanaviṣaye 'agnirdahati',
'edhāḥ pacanti' 'sthālī pacati' ityādau 'kūlaṃ patati, 'nadī vahati'
ityādau ca kartṛtvaṃ na syāt | ete hi dharmā yadā kartṛlakṣaṇāya
vivakṣyante śabdena ca yathāvivakṣaṃ pratyāyyante tadā śabdāt kartā
supratiṣṭhitalakṣaṇaḥ pratīyata iti karaṇamiva kartāpi vaivakṣikaḥ
pratipāditaḥ | evaṃ ca kṛtvācetaneṣūpacaritamapi na bhavati
kartṛtvam, sarvatrāskhaladvatitvātprayogasya mukhyatāsaṃbhavāt |
vyākaraṇe hi śabdārtho'rthaḥ na vastvarthaḥ | tataśca vyākaraṇāntare
arthī kartā
tathāyuktaśca 
iti lakṣaṇadvayaṃ cetanācetanaviṣayatayā yat praṇītaṃ tanna
kauśalamityuktaṃ bhavati || 103 ||
yadā ca vivakṣādīnaṃ svātantryaṃ tadedemapi siddhaṃ bhavatītyāha |
ekasya buddhyavasthābhirbhede ca parikalpite |
karmatvaṃ karaṇatvaṃ ca kartṛtvaṃ copajāyate || 104 ||
(100) 'hantyātmānamātmanā' ityekasyaivātmano
vivakṣāprāpitarūpabhedādhīnaḥ kārakatrayayogo dṛśyate | sa vāstave
kārakavyavahāre na ghaṭanāmeti | na hyaekasya vastuno yugapad
bhinnānekarūpatā śakyāvasātum | na cāmūrtasyātmanaḥ
śastrādivyāpārajanyavadhasaṃbhavaḥ | vivakṣā tu śabdanibandhanā na
vihanyate'tra | tathā hi buddheravasthā pratibhāsanānātvam,
tadanusāreṇa vivakṣayā bhedaparikalpaḥ |
śāstrādyaudyamananipatanavyāpāravān hantīti kartā | ātmaivātra
viṣayabhāvāpatitastathaiva karma | saukaryātiśayavivakṣāyāṃ
śastrādivyāpārasya nirāse karamatvamātmanaḥ | karmakaraṇe hyanyatra
bhedena dṛṣṭe iti tannirāsāya kartaivātra tathocyate bhedena | ata eva
vārtike karmavadbhāvo'nena nyāyena pratyākhyātaḥ |
siddhaṃ tu prākṛtakarmatvāt | (ja. od gh. 3.1.87)
ātmasaṃyoge'karmakartuḥ karmadarśanāt | (ja.odgh. 3.1.87)
iti | 
"hantyātmānam, ātmanā hanyata iti || 104 ||
anyadapi śābde kartṛtve siddhayatītyāha |
utpateḥ prāgasadbhāvo buddhayavasthānibandhanaḥ |
aviśiṣṭaḥ satā'nyena kartā bhavati janmanaḥ || 105 ||
(101) 'aṅkuro jāyate' iti janikartṛtvaṃ sato'sato vā | tatra 
yadi sajjāyate kasmādathāsajjāyate katham |
ityādinā | tatra vaivakṣike kartṛtve upacārārtho'pi na
kaścidityucyate | tathā hi vivakṣārūpāyā buddheḥ śabdanimitāyā
ye'vasthāviśeṣāḥ pratiniyataśabdanibandhanāḥ tadāśrayeṇātra
kriyāntarakartṛtvatulyaṃ janikartṛtvaṃ siddham |
vivakṣāprāpitasannidhāne hyarthe'ṅkura iti śabdaḥ prayujyate | sa ca
sadeva vastu darśayati | tato 'jāyate' iti
padasamavadhānāvadhāritajanmano'sya kutaḥ satvamiti buddhyā
niścitasatvasya kriyāsiddhāvupagṛhītasāmarthyasya bāhyaena rūpeṇa
janmeti vākyārtho'vatiṣṭhate | tataśca yena rūpeṇa satvaṃ na tena
kartṛtvaṃ na tena satvamiti neyaṃ vāstavī vyavasthā api tu
śābdayevetyarthaḥ | śabdārtha eva cārtha ityaskhaladvṛtitvāt
prayogasya mukhyataiva sarvatra | nopacārārthaḥ kaścit || 105 ||
vastuvṛtacaryāyāmapi cāṅkuro jāyata iti prayoga ityāha |
kāraṇaṃ kāryabhāvena yathā vā vyavatiṣṭhate |
kāryaśabdaṃ tadā labdhvā kāryatvenātha jāyate || 106 ||
(102) kāryamukhena kartṛtā janma ca sadasatpakṣabhedena nopapadyata iti
buddhyavasthāsamāśrayeṇa pūrvamupapāditam | idānīṃ kāraṇamukhena
pratipādyate | ata eva pakṣāntarasūcako vā śabdaḥ | tatra kāraṇāt
pṛthageva kāryamājāyate iti pūrvapakṣa uktaḥ | pariṇāmadarśane
tvabhedapakṣa idaṃ kathyate | kāryakāraṇayoḥ prakṛtivikārarūpatayā
'kṣīraṃ dadhi sampadyate,' bījamaṅkuro jāyata ityabhedena
vyavahāradekasyāvasthāturavasthāviśeṣasvīkaraṇamātraṃ satatvamiti
kāryonmukhatāyā viśiṣṭavikārābhinirvṛtiprakrame
viśiṣṭotarāvasthā viśeṣagatavācakādhyāsāt | kāraṇameva sadrūpaṃ
kartṛ bhavadutarāvasthāsādhanalakṣaṇaṃ janma pratipadyate iti yujyate
'jāyate'ṅkura' iti prayoga ityarthaḥ || 106 ||
yuktaṃ caitadyaduta kāraṇameva kāryabhāvenāvatiṣṭhata iti
svasiddhāntenaitadupapadayitumāha |
yathāheḥ kuṇḍalībhāvo vyagrāṇāṃ ca samagratā |
tathaiva janmarūpatvaṃ satāmeke pracakṣate || 107 ||
(103) pūrvaṃ sadasatpakṣabhedena kāryakāraṇabhāvāsabhavaṃ pratipādya
atyadbhutā tviyaṃ vṛtiḥ (ja.kṣkṣkṣ.sa.81)
ityādinā vivartanayena siddhāntitam | tatra ceyāneva janmārtho
vicāryamāṇo'vatiṣṭhate yaduta tathā tathā sanniveśamātram | tadyathā
sarpasya kauṭilyamanarthāntaram, pṛthagbhūtānāṃ cāṅgulyādīnāṃ
saṅghāto muṣṭaḷadirityavasthāviśeṣamātrasāraḥ kāryakāraṇabhāva
ityavasthāturabhedādavasthāsvanugamāt satve janikartṛtāsiddhiḥ || 107
||
idānīṃ kāryakāraṇayorbhedavāde'pi vāstavena rūpeṇa janikartṛtvaṃ
pratipādayitumāha |
vibhaktayoni yatkāryaṃ kāraṇebhyaḥ pravartate |
svā jātirvyaktirūpeṇa tasyāpi vyavatiṣṭhate || 108 ||
(104) vibhaktā vyatiriktā yoniḥ kāraṇamasya tasyāsato vikāryasya
jāyamānasya kāraṇebhyaḥ sakāśādvayariktasyotpateḥ pūrvaṃ
vyaktirūpeṇāsatve'pi jātyātmanā satvātajjātīyatayā nirūpya
sādhanaviniyogājjātireva kāraṇānāṃ svābhivyaṃjakasampatyarthaṃ
prayojiketi saiva vyaktyātmanā jāyata iti vyavahārājjātirūpeṇa
kartṛtvaṃ vyaktirūpeṇa janyatvamiti atyantaṃ
vyatirekābhāvājjātivyaktyoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyādupapadyata ityarthaḥ
|| 108 ||
idānīṃ śabdārtho'rtha iti svasiddhāntaṃ nigamayitumāha |
bhāvaṣveva padanyāsaḥ prajñāyā vāca eva vā |
nāstītyapyapade nāsti na cāsadbhidyate tataḥ || 109 ||
(105) ākārāvagraheṇa jñānaśabdayoḥ pravṛternirālaṃbanatāyogāt
pratīyamāne evākāro bhāvaśabdāvācyaḥ sadaiva viṣayaḥ
padavinyāsasthānaṃ tayoḥ | tathā hi nāstītyabhāvālaṃbanau kaÃcana
paraparikalpitaṃ svayamutprekṣitaṃ vākāramavalaṃbya jāyete yena
sadākārāvalabanābhyāṃ jñānaśabdabhyāmanayorviśeṣābhāvaḥ,
svākārāvagrahasāmyāditi vyavahāre sarvameva bhāvābhāvajātaṃ
samānam | bahirvastucarcā tu nāṅgam, iyataiva
vyavahāraparisamāpteriti sadākārāvagrahapravṛtau jāyate'ṅkura
iti prayogaḥ sūpapāda ityarthaḥ || 109 ||
buddhiśabdaviṣayabāvātirekeṇa vyavahāre vastūnāṃ bāhyā satā na
kācana vidyata ityāha |
buddhiśabdau pravartete yathā bhūteṣu vastuṣu |
teṣāmanyena tatvena vyavahāro na vidyate || 110 ||
(106) abhidhānapratyayāveva vyavahāra iti yathāpratyayaṃ yathābhidhānaṃ ca
padārthatatvameṣaṇīyam, nānyat
padārthasvarūpamapratīyamānamanabhidhīyamānaṃ cāśritya
pratyavastheyamityarthaḥ | tathā cārthollekharahitāyā
buddherayogātatsiddhena tatvena jāyamānaḥ kartā nirūpyate ;
nirūpitaścābhidhīyate | yathā nāstīti pratyaye bahirasannapi padārtho
niyatadeśakālayorniṣidhyamāno
deśakālāntaropalabdhenākāreṇāvabhāsamānaḥ
kartṛpratyayenābhidhīyate, tathā kāryonmukhatāyāmanāgatamapi vastu
tajjātīyānubhavavāsanayā buddhāvabhimukhībhavatsamarthahetuvaśājjāyata
iti janmanaḥ kartṛtvenābhidhīyata iti prakaraṇārthaḥ || 110 ||
idānīṃ saṃbandhasamuddeśoktaṃ nyāyaṃ cetasi sanniveśyādvaitanayena
janmanāśāsaṃbhavaṃ pratipādayati |
ākāśasya yathā bhedaśchāyāyāścalanaṃ yathā |
janmanāśāvabhede'pi tathā kaiścitprakalpitau || 111 ||
(107) niṣpradeśasyāpyākāśasya nityasya saṃyogipadārthapravibhāgena
deśādhyāropād bhedaparikalpanetyuktam | bhāsāmabhāvarūpāyāḥ
chāyāyāśca niṣkriyatve'pi vārakadravyakriyānugāmitayā
sakriyatvādhyāparopaḥ | evamabhāvasya pūrvaṃ nirākṛtatvāt janmani
kāraṇavyāpārasya ca niṣedhād vināśe'pi
tadanupapaterāveditatvāt
nirhetukalavināśapratiṣedhāccābinnasyādvayasya tatvasya nityasya
janmanāśāyogādavidyāvaśāt pratibhāsamātraniṣṭhajanmanāśavaśena
parikalpitabhāvābhāvārūpatayā vyavahāra iti saṃbandhasamuddeśa eva
vistarato nirṇītam | evaṃ ca bhāvābhāvavyavahāravajjāyata
ityartho'vicāritaramaṇīyaḥ siddha ityarthaḥ || 111 ||
nāśasya kalpitatvamupapādayitumāha |
yathaivākāṣanāstitvamasannūrtinirūpitam |
tathaiva mūrtināstitvamasadākāśaniśrayam || 112 ||
(108) vyāpakasyākāśasya sarvatra bhavato vastuno na kvacidabhāvaḥ |
tataścāsadapyākāśanāstitvaṃ parimitadeśaghaṭādimūrtyavaṣṭhṃabhavaśena
kalpyate | yatra hi ghaṭādayastatrāsadākāśamiti vyavahāraḥ | evaṃ
mūrtīnāmapi ghaṭādīnāṃ kvacin nāstitvaṃ bhāvato'vidyamānamari
satsvabhāvasya svarūpavināśāyogādasatsvabhāvatve
kadācidapyabhāvāditi nyāyādākāsasyāntarāle darśanādadhyavasīyate
| antarāle hi mūrtīnāṃ satāyāstirodhāne gaganameva
tadrahitamavadhārayanto janma pratipadyanta ityetat prakaraṇādavagantavyam
| tathā ca dvayorapi janmanāśayoḥ kalpitatvamupapāditaṃ bhavati |
yathādhyavasāyaṃ ca vyavahārādajanmanirodhā eva padārthāḥ siddhāḥ | na
hyākāśamabhāvo vastutaḥ padārthānāmabhāvābhāve ca
sadaivāvasthānājjānmārtho'pi na kaścit || 112 ||
idānīṃ vāstave'pi janmani prakārāntareṇa jāyata iti prayogaṃ
samarthayitumāha |
yathā tadarthairvyāpāraiḥ kriyātmā vyapadiśyate |
abhedagrahaṇādeṣa kāryakāraṇayoḥ kramaḥ || 113 || 
(109) 'karaṇaṃ kāryabhāvena'
ityādinā kāryakāraṇayorvāstavamabhedamāśritya samarthitam | adhunā
tūpacaritābhādāśrayeṇocyate yathā hi pacerviklitiḥ pradhānārtha iti
tadarthaiḥ guṇavyāpārairaghiśrayaṇodakāsecanādibhiḥ
kartrādigataistādarthyādamadhyāropya pradhānakriyārūpamucyate,
adhiśrayaṇodakāsecanataṇḍulāvapanaidhopakarṣaṇakriyāḥ pradhānasya kartuḥ
pākaḥ, kāṣṭhāni pacanti ā vikliterjvalantīti karaṇasya pāka
ityādinā tathā kāryārthatvāt kāraṇasyaivaiṣānupūrvī kramo'bhedena

nanu ca kāryakāraṇayorabhedādhyārope'pi janmanaḥ kartṛtvaṃ
kāryasyaiva yujyate na tu kāraṇasya siddhatvādityāśaṅkya

(110) iha prakṛtivikāraviṣaye sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ dṛśyate kṣīraṃ dadhi

vikāro'ṅgam, uta viparyayaḥ | tatra pradhānasya kartṛtvaṃ janiṃ prati |

(114) iha na sarvathaivāniṣpanni jāyata ityabhidhīyate, api tu pūrvāṃ

(116) 'atvaṃ tvaṃ sampadyate, tvadbhavasi' ityevaṃ yuṣmadarthāśraye'tra

gatamānuṣaṅgikam | idamidānīṃ vaktavyam | 'prāganyataḥ

iti | hetuḥ prayojakaḥ, tadvati prayojya ityarthaḥ |
hetusaṃjñāyastvadhyeṣaṇaṃ syādviṣayo yatra niyujyamānasyāpi
tatprayojakatvaṃ na virudhyata iti 'hetumati' ityupalakṣaṇaṃ yuktam | 
atra parihāravārtikam
'na vā svātantryāt'(ja.od gh. 1.4.54.)
iti | svavyāpāre'styeva prayojyasya svātantryamiti siddhā kartṛsaṃjñā
| nanvevaṃ karaṇādīnāmapi syāt | naitadasti | teṣāṃ yathāsvaṃ
karaṇādisaṃjñāvidhānāt | prayojyasya tu na kadācidanyā saṃjñokteti
kartṛsaṃjñaiva | evaṃ sthite prayojakāyatatvāt prayojyapravṛteḥ kataṃ
svātactryamityāśaṅkyopapādayati |
sambhāvanāt kriyāsiddhau kartṛtvena samāśritaḥ |
kriyāyāmātmasādhyāyāṃ sādhanānāṃ prayojakaḥ || 122 ||
prayogamātre nyagbhāvaṃ svātantryādeva niśritaḥ |
aviśiṣṭo bhavatyanyaiḥ svatantrairmuktasaṃśayaiḥ || 123 ||
(118) yastāvadapravṛtakriyaḥ prayojyaḥ so'numitakriyāsāmarthyā eva
prayujyate | anyathā prayoga eva na syāt | na hyaśaktaḥ kasyāṃcit
kriyāyāṃ kenacid viniyujyate sacetasā | tataścāvadhṛtaśaktiḥ,
saṃbhāvitakriyaḥ kartaiva svatantro viniyujyate | yo'pi
ātmasādhyāyāṃ kriyāyāṃ pravṛtaḥ savyāpāraḥ karaṇādikārakakalāpaṃ
prayojayanneva pralṛtakriyaḥ san prayujyate virāmāśaṅkāyāṃ mā
viraṃsīditi buddhyā so'pi svatantraḥ kartaiva prayojyaḥ svavyāpāre
svātantryādeva ca prayogamātre prayojakapraiṣe nyagbhāvam āyatatāṃ
niśritaḥ prāptaḥ | kriyāsiddhaye hi praiṣaḥ | evaṃ ca kriyā sidhyati yadi

"itarathā hyakurvatyapi kārayatīti syāt | (ja.od gh. 1.4.54)
nā'kurvatīti cet svatantraḥ | (ja. od gh. 1.4.54)

kartṛmātrasya tasya 'kartṛmati ṇic' ityevāstu | tathā ca |

'bhiyohetubhaye ṣuk' (gh. 7.3.40.)
'bibheterhetubhaye'(gh. 6.1.56)
ityatra 'kartṛbhaye' iti vacane'niṣṭaṃ syāt | tasmāt svatantrasya
prayojaka iti karaṇādiprayojakasya hetusaṃjñāvyudāse prayatanīyam |
atrocyate | tasya prayojaka iti sarvanāmnānubhavakartṛsaṃjñasya
svatantrasya parāmarśādipratilabdhakartṛsaṃjñānāṃ karaṇādīnāṃ
prayojakasya hetusaṃjñā na bhavati | tathā hi svavyāpāre
svatantratve'pi karaṇādeḥ pradhānavyāpāre pāratantryāt kartṛsaṃjñā na
bhavati | svatantraśrutisāmarthyāddhi prakṛṣṭaṃ sāmarthyaṃ vivakṣitam |
karaṇādīnāṃ tvanukurvāṇānāṃ kārakatvāyogāt
tadanyathānupapatyānumīyamānaṃ svavyāpāre svātantryaṃ yadi paraṃ
kārakasaṃjñāyāmupayujyeta | na tu kartṛsaṃjñāyām;
pradhānaviniyogenābhibhūtatvāt | prakṛṣṭasya svātantryasyābhāvāt |
tataśca na te kartari santaḥ preṣyante | prayojyastu prayokto'pi
sādhanāntaraviniyoktṛtvalakṣaṇaṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ svādhīnatvaṃ naiva tyajatīti
sarvathā'sau kartaiva yujyate | ata evoktaṃ "kartṛtvena samāśritaḥ" iti
| bhūtena bhāvinā vā saṃbhāvyamānena buddhyābhisaṃhitena
kartṛtveneti siddhamiṣṭam || 122,123 ||
uktamidaṃ "prayogamātre nyagbhāvaṃ svātantryādena niśritaḥ" iti | tadapi
vā nāstītyāha |
nimitebhyaḥ pravartante sarva eva svabhūtaye |
abhiprāyānurodho'pi svārthasyaiva prasiddhaye || 124 ||
(119) svārthalipsayā sarvaḥ pravartamānaḥ parādhīnatāṃ pravṛtau
nāsādayatīṃti kevalaṃ kasyacit pravṛtiḥ
kasyacidarthasiddhaye'pyaṅgamityetāvatā bhṛtyādeḥ
svāmyādyāśayānuvartanena prayojyatā kathyate | nirveśanimitā hi
bhṛtyānāṃ pravṛtiḥ svārthasādhanāyā bītarāgāṇāmapi kāruṇyāt
pravṛtau parārthasampādanameva svārthaḥ phalam, iṣṭalakṣaṇatvātasya |
tadetad bhāṣye'bhihitam---
"sarva ime svabhūtyarthaṃ yatante | neha kaścit
paro'nugṛhītavya iti|" (g.eṇa. od gh. 3.1.26.)
iti | svabhūtyarthaṃ pravartamāneṣu kaḥ prayojyārtha iti codye
samādhānamapyuktam---
"yadabhiprāyeṣu sajjante" (g.eṇa.od gh. 3.1.26)
iti | prayojakābhiprāyaḥ kriyāsampādanam | tacca
svārthaphalābhisaṃdhānena pravartamāno'pi prayojyaḥ sādhayatyeva | evaṃ
ca kṛtvā 
'hetumati ca'(gh. 3.1.26)
iti | prayojakābhiprāyaḥ kriyāsampādanam | tacca
svārthaphalābhisaṃdhānena pravartamāno'pi prayojyaḥ sādhayatyeva | evaṃ
ca kṛtvā 
'hetumati ca ' (gh. 3.1.26)
ityatropasaṃkhyānāni pratyākhyātāni |
'na vā sāmānyakṛtatvāddhetuto hyaviśiṣṭam'(ja.od gh. 3.1.26)
iti | svarasapravṛtitvāt sūryasya māhiṣmatyāmudgamanaṃ
pathikasyābhipretaṃ kākatālīyāt sampannamityabhiprāyānuvartanāt
sūryaḥ prayojyo bhavatītyādi yojyam | tadāha vārtikakāraḥ---
'pravṛtirhyubhayatrānapekṣya'(ja. od gh. 3.1.26)
iti | muktasaṃśaye'pi prayojye yā pravṛtiḥ sāpi na prayojyasya
svārthamapekṣya, api tvātmana evetyarthaḥ | 'kaṃsavadhamācaṣṭe' ityādau
tu 
'pratyakṣamiva kaṃsādīn'
ityādinā pūrvameva samarthitam | acetanaviṣaye'pi tarhi bhikṣā
vāsayati' ityādau vāsādyānukūlyādabhiprāyādhyāropāt
prayojakatvaṃ samarthanīyam | evaṃ ca yogyatāmātreṇa nimitamātraṃ
hetuḥ prayojaka ityuktaṃ nimitebhyaḥ iti | vārtike'pyuktam---
'hetunirdeaśaśca nimitamātram bhikṣādiṣu darśanāt'
(ja. od gh. 3.1.26)
ityavasitavicāraḥ kartā || 124 ||
iti bhūtirājatatanayahelārājakṛte prakīrṇaprakāśe kartradhikāraḥ |

kartaiva prayojakaḥ san heturiti kartṛvicārānantaraṃ hetuṃ
vicārayati |
preṣaṇādhyeṣaṇe kurvastatsamarthāni cācaran |
kartaéva vihitāṃ śāstre hetusaṃjñāṃ prapadyate || 125 ||
(120) ājñāpuraḥsarā vyāpāraṇā preṣaṇam, nikṛṣṭaviṣayo niyoga
ityarthaḥ | yatpunarabhyarhitaṃ vyāpārayati tadadhyeṣaṇam |
abhyarhitaviṣayaṃ prabodhanamityarthaḥ | tatsamarthāni
prayojyakriyāsampatyanukūlāni ceṣṭitāni | ayameva ca mukhyaḥ
prayojakavyāpāro yat prayojyakriyāsampatisamarthācaraṇam |
vyāpakatvād bhikṣādiṣvapi bhāvāt | asyaiva
nikṛṣṭotkṛṣṭaviṣayatayā preṣaṇādhyeṇarūpeṇa pravibhāgaḥ | tathā ca
gobalīvardanyāyena tadvilakṣaṇaṃ tatsamarthācaraṇaṃ
bhikṣāviṣayamatrābhipretam | vyavahārabhedādbhedenaiṣamupādānam |
yathābhiprāyānuvidhānaṃ prayojyatvamuktaṃ tathā svābhiprāyaprakāśanamapi
prayojakatvamiti tatsamarthācaraṇameva sarvatra prayojakavyāparaḥ |
'kartaiva' iti |
'tatprayojako hetuśca' (gh. 1.4.55.)
iti saṃjñāsamāveśārthaṃ cakāraṃ vyācaṣṭe | asati
cakāre'traikasaṃjñādhikārāt kartṛsaṃjñā na syāditi
samāveśārtho'sau | taduktaṃ vārtike |
'gatidivihetumatmu ca grahaṇam'
iti | vihitāṃ śāstre' iti | pradeśe'syaiva grahaṇaṃ yathā syādityarthaḥ
| phalasādhanayogyaḥ padārtho hi laukiko heturdravyādiviṣayaḥ |
tasya tvayaṃ viśeṣaḥ | kriyāviṣayatvena kārakaṃ hetuḥ
prathojyaprayojakaḥ | ata e kārakatvanibandhanaḥ preṣaṇādiḥ
svavyāpāro'tra nirdiṣṭaḥ | tatra 
'hetumati ca'(gh. 3.1.26)

'akartari...' (gh. 2.3.24)

'lakṣaṇahetvoḥ kriyāyāḥ'(gh. 3.2.126)
ityatrāpi prayojyasya kārakatvāt kriyāyogāvyabhicārād
viśeṣaṇasāmarthyād dravyaguṇakriyāviṣayatvena

na hi dhātuḥ sādhanarūpe hetau vartate | evaṃ 
hetuhetumatorliṅ (gh. 3.3.156)

dhātvarthatvābhāvāt laukika eva hetuḥ | 
'kuño hetutācchīlyānulomyeṣu'(gh. 3.2.20)

tijrahitasya nirdeaśāt | tenātra kṛtvāt prāptasya kartuḥ laukiko
heturviśeṣaṇam | tena ca virodhābhāvānna nivartyate kartā |
yogyatārūpeṇaiva ca kṛdbhiḥ karturabhidhānānnimitamātraṃ
heturupadīyamāno viśeṣaṇaṃ punaḥ śruteravadhāraṇaṃ prakalpayati
'hetureva' iti | tenāvyabhicāri kāraṇaṃ vidyādi yaśaḥ prabhṛtiṣu
pratipāditaṃ bhavati | ete cātra hetvādayaḥ
kartṛviśeṣapratītisāmarthyād gamyamānāḥ paramparayā padārthā eva |
anyathā vākyāvasthāyāṃ pratīyamānāḥ padāvadhikasyānvākhyānasya
nimitabhāvaṃ nopeyuḥ | iha 
praiṣātisarga.....(gh. 3.3.163)
ityādisūtre cakāreṇa samuccito loṭ iti praiṣe
ṇijloṭorvidhānādvikalpaprasaṅga iti bhāṣye coditam |
pṛccchatu mā bhavān, anuyāṅktāṃ mā bhavānityatra ṇic kasmānna
bhavati |
(g.eṇa.od gh. 3.1.26)
iti | 'mā bhavān' ityātmanirdeaśena prayojakāśritaṃ praiṣaṃ pradarśayan
ṇicaḥ prāptiṃ paripuṣṇāti | atra parihāro bhāṣye'bhihitaḥ |
'akartṛtvāt' (g.eṇa.od gh. 3.1.26)
iti | svatantrasya kartuḥ prayojako hi tadvyāpāre ṇij
vidhīyamāno'tra pṛccchikriyāyāmapravṛtasya tasya
kartṛtvābhāvātatprayojakasya hetusaṃjñābhāvātadvyāpāre praiṣe
ṇij na bhavatītyarthaḥ | codakastu kālādeḥ
praśnādyaśaktasyāprayojyatvāt pravṛtatya ca niyogānarthakyāt
saṃbhāvitakriyatvena kartuṃ preṣaṇamidamityāha---
"kiṃ ca bhoḥ vartamānāyā eva kriyāyāḥ kartrā bhavitavyam,
na bhūtabhaviṣyatkālāyāḥ" (g.eṇa.od gh. 3.1.26)
iti | atrotaram |
'abhisaṃbandhastatra kriyate |' (g.eṇa od gh. 3.1.26)
iti| sāmarthyasambhāvito bhūtabhaviṣyatkriyāviṣaye kriyākārakabhāvaḥ |
tatra ca loḍviṣaye prayojyakriyaivaikā sambhāvyata iti 'kriyāyāṃ
kartā bhava' iti | na tvaparaḥ prayojyavyāpāraviṣayaḥ
prayojakavyāpāro'sti | anantaraṃ bhāṣyam |
'yacca kartā kathaṃ kartṛpratyayenocyate' (g.eṇa. od gh. 3.1.26)
iti | atrotaram |
kathamasminnapṛccchatyayaṃ pṛccchirvartate |'(g.eṇa.od gh. 3.1.26)
iti | tat uktam |
abhisaṃbandhastatra kriyate, imāṃ kriyāṃ kurvīti |'
(g.eṇa. od gh. 3.1.26)
iti | anantaramuktam |
kartrāpi tarhyabhisaṃbandhaḥ kriyate | asyāṃ krithāyāṃ kartā bhaveti
(j.eṇa.od gh. 3.1.26)
iti | tathā ca yathātra saṃpratyapṛccchatyapi pṛccchikriyābhisandhānena
pṛccchirdhāturvartate tathā tatkartrāpyabhisaṃbandhāt kartṛpratyayo
loṭ kriyata iti kartrāpi tarhyabhisaṃbandhaḥ kriyata ityuktam | evaṃ ca
vadatā bhāṣyakāreṇa sampratyakartṛtvādatra ṇij na bhavatītyuktaṃ bhavati
| tathā hi navamevāsyādhīyamānaṃ kartṛtvaṃ na tu vidyamānam |
kriyāyāṃ kartā bhaveti hi preṣyate, na tu kartaiva san | tena kartuḥ
sataḥ praiṣaḥ prayojakasamavāyī ṇijviṣayaḥ, loṭpratyayastu kartrādiṣu
vidhīyate | dhātvarthopādhistu tasya praiṣo dyotyaḥ | sa ca na
dhātupratyayavācyaḥ, api tu sāmarthyād vaktaiva prayojako'vatiṣṭhata
iti taddharmaḥ praiṣo'vatiṣṭhamāno na dhātupratyayena
niyatakālatayātrāvaccchidyate | tathā ca sampratyapṛccchatyapi pṛccchatviti
prayogaḥ | ṇicastu praiṣo vācya iti śabdopādānatvādabhidheyadharmo
bhavan niyatakālābhidhāyinā pratyayena laḍādinā
kālaniyamenāvaccchidyate | śabdena hi ṇyantādutpannena kartṛvācinā
lakāreṇa tṛjādinā copāto yaḥ kartā prayojakastasya saṃbandhī
pratīyate'sāvityabhidheyadharmaḥ | dvividho hyarthaḥ
śāstre'nvākhyānāṅgabhāvenopādīyate | kaścit prayoktṛdharmo
yathā vīpsābādhāsūyākutsanādiḥ | anyaḥ pratipādyadharmaḥ | tatrāyaṃ
loḍviṣayaḥ praiṣa ābādhādivat prayoktṛdharma ityabhidheyatvād
vyāvṛtaḥ || 125 ||
tadetat tātparyeṇa pratipādayati |
dravyamātrasya tu praiṣe pṛccchayāderloḍ vidhīyate | 
sakriyasya prayogastu yadā sa viṣayo ṇicaḥ || 126 ||
(121) apravṛtakriyasya dravyamātrasya apratilabdhakartṛbhāvasya kartṛtvārtha
eva praiṣe dyotye loḍupidaśyate | pṛccchayanuyujyāderdhātoḥ paraḥ
pratyayaḥ kartrādikārake vācye | pravṛtakriyasya tu virāmāśaṅkāyāṃ
mā viraṃsīdityabhisaṃdhāya kartureva svatantrasya
prayojakahetuvyāpāre ṇij vācaka upadiśyate |
pratyayārthaviśeṣaṇapakṣo hi 'hetumati ca' ityatra siddhāntitaḥ | tathā
ca vārtikam |
'hetumatīti kārakopādānaṃ pratyayaparigrahārtham |'
(ja od gh. 3.1.26)
iti | tadevaṃ ṇijloṭorvibhakta eva viṣayaḥ
saukikāccchabdaprayogāt praiṣabhedasyātrāvadhāraṇāt | ṇijviṣayasya
praiṣasyābhidheyadharmatvena ṇijvācyatayā ca
taddharmabhedālloḍviṣayasya tu dyotyatvena prayoktṛdharmatvena ca
bhedāt | ata eva tuśabdadvayena sambandhanīyaḥ | pṛccchatu mā
bhavānityādau na praiṣakarturvācakaḥ kaścid vidyate |
kevalamuccārayitaiva kartā sāmarthyāt pratīyate ityabhidheyadharmo'tra
praiṣo na bhavati | nāpi svarūpeṇābhidheyaḥ loṭā tadanabhidhānāt
loḍantaśabdoccāraṇāddhi prayoktṛdharmatvenābādhādivat pratīyate
praiṣo na tvasya vācakaḥ kaścidasti | yadi tarhi evamanayoḥ praiṣayoḥ
sphuṭo bhedaḥ kathaṃ bhāṣye coditam---
'iha kasmānna ṇij bhavati pṛccchatu mā bhavān'
(g.eṇa. od gh. 3.1.26)
iti | lokavyavahārādhigamyo'yaṃ vibhāgaḥ, śāstre'nvākhyānavibhāgo
na kṛta iti praiṣamātraṃ samānaṃ matvā pūrvapakṣaḥ || 126 ||
gatametat | iha prayojyasya praiṣaṇādhyeṣaṇādinā sarvasya
prayojakavyāpāreṇāpyamānatvāt karmasaṃjñāprasaṅga ityāśaṅkyāha | 
guṇakriyāyāṃ svātantryāt preṣaṇe karmatāṃ gataḥ |
niyamāt karmasaṃjñāyāḥ svadharmeṇābhidhīyate || 127 ||
(122) svatantratāmaparityajanneva prayojyaḥ prayojakavyāpāre
pārādhīnyaṃ pratipadyate | na hyasya prayojakena svātantryakhaṇḍanā kriyata
ityuktam | itarathā hyakurvatyapi kārayatīti syāditi | tathā ca
praiṣe'pi svavyāpāre svātantryāt kartṛsaṃjñā bhavatyeva | karmasaṃjñā
tu praiṣaṇāpyamānatvena prāptā 
'gatibuddhi...' (gh. 1.4.52)
ityādinā niyamitā | gatyādidhātuviṣaya eva prayojyaḥ karmasaṃjño
nānyatreti pācayatyodanaṃ devadatenetyādau svadharmeṇa kartṛtvena
prayojyaḥ abhidhīyate ityarthaḥ | nanu praṣe'pi svātantryasyānivṛtau
kartṛsaṃjñaiva paratvāt syāditi kathaṃ gatibuddhītyādiniyamaḥ kathyate
| kartṛsaṃjñāprasaṅge hi karmasaṃjñā ārabhyata iti vidhyarthatā syāt |
atrocyate - iha guṇapradhānabhūtakriyādvayaviṣayaśaktidvayaviṣaye
pradhānaśaktireva svakāryaṃ prayuṅkte na guṇaśaktiriti prāgeva
pratipāditam | tatra dhātuvācyaḥ prayojyavyāpāro
ṇyarthaviśeṣaṇatvādapradhānaṃ prayojakavyāpārastu ṇijvācyaḥ
pradhānam | prakṛtyarthāvaccchinnatvāddhi pratyayārthaḥ sarvatraiva pradhānam ,
prakṛtipratyayo pratyayārthaṃ saha brūta iti | ataścātra
prayojakavyāpārasya prādhānyāt tadapekṣayā karmaśaktireva svakāryaṃ
prāpayati, na tu prayojyavyāpāraviṣayā kartṛśaktiḥ | tadyathāodanasya
viklitau svātantrye'pi na kartṛsaṃjñā, api tu vikledanena
devadatvāyāpāreṇepsitatamatvāt karmasaṃjñaiva pācayatyodanamiti |
tadevamatrobhayaprasaṅgābhāve vipratiṣedhābhāvāt
karta-saṃjñāpratptyā siddhaiva gatyāda#iviṣaye'pi karmasaṃjñeti
niyamārthaḥ āraṃbhaḥ | pacatyādiviṣaye niyamena pradhānakārye
prasārite niṣpratipakṣaṃ guṇakriyā svātantryaṃ svakāryaṃ kartṛsaṃjñāṃ
pravartayatyeveti siddham || 127 ||
prayojako heturityuktam | karmaṇaścāpi phalabhūtasya kriyāṃ
pratyastyeva prayojakatvamiti tasyāpi hetutvaprasaṅga ityāśaṅkya
hetukarmaṇorbhedaṃ darśayati |
kriyāyāḥ prerakaṃ karma hetuḥ kartuḥ prayojakaḥ |
karmārthā ca kriyotpatiḥ saṃskārapratipatibhiḥ || 128 ||
(123) nirvṛtivikārapratipatibhiryathākramaṃ
nirvartyāditrividhakarmasampādakatvāt kriyāphalaṃ karmātmasampatyarthaṃ
kriyāṃ prayuṅkte | hetuḥ punaḥ kriyārthī sādhanaviniyoge kartāraṃ
prayuṅkte, na tu sākṣāt kriyām | tatparipoṣāya hi kartuḥ
pravṛtiḥ | tasya kartuḥ prayojaka iti vacanāt , iti
bhinnaviṣayatvāddhetukarmaṇoḥ prayojakatvasya svarūpabhedaḥ sphuṭa ityarthaḥ
| 'ca' śabdo hetau | yasmādutpatyādibhirupakārāt kriyā karmārthā
tataḥ karma kriyāyāḥ prayojakam | yaddhi yadarthaṃ tatasya prayojakamiti
vyapadiśyate | taduddeśena tatpravṛteḥ | kārakāntarāṇi tu
kriyārthāni na tu tadarthā kriyeti na tāni tasthāḥ prayojakāni | na
hi karaṇādyarthaṃ kriyāpravṛtiḥ | upāyamātraṃ hi tat |
tadayamavasitavicāro hetuḥ || 128 ||
iti bhūtirājatanayahelārājakṛte prakīrṇaprakāśe sādhanasamuddeśe
hetvadhikāraḥ ||
