vibhaktividhānakrameṇa sādhanānāṃ vicāre tṛtīyārthe kartari nirṇīte
caturthyarthaṃ sampradānaṃ vicārayitumāha |
anirākaraṇāt kartustyāgāṅgaṃ karmaṇopsitam |
preraṇānumatibhyāṃ vā labhate sampradānatām || 129 ||
(124) anvarthatvāt sampradānaśabdasya 'tyāgāṅgam' iti lakṣaṇalābhaḥ |
tyāgo dīyamānasya svatvanivṛtyā parasvatvāpādanam | tatra cāṅgaṃ
nimitaṃ pāṇyādyapi bhavatītyāha - karmaṇepsitam iti | yat
tyajyamānaṃ karma tena karaṇabhūtena prāraṃbha eva kartrāptumiṣṭamityarthaḥ
| tathā ca 
'karmaṇā yamabhipraiti' (gh. 1.4.32)
iti lakṣaṇaparigrahaḥ | tadevavidhaṃ tyāgāṅgaṃ kathaṃ kārakaviśeṣarūpāṃ
sampradānatāṃ labhata ityāśaṅkya kārakatvanibandhanaṃ vyāpāraṃ
kriyāsiddhau nirdiśati anirākaraṇād ityādinā | 'upādhyāyāya gāṃ
dadāti' ityādau tyajyamānaṃ karma gavādyaupādhyāyādinā saṃbadhyate,
tadabhiprāyeṇaiva tyāgāt | sa copādhyāyastadamisaṃbadhyamānaṃ na
nirākaroti, nāpi tattyaktuḥ 'mā tyākṣīḥ' iti nirākaraṇaṃ
vidhate | yadi hi nirākuryāt tyāga eva na sampadyate |
parasvatvāpatiparyantatvātasya | anumanyate ca dīyamānam | tathā
dīyamānaṃ na nirākaroti balyādi devatādīti tadapi kriyāṅgam
| yāñcāpūrvake ca dāne preraṇam abhyarthanayā dāne pravartanamapi
sampradānavyāpāraḥ | tathā ca tyāgopakāritvāt tyāgāṅgaṃ
bhavatyeva kārakaṃ sampradānam | nanu ca dānasya tadarthatvāt
tādarthye caturthīprayogāt kimarthaṃ sampradānasaṃjñā | naitannyāyyam |
dānakriyārthaṃ hi sampradānam, na tu dānakriyā tadarthā, kārakāṇāṃ
kriyārthatvāt sampradānārthaṃ tu dīyamānaṃ karmeti vākyārthabhūtāyā
dānakriyāyā atādarthyāt tādarthye caturthyā aprāptau tadarthā
samprādanasaṃjñā nyāyyā | 'rajakasya vastraṃ dadāti ,'ṭhghnataḥ pṛṣṭhṃa
dadāti' ityādau tu dadātyartho nāsti | tyāgo hi saḥ | tena ca
hānātmakena mamatāvicchedasyākṣepādeva paropayogābhisaṃdhinā
parasvatvāpādanamapi svīkṛtam | atra ca tadabhāva iti gauṇo'yaṃ
dadāteḥ prayogaḥ | devatāviṣaye tu dāne
dātustatsvāmikatvasaṅkalpo vidyate eva | evam 'arocakine'nnaṃ
dadāti' ityādau upayogābhāvastu na śābdaḥ | na śūdrāya yatiṃ
dadyādityatrāpi matisaṃtānasyāparakrāmato'styeva paropayogaḥ | tathā
ca 'upādhyāyādāgamayati' ityadhyayanasaṃtānasyāpakrāmato
bhavatthapādānamupādhyāyaḥ matisaṃtānasthokadeśastyajyamāna iti
svatvanivūtyaṅgaparasvatvāpādanalakṣaṇo mukhya evātra dadātyarthaḥ |
anye tu mateḥ dātṛsaṃbandhānavagamādnauṇa ityāhuḥ | "kānyāṃ
dadāti" iti janyajanakabhāvāvyāvṛtāvapi svasvābhisaṃbandhasya
nivṛtermukhya eva dadāttharthaḥ "khaṇḍikopādhyāyastamai capeṭāṃ
dadāti" ityādau vastuto'satyapi capeṭādisvāmye tadupakāritayā
dātuḥ svāmitvābhisaṃdhirastyeva | yadyapi
pratikūlarūpatvāccapeṭāyāstadānīmupayogo nāsti tathāpi
phalādvareṇāstyeva paropayogitvam | capeṭāsahatve
śāstrābhyāsayogyatvāt phalāvāpteḥ | 'mṛjirasmāya
viśeṣeṇopadiṣṭaḥ' (g.eṇa.od ja od gh. 1.1.1.) iti tādarthye caturthī |
aviśeṣeṇa gaṇe paṭhito mṛjirmārjakaśabdārtho'pi bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
129 ||
tadevaṃ prathamaṃ sampradānaṃ vyākhyātam | idānīmutaraṃ vyākhyātumāha |
hetutve karmasaṃjñāyāṃ śeṣatve vāpi kārakam |
rucyārthādiṣu śāstreṇa sampradānākhyamucyate || 130 ||
(125) yathā saṃjñāntarapūrvakaṃ karma prāguktaṃ tathedamapi
saṃmpradānamanyapūrvakaṃ śāstreṇa 
'rucyarthānāṃ prīyamāṇaḥ' (gh. 1.4.33)
ityādinā vyākhyāyate | tathā hi 'devadatāya rocate modakaḥ'
ityatrānyakartṛko'bhilāṣo rucyartha ityabhilāṣaviṣayabhāvamāpadyamānaṃ
modakaṃ devadataḥ prayuṅkte laulyātadānuguṇyamācaratīti
hetusaṃjñāyāṃ prayojakasya devadatasya prāptāyāṃ sampradānasaṃjñā
kathyate | tathā ca hetusaṃjñābādhe ṇico'bhāvāddhetusamavāyinyā
dvitīyasyāḥ kriyāyā apratītermodakaḥ svakriyāyāṃ dhātuvācyāyāṃ
kartā bhavati na karma | rocate abhilāṣaviṣayabhāvamāpadyata ityarthaḥ |
hetusaṃjñāsaniyuktāyāḥ kartṛsaṃjñāyā api devadatasya bādhanāt
kartuḥ kriyayepsitatamatvābhāvāt | svātantryopalakṣaṇāyāmapi
kartṛśrutau sārthīddhīyata iti karmaṇi lakārasamarthanārthamatra modakasya
karmasaṃjñā na bhavati | yatra hi śabdopātāyā #ṃ kriyāyāṃ svātantryaṃ
pratīyate tatra bhavetadāśrayaṃ karmatvam | yathā hīyata iti hāne
śabdopāte sārthasyeti tadāśrayā pathikasya karmasaṃjñā | iha tu
śabdopātāyāṃ modakakriyāyāṃ rocata ityasmānna devadatasya
svātantryaṃ pratīyate, śabdavyāpārānapekṣāyāṃ tu vāstavasvātantryasya
kārakamātraviṣayatvāt kartṛśrutiranarthikā syāt | yadā tu
devadatāya rocate modaka ityayamartho devadataṃmodakaḥ
prīṇayatīti, tathā ca prīyamāṇa iti viśeṣaṇaṃ tadā karmasaṃjñāyāṃ
prāptāyāṃ sampradānasaṃjñāraṃbhaḥ | dhātorarthāntare hi vṛtau
sakarmakatvam | evaṃ 'devadatāya ślāghate' iti guṇotkarṣeṇa devadataḥ
śasyamāno guṇavatayā tatsamarthācaraṇādadhyāropitaprayojakabhāvo
hetusaṃjñāṃ prāpto guṇākhyānena vā jñāpayitumiṣṭo
jñāpanenāpyamānatvāt karmasaṃjña iti 
'ślāghahnuṅ' (gh. 1.4.34)
ityādinā sampradānasaṃjñaḥ kathyate | evaṃ hnautiprabhṛtīnāṃ yathāyogaṃ
vācyam | hnautiprabhṛtayo hyatra svārthopasarjanajñāpanavacanatvāt
sakarmakāḥ | tathā ca jñīpsyamāna iti saṃjñiviśeṣaṇamupātam |
'devadatāya śataṃ dhārayati' iti svarūpe'vatiṣṭhamānaṃ
śatamavasthāpayatyaghamarṇo dhārayati | atrotamarṇo devadato
dānakriyādvāreṇa nimitaṃ dhāraṇasya | yato'sau dadāti śataṃ na ca
tatra dadātikriyā śrūyate ityaśrūyamāṇakriyāviṣaye'tra kārakaśeṣe
ṣaṣṭhaḷaṃ prāptāyāṃ 
'dhārerutamarṇaḥ '(gh. 1.4.35)
iti sampradānasaṃjñā | yathāsaṃbhavaṃ hi hetvāditrayaṃ prāptamucyate na
tu pratyekam | 'puṣpebhyaḥ spṛ-hayati' iti
puṣpāṇāmīpsitatamatvāt karmasaṃjñāprasaṅga uktaḥ | druhyādau tu
karmasaṃjñāprasaṅgaḥ | drohasyāpakāratvāt tadarthāḥ sakarmakāḥ |
evamīrṣyāsūyārthā api | 'devadatāya rādhyati, īkṣate iti tu daivaṃ
paryālocanaṃ gaṇakaṃ devadataḥ prayuṅkte iti hetusaṃjñāyāṃ
prāptāyāṃ 
'rādhīkṣyoḥ ' (gh. 1.4.39)
iti sampradānatā | 'devadatāya gāṃ pratiśṛṇoti, aśṛṇoti' ityatra
tu devadato yāñcayā gāṃ pratijānānaṃ prayuṅkte iti hetusaṃjñāyāṃ
prāptāyāṃ 
'pratyāṅbhyāṃ śruvaḥ'(gh. 1.4.40)
iti sampradānatā | pūrvasya hi dhātvarthasya yācanalakṣaṇasya devadataḥ
kartā bhavan pratiśrave'ṅgīkāpare prayojako bhavati |
'hotre'nugṛṇāti, pratigṛṇāti ' ityatrāpi 'hotāraṃ śaṃsanena
protsāhayati' iti hotuḥ karmasaṃjñāprasaṅge
'anupratiguṇaśca' (gh. 1.4.41)
iti sampradānatā | śatāya parikrīto'nubrūhi' iti sādhakatamasya
parikrayaṇe niyatakālasvīkāre sampradānatā vikalpenoktā | tatra
rucyarthādiṣvati ādiśabdopātasya sujñānatvāt karaṇasaṃjñāprasaṅgo
noktaḥ | viśeṣavivakṣāyā abhāve cātra sarvatraiva
kriyānimitatvamātrāśrayeṇa śeṣatve prāpte sampradānatodāhāryā |
duhyādiparigaṇanāddhi akathitakarmatātra nāsti | nanu ca pūrvamapi
sampradānaśāstreṇaivoktaṃ
'karmaṇā yamabhipraiti' (gh. 1.4.32)
iti | tatkathamatra śāstreṇeti viśeṣyabhaṇitiḥ | ucyate | anvarthatvād
sampradānaśabdasya laukika eva sampradānārthaḥ pūrvamuktaḥ | iha tu
rucyarthādiviṣaye tadarthābhāvāccchabdasaṃskāramātrapratipādanāya
śāstrāraṃbhasāmarthyāt sampradānatvamucyata iti śāstrīyalaukikabhedena
dvividhaṃ sampradānaṃ vyākhyātam || 130 ||
iha śrāddhaya nigalhate, yuddhāya saṃnahyate, patye śete
ityakarmakadhātuviṣaye karmaṇo'bhāvātenābhipreyamāṇasya sampradānatā
na siddhyatīti 
'kriyāgrahaṇamapi kartavyam'
iti vārtike'bhihitam | kriyayābhipreyamāṇasyātra syāt
sampradānasaṃjñā | etadbhāṣye pratyākhyātam |
kriyāpi kriyayāpyamānatvāt kṛtrimaṃ karma | śrāddhāya nigalhate
ityādau ca kayā kriyayā kriyāpyamāneti sandarśanādikriyayetyuktam
| tatra saṃdarśanādikriyayā abhedena dhātunābhidhānāt |
kathamabhedāśrayaḥ kriyākārakabhāvo yujyata iti samarthayitumāha |
bhedasya ca vivakṣāyāṃ pūrvā pūrvā kriyāṃ prati |
parasyāṅgasya karmatvānna kriyāgrahaṇaṃ kṛtam || 131 ||
(126) sandarśanaprārthanādyavasāyairāpyamānatvāt kriyāpi kṛtrimaṃ karma
| tatra praikṣāpūrvakāriṇaḥ pravṛtāvāvaśyakena sandarśanādayaḥ
sannihitāḥ pratīyanta iti teṣāṃ pradhānakriyāyā bhedavivakṣāyāṃ
pūrvapūrvakriyāpekṣayotarotarasyāṅgasya pradhānakriyārthasya
karmatvamaviruddhaṃ pūrvapūrvakriyayāpyamānatvāditi kriyāyā api
karmatāyāṃ tayābhisaṃbadhyamānasya sūtreṇaiva sampradānatā siddheti
kriyāgrahaṇaṃ pratyākhyātam | dadātikarmaṇetyayaṃ nāṅgīkriyate |
tadatra dhātunā pradhānakriyaivocyate | sāmarthyaprāptāstu
saṃdarśanādayaḥ tato bhinnā iti kriyākārakabhāvopapatiḥ | tadyathā |
sārthāddhīyate iti pratīyamānaṃ sārthasya svātantryaṃ karmatvanimitaṃ
yadi vā tādarthyāt pradhānakriyayā prāptābhedānāṃ
dhātubhirabhidhāne'pi na sarvathaiva bhedatiraskāra
ityabhedākhyakāryānumitabhedapratītyā karmatopapatiḥ || 131 ||
yadyevamodanaṃ pacatītyādāvapi kriyākarmaṇābhipreyamāṇasya
sampradānasaṃjñā syāt | odanādeḥ karmasaṃjñā tu kriyākarmasu
sāvakāśetyata āha |
kriyāṇāṃ samudāye tu yadaikatvaṃ vivakṣitam |
tadā karma kriyāyogāt svākhyayaivopacaryate || 132 ||
(127) sandarśanādikriyāsamudāyaḥ parasparamaṅgābhāvamanāpannaḥ
samakakṣyatathā samuccīyamānarūpo yadaikaphalatvādabhedena
dhātunāṅgīkriyate tadā bhedanibandhanasya
kriyākārakabhāvasyāpratīterapratilabdhakarmabhāvayā kriyayā
saṃbadhyamānamodanādikarma svākhyayaiva
kriyapepsitatamatvanimitabhūtayā karmasaṃjñayaiva vyavahriyate, na tu
sampradānasajñayetyarthaḥ | samuccayenābhidhānādaṅgāṅgibhāvābhāvāt
kriyāyāḥ karmarūpatā nāstyatra pakṣe || 132 ||
bhedavivakṣāyāṃ tarhi sampradānatātra syādityāśaṅkayāha |
bhedābhedavivakṣā ca svabhāvena vyavasthitā |
tasmādgatyarthakarmatve vyabhicāro na dṛśyate || 133 ||
(128) lokikaprayogaḥ prasiddha iti tamuddiśyānvākhyāne
yathāprayogamupāyaparikalpanayā niyatabhedābhedavivakṣetyarthaḥ | evaṃ
ca gatyarthakarmaṇi dvitīyācaturthyau api ubhayavivakṣāśrayeṇa siddheti
nārthastadāraṃbheṇa | anyatrābhedavivakṣāniyamād
vyabhicāro'tiprasaṅgo nāsti || 133 ||
etadevāgamena dḍhayati |
vikalpenaiva sarvatra saṃjñe syātāmubhe yadi |
āraṃbheṇa na yogasya pratyākhyānaṃ samaṃ bhavate || 134 ||
(129) bhedābhedavivakṣāniyamānāśrayaṇe sarvatra
karmasampradānasaṃjñayorbhāvānniyamārthaṃ gatyarthakarmaṇītyādisūtraṃ
bhāṣyakāro na pratyācakṣīta | pratyākhyāne hyatiprasaṅgaḥ syāt | yadā
tu vivakṣāniyamaścetasi bhāṣyakārasya spharati
tadāraṃbhapratyākhyānayoraviśeṣaṃ manyamāno yuktaṃ yat pratyācakṣīta |
tadevaṃ gatyarthaviṣaye vivakṣādvayam | śrāddhāya nigalhata ityevamādau
tu bhedavivakṣaiva | pacatyodanamityādāvabhedavivakṣaiva |
ceṣṭāyāmanadhvanītyetatpratyudāharaṇayorapi cābhedavivakṣaiva, manasā
pāṭaliputraṃ gaccchatyadhvānaṃ gaccchatīti | sūtrāraṃbhe ca striyaṃ
gaccchatyajāṃ nayatītyatiprasaṅge 
'siddhaṃ tvasaṃprāptavacanāt' (ja.od gh. 2.3.12)
ityuktam | asamprāptaṃ gamanenāsaṃbaddhaṃ yatkarma tatra dvitīyācaturthyau
vaktavye | ajāstriyau tu samprāpte karmaṇītyatiprasaṅgo na bhavati |
evaṃ ca viśeṣasūtrārambhe 'adhvanaścānapavādaḥ' (ja od gh. 2.3.12)
ityuktam | adhvanīti pratiṣedho na vaktavyaḥ | adhvā hi samprāptaṃ karmeha
pratiṣeddhumabhipretaḥ | tasya cāsamprāptavacanādeva vyudāsaḥ siddhaḥ | ata
eva bhāṣye'bhihitam | 
āsthitapratiṣedho vaktavyaḥ (g.eṇa od gh. 2.3.12)
iti | āsthito'dhvātra pratiṣedhaviṣayo vijñeyaḥ || 134 ||
(130) 'adhvanyarthagrahaṇam' (ja od gh. 2.3.12)
iti vacanādadhvaparyāyebhyo'pi pratiṣedhaḥ, panthānaṃ gaccchatītyādau |
āsthita ākrānto gamikriyayā adhvā | udāharaṇam | pravṛtagamano hi
mārga āsthita ityucyate | atra yeyaṃ gamikriyā sā dvirūpā bhavati,
ākrāntasya patho yo'tikamaṇīyo bhāgaḥ tatra tyāgarūpaṃ hīnarūpaṃ
kriyāyāḥ | prāpye tvāsādanīye bhāge saṃsargarūpaṃ prāptarūpamiti
tyāgopādānalakṣaṇāvayavabhedāt kriyā dvividhā tathā ca
tyāgopādānarūpāviccchinnapravāhagamikriyākrānto'dhvā
kriyāviṣayabhāvamātravidhīyamānakarmasaṃjñāgocaro na tu kriyayā
karmabhūtayā'bhisaṃbadhyate kriyābhedābhāvāditi sampradānasaṃjñābhāvaḥ |
grāmādereva tadupāyāprāpyasyānadhvarūpasya tadyogaḥ |
yastvanāsthito'dhvā tatra dve eva vibhaktī bhavataḥ | kathaṃ
cānāsthito'dhvā bhavati | yadotpathena prāpnoti panthānaṃ pathe
gaccchati, panthānaṃ gaccchatīti, tadevaṃ sūtrāraṃbhe viśeṣo vaktavyaḥ |
pratyākhyāne tu vivakṣāniyamenaiveṣṭaṃ siddham || 135 ||
iti bhūtirājatanayahelārājakṛte prakīrṇaprakāśe sādhanasamuddeśe
sampradānādhikāraḥ ||
--------------
(131) idānīṃ vibhaktividhānakrameṇa tadarthavicāre catuthyarye nirṇīte
prāptāvasaraṃ paṃcamyarthamapādānaṃ vicārayitumāha |
nirdiṣṭaviṣayaṃ kiṃcidupātaviṣayaṃ tathā |
apekṣitakriyaṃ ceti tridhāpādānamucyate || 136 ||
apāye sādhye yad
dhruvamekarūpamanāviṣṭāpāyalakṣaṇakriyamanyasminnapāyenāviṣṭe'pi
tatsādhanamapādānamityavadhiviśeṣāpekṣatvāt
saṃrabdhodāsīnobhayakartṛko'pāyo'pādānasya viṣayaḥ sa nirdiṣṭaḥ
svaśabdenopāto yasya tadekamapādānamū | tadyathā 'grāmādāgaccchati',
'parvatādavarohati' ityādi | upātaḥ kriyāntarasya guṇabhāvena
pradhānabhāvena vā yatrāpāyalakṣaṇo
viṣayastadanyadupātaviṣayapādānam | tadyathā 'valahakāt vidyotate',
'kusūlāt pacati', 'brāhmaṇācchṃasī' iti | atra hi niḥsaraṇāṃṅge
vidyotane nṛvidyotanāṅge vā niḥsaraṇe vidyautirvartate iti
niḥsaraṇavakṣaṇo'pāyo vidyotanasya guṇapradhānabhāvenopātaḥ |
valāhakānniḥsṛtya jyotirvidyotate, valāhakādvā vidyotamānaṃ
niḥsaratītyarthaḥ | kusūlādadāya pacatītyādānāṅge pāke paciḥ |
brāhmaṇād gṛhṇitvā śaṃsatīti grahaṇāṅge śaṃsane śaṃsirvartate |
'paṃcamīprakaraṇe brāhmaṇācchṃasina upasaṃkhyānam'
(ja. od gh. 6.3.2)
ityaluk | tatra vārtikakārasya dvitīyārthe paṃcamyeṣābhipretā | yadāha
'anyārthe ca' (ja. od gh. 6.3.2)
iti | brāhmaṇāni granthaviśeṣarūpāṇi yaḥ śaṃsati sa kathyate
brāhmaṇācchṃasīti vṛtiviṣaye'tra dvitīyārthe paṃcamī vaktavyā |
bhāṣyakārastu brāhmaṇād gṛhītvā āhṛtya śaṃsatītyāharaṇāṅge
śaṃsane śaṃsirvartate iti bruvannupātaviṣayametadupādānaṃ manyate |
apekṣitakriyaṃ tvapādānaṃ yatra sarvathaiva kriyāśabdasyānupalabdheraśrutā
kriyānumīyamānā saṃbandhiṣu dravyeṣu saṃjñāviśeṣahetūnāṃ
śaktibhedānāmāvirbhāve nimitatvāya kalapate | tadyathā māthurā
pāṭaliputrakebhya āḍhaḷtarā iti | atra hi prakarṣapratyasāmarthyāt
vibhāgaheturanumīyamānāpakarṣaṇakriyāpāyalakṣaṇetyapekṣitāsāvatra
kriyā | upātaviṣaye tu dhātunaivāpāyaḥ svīkṛtaḥ | anekārthatvena
dhātūnām | valāhako vidyotate, caitraḥ pacati, dāśatayīḥ śaṃsati
ityādau vidyotanādimātravacanatve'pyanyatra niḥsaraṇādyaṅge
vṛteravirodhāt, paṃcamīśrutisāmarthyāddhyapāyo'tra pratīyate | sa
ca niḥsaraṇādyaṅgatve dhātvarthasyopapadyate nānyatheti
tadarthatāniścayaḥ || 136 ||
kaḥ punaramapāyaḥ, kiṃ gatimātraṃ netyāha |
saṃyogibhedādebhinnātmā gamireva bhramiryathā |
dhruvāvadhirapāyo'pi samavetastathādhruve || 137 ||
(132) saṃyogena sāhacaryādvibhāgo'pyavadhiḥ svīkṛtaḥ | tena
digviśeṣāvaccchinnasaṃyogavibhāgakāryaṃ yathā gamanameva bhramaṇamiti
vyapadiśyate yathā ca gamanagrahaṇād bhramaṇarecanaspandanādayo gṛhyante
na jātyantarāṇīti kāṇādāḥ evaṃ
dhruvamacalamanāviṣṭāpāyamavadhiryasyādhruve cale samavetasyāsau
saṃrabdhodāsīnasādhanasādhyastyāgastadubhayakārakagatavibhāgalakṣaṇaphalo'pāyākhyo gativiśeṣa eva boddhavyaḥ gantari samavetāpīyaṃ kriyā'vadhyapekṣaṇādapāyākhyāṃ labhata iti tatra dhruvasya kriyāsiddhau nimitatvaṃ kārakatvamupapadyata ityāśayaḥ | sa cāpādānasaṃjñāyā viṣayabhāvenāvasthito'pāyo dhātunābhidhīyamāno'numīyamāno vā dhruvamapādānasaṃjñamapāye sādhya iti tatra nimitatvaṃ dhruvasya kārakasya | evamapāyo vyākhyātaḥ | dhruvaṃ hi sthiramacalaṃ loke prasiddham | tataśca vārtike coditam |
'gatiyukteṣvapādānasaṃjñā nopapadyate adhrutvāt ' (ja. od gh. 1.4.25)
iti | dhāvato'śvāt patitaḥ, sārthād gaccchato hīna ityādau
gatiyukteṣvapādānasaṃjñiṣu dhruvatvasya sthipatvasyācalatvasyābhāvāt
saṃjñā na prāpnotītyarthaḥ || atra parihāro'bhihito 
'na vā dhrauvyasyāvivakṣitatvāt'(ja. od gh. 1.4.25)
iti || 137 ||
etad vyākhyātumāha |
dravyatvabhāvo na dhnauvyamiti sūtre pratīyate |
apāyaviṣayaṃ ghrauvyaṃ yatu tāvadvivakṣitam || 138 ||
(133) dhruvaṃ kūṭasthaṃ niṣkriyamiti dravyasvabhāvo dhnauvyaṃ sūtre na
pratyevyam, api tvasaṃtuṣṭacalano'tra dhruvaśabdaḥ | tathā hyapāyasādhye
yaddhruvaṃ tenāpāyenāsaṃspṛṣṭamityeṣo'rthaḥ | evaṃ ca kṛtvā
dhāvatoṣaÃśvātpatita ityatra pātasyāśvakartṛkatvābhāvātenānāveśe
dhruvatāśvasya tatrāstyeva | saṃrabdhodāsīnasādhanasādhyo hyapāyaḥ |
tatrāśva udāsīnaḥ, apetā tu saṃrabdhaḥ | grāmādāgaccchati pathā
parvatamiti tu pathaḥ karaṇasya parvatasya karmaṇo'pāyenānāveśād
dhruvatāyāṃ satyāmapi paratvāt
karaṇādisaṃjñābhirbādhanādapādānasaṃjñā na bhavati | tathā ca vārtikam

apādānamutarāṇi | dhanuṣā vidhyati, kaṃsapātryāṃ bhuṅkte, gāṃ
dodhi, dhanurvidhyati |
(ja od gh. 1.4.1)
iti |
tadevamapāyaviṣayasyāśrayaṇādaśvasyāpāyenānāveśādvastutastadviṣayamadhrauvyamabhavadevāvivakṣitamiti vārtikārthaḥ || 138 ||
tathā hi |
saraṇe devadatasya dhrauvyaṃ pāte tu vājinaḥ |
āviṣṭaṃ yadapāyena tasyādhrauvyaṃ pracakṣate || 139 ||
(134) saraṇe'śvasamavāyinyāṃ vegitāyāṃ gatau yathā devadatasya
dhrauvyaṃ tena saraṇenānāveśāt tathā devadatasamavete pāte
patanakriyāyāṃ vājinaḥ aśvasya tayānāveśāt dhrauvyamiti
dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikabhāvāntarbhāvenedamudāharaṇam |
hetutvenāpāyānāveśo vyatirekamukhenomayatrāpi dhrauvya
upātaḥ | yadyapi cāśvasaraṇe devadatasya dhrauvyaṃ tathāpyapādānasaṃjñā
na bhavati 
'gatirvinā tvavadhinā nāpāya iti gamyate' iti
vakṣmayamāṇatvādavadhibhāvena devadatasyānupādānāt | tathopādāne
tu bhavatyeva sarato devadatāddhāvatyaśva iti | 
yatra tarhi dvāvapi saparispandau tatra kathaṃ dhruvādhruvavibhāgo niśceya
ityāśaṅkyāha | 
ubhāvapyadhrūvau meṣau yadyapyubhayakarmaje |
vibhāge pravibhakte tu kriye tatra vivakṣite || 140 ||
(135) sthāṇoḥ śyeno'pasarpatītyanyatarakarmaje vibhāge
sphuṭabhevāpāyenānāveśāt tyāṇau dhruvatā niścīyate | apasarpato
meṣānmeṣo'pasarpatītyatrāpyubhayakarmaje vibhāge yadyapyubhāvapi
meṣāvapāyāveśādadhruvau tathāpītarāpāyenetaralasyānāveśāt
kartṛbhedādbhinne evāpāyalakṣaṇe kriye || 140 ||
tataśca 
meṣāntarakriyāpekṣamavadhitvaṃ pṛthak pṛthak |
meṣayoḥ svakriyāpekṣaṃ kartṛtvaṃ ca pṛthak pṛthak || 141 ||
(136) itaretarakriyametaretarasyānāveśādanyakriyāyāmanyasya
dhrauvyaṃ svakriyāyāṃ tu kartṛtvaṃ dvayorapi | yadā tu
parasparamavadhibhāvo na vivakṣyate, api tvekaśabdopādāne
dvigate'pi kriye vivakṣyate || 141 ||
tadāha |
abhedena kriyaikā tu dvisādhyā cedvivakṣitā |
meṣāvapāye kartārau yadyanyo vidyate'vadhiḥ || 142 ||
(137) meṣāvapasarpata ityākhyātapadena kartṛdvayanirvartyā kriyekyena
pratyāyiteti dvayorapi meṣayoḥ kriyāveśena kartṛtvamapāyena
tvekasyāvadhitvamityapādānatā na bhavati | evaṃ ca kṛtvāpāyakartṛtvaṃ
meṣayorbhavati yadānyaḥ parvatādapasarpata iti parasparo
meṣāvapasarpata iti parasparato meṣavapasarpata iti | evaṃ tvabhidhāne
meṣāntarakriyāpekṣamavadhitvaṃ dvayoḥ svakriyāpekṣaṃ ca
kartṛtvaṃmityuktam | kartṛbhedāddhyatra
kriyābhedānumānamākhyātādekyenāpi pratīyate || 142 ||
yadyanyo vidyate'vadhiḥ tadā meṣāvapāyakartārau nānyathetyetad
bhāṣyagrānthasaṃvādenopapādayitumāha |
gatirvinā tvavadhinā nāpāya iti gamyate |
vṛkṣasya parṇaṃ patatītyevaṃ bhāṣye nidarśitam || 143 ||
(138) kārakasūtre saṃjñinirdeaśaḥ kartavyatayā coditaḥ , sādhakaṃ
kārakamiti vaktavyam, anyathā akriyānimitasyāpi kārakaprasaṅgaḥ
'apādānaṃ ca, vṛkṣasya parṇaṃ patatīti' (ja. od gh. 1.4.23)
ityudāhato vārtike | savārtikaṃ ca bhāṣyamiti bhāṣya ityāha | etacca
gateḥ sāvadhikāya pāyatve na liṅgam | api tvasya parihārāya 
'na vā'pāyasyāvivakṣitatvāt' (ja. od gh.  1.4.23)
iti | yaduktamityanena bhāṣyaprabandho'tra nirdiṣṭaḥ | ayaṃ cātrārthaḥ |
vṛkṣasaṃbandhinaḥ parṇasya pāto vākyārtho vivakṣitaḥ iti
vṛkṣasyāvadhitvavivakṣātra nāsti | tathā cāpāyo'yaṃ na bhavati |
apāyatvenāvadhyapekṣārūpeṇāvivakṣitatvādityatrāpādānatā vṛkṣasya na
bhavatīti, yadi tvapāyo'tra vivakṣyate tadā vṛkṣasyāvadhitvāt
vṛkṣāt parṇaṃ patatīti bhavati, tataśca na jñāyeta kasya saṃbandhi parṇaṃ
kaṅkasya vā kurasya vetyuktam | tasmātparṇaviśeṣaṇatvena vṛkṣasya
vivakṣitatvād vṛkṣamatyajatyapi parṇe sākhārathe bhūmiṃ spṛśati
prayogo'yaṃ bhavatyevetyapāyavivakṣoktā | etasmāccāvasīyate
sāvadhikagatirapāyo na śuddhā,
anyathāvadhyavivakṣāyāmatrāpāyasambhavādapādānatvaṃ syādeveti
jñāpakametat | evaṃ ca cāpāyenaivāvadherākṣepātasyaiva viśeṣaṇam |
śabdavat saṃjñipratipatyarthaṃ dhruvagrahaṇaṃ
saṃrabdhodāsīnobhayasādhyatāmapāyasya darśayitumiti vyākhyātām ||
143 ||
gatamadaḥ | idamidānīṃ vaktavyam 'apādānamutarāṇi' iti vipratiṣedhaḥ
kātyāyanenoktaḥ | tathā coktaiḥ kārakairapādānasya bādhitatvāt
valāhakādi vidyotate, valāhake vidyotate, valāhako vidyotate iti
bhāṣye saṃjñāsamāveśo darśito viruddha ityāśaṅkya bhāṣyaṃ
samarthayitumāha |
bhedābhedau pṛthagbhāvaḥ sthitiśceti virodhinaḥ |
yugapanna vivakṣvante sarve dharmā valāhake || 144 ||
(139) dhūmajyotiḥ salilamarutāṃ saṃghāto valāhakaḥ, tatrāvayavasya
jyotiṣo vidyotanam | tacca jyotiryadā tasmāt samudāyātmano
valāhakād bhedena vivakṣyate tadā bhedavivakṣāyāṃ pṛthagbhāvenaiva
vā vivakṣyate tatrāvasthānena vā | yadā pṛthagbhāvena vivakṣyate tadā
valāhako'vadhiḥ
pṛthagbhāvalakṣaṇasyāpāyasyetyupātaviṣayamidamapādānaṃ
vyākhyātam-kalāhakād vidyotate vidyaujjyotīrūpeti valāhakāt
pṛthagbhūya niḥsṛtyeti niḥsaraṇāṅge vidyotatane dhātuḥ | yadā tu
nirādhārasya tejaso'vasthānābhāvād
valāhako'vasthitihetutvādādhārabhāvena vivakṣyate
tadādhikaraṇasaṃjño'sau jāyate 'valāhake vidyotate' iti, valāhake
sthitvā prakāśata ityarthaḥ | abindhanatvādvaidyautasya jātavedaso
valāhako jalapūrṇo'pyādhāro yujyate | evamudakasaṃbhedāt
pārthivena dhūmena cāvilīkārād valāhakādbhedo nāsti
tāvatejasaḥ prakāśakartṛtvābhāva iti bhedavivakṣāyāṃ
śaktidvayāvirbhāvo valāhake darśitaḥ | pṛthagbhāvo sthitau ca
bhinnaviṣaye eva | yadā tu svaccchatvād valāhakadravyasya
tatrapyamudakādi tejasaḥ prakāśe pratibandhakaṃ na bhavatītyabhedena
valāhakākhyaḥ samudāyastejo'vayavavaśādvidyotanasamartho vivakṣyate
tadātra kartṛśaktirevojbhṛmbhate valāhako vidyotata iti |
avayavanibandhanā hi samudāyamanupatanti vyapadeśāḥ | tadyathā
kisalayita mupavanamiti vṛkṣasamudāyasyopavanaśabdavācyatve
tadavayavānāṃ saṃjātakisalayatve samudāyastathā vyapadeśārhaḥ |
tadevaṃ vivakṣābhedāt paryāyeṇa bhinnaśaktirvalāhako bhāṣya udāhṛtaḥ,
na tu yupaccchaktivivakṣāyāmiti na vipratiṣedhavacanenāsya virodhaḥ
kaścit || 144 ||
yatu vipratiṣedhodāharaṇaṃ tatra yugapadeva śatkyorvivakṣetyāha |
dhanuṣā vidhyatītyatra vināpāyavivakṣayā |
karaṇatvaṃ yato nāsti tasmātadubhayaṃ saha || 145 ||
(140) dhanuṣā vidhyatīti paratvātkaraṇasaṃjñā'pādānasaṃjñāṃ bādhata
ityudāhṛtam | tatra yugapad prāptirāvaśyakena saṃjñayoriti tad
dvayaṃ sādhakatamatvamapāyaśca sahaikasmin dravye vartate |
yato'pāyavivakṣāmantareṇa sādhakatamatvaṃ vyadhane dhanuṣo nāsti | na
hi daṇḍeneva dhanuṣā vidhyati, api tvapasarpataḥ
śarasyavādhibhāvamupayaddhanurvyadhananimitatāṃ pratipadyata iti
yugapadubhayavivakṣāyāṃ bhinnaviṣayayorapi śaktayoḥ
nimitanimitibhāvenāvasthānādekatra dravye samāveśāt
svanimitasaṃjñādvayahetutve yugapadekasmāt prātipadikāt
tatkāryavibhaktidvayotpatyayogāt ekasaṃjñādhikāropajanite
virodhe paratvāt karaṇasaṃjñānimitā vibhaktirbhavati | śaktyorhi
nāstyatra virodhaḥ, bhinnaviṣayatvāt | śaraniḥsaraṇe hyapādānaṃ vyadhane
tu karaṇaṃ dhanuḥ | āviṣyaśakti ca dravyameva saṃjñāviṣaya iti
tadanapekṣayaikatra prāptiḥ | tathā hi vibhaktividhāne 'apādāne'
'karaṇe' ityādisaṃjñāviśeṣeṇa dravyatvameva viśiṣyate |
ekasyānekasaṃjñāpravṛtāvapi ca nāsti virodha iti
saṃjñākāryavibhaktimukhena virodho'tra vyākhyeyaḥ |
etaccodāharaṇamupalakṣaṇārthamupātamityanyatrāpi
vipratiṣedhodāharaṇe yugapadavivakṣā yojyā | tathā hi 'kaṃsapātryāṃ
bhuṅkte' ityatrānnādanādirūpāyā bhujeḥ kaṃsapātrī
dhārayantyannamatrādhāraḥ | sa cāsyādhārabhāvastāvannopapadyate
yāvadapādānatā nāsti | kaṃsapātryā hyannamuddhṛtya bhujyata iti
bhavatyapādānam | na hi parṇapuṭena sahānnamabhyavaharato
hastyādestatra bhuṅkta iti bhavatyādhārasampratyayaḥ | yadi ca kaṃsapātrī
na bhavedannādānasyāvadhistadā bhujirnaiva niṣpadyetetyapāthavivakṣayā
vinādhikaraṇatvābhāvādubhayoryugapadvivakṣāyāṃ saṃjñādvayaprasaṅge
'apādānamutarāṇi' ityadhikaraṇasaṃjñayā parayā pūrvā'pādānasaṃjñā
bācyate eva | 'gāṃ dogdhi' iti kṣāraṇavacane duhau
nāntarīyako'pāyaḥ | na hi goranapayati payasi kṣāraṇaṃ sampadyata iti
pādānatānāntarīyakā karmaśaktiḥ goḥ paratvāt kāryaṃ prayuṅkte |
evaṃ dhanurvidhyatītyapāyavivakṣāṃ vinā vyadhane dhanuṣaḥ svātantryaṃ
nāstītyubhayasaṃnidhau 'apādānamutarāṇi' iti parā kartṛsaṃjñaiva
bhavati || 145 ||
evamatra viṣame dve śaktī abhyupagamya nāntarīyakatvādekatra dravye
samāveśe saṃjñādvayaprāptau vibhaktyākhyākāryadvāreṇa virodhe sati
paratvena vyavasthoktā | samprati śaktyabhedamāśritya
saṃjñayorabhinnaviṣayopanipātamāha |
ekaiva sā satī śaktirdvirūpā vyavatiṣṭhate |
nimitaṃ saṃjñayostatra parayā bādhyate'parā || 146 ||
(141) bhinnakriyāviṣayāpi dravyasyaikaiva śaktiḥ, tayā hi tad dravyaṃ
kriyādvayaṃ sampādayati, iti tatra
kriyādvayaviṣaye'pādānakaraṇasaṃjñādvayaṃ labhata ityekatra
śaktausaṃjñādvayaprāptau parayā saṃjñayā na parā aparā pūrvā saṃjñā
bādhyate virodhād, ekasaṃjñādhikāranimitādityarthaḥ |
śaraniḥsaraṇe'vadhibhāvo dhanuṣaḥ sādhakatamatvaśktyaivāntarbhāvitaḥ |
tena vānā tadayogāditi tāvatyekaiva śaktirmaṇyate |
parasparanirapekṣasya kāryadvayasya
pravibhaktarūpaśaktidvayaparikalpakasyābhāvāt | vyadhanakriyāyā hi
śarāpagamanakriyātra guṇabhūteti pṛthagātmani śaktyantaraṃ nākṣipati
| kevalamekaiva śaktirguṇapradhānabhūtakriyādvayaviṣayatayā bhinnarūpeva
cakāsti | dravyameva tarhyekaṃ kriyādvayaṃ sampādayat sādhanamastu, kiṃ
śaktiparikalpanayā | naitadupapadyate | abhinnasvabāvatvādasya
nānākriyāviṣayatāyogāt | śaktistu sāmarthyamanekakāryamapi
bhavati | yathoktam ---
"na śaktīnāṃ tathā bhedo yathā śaktimatāṃ sthitiḥ"
iti | yadyevaṃ pakūtvaudano bhujyatāmityatrāpyekaiva śaktirastu |
naitadupapadyate | antarbhūtenopāyena pṛthagātmānalabhamānena
svanimitaśaktibhedānākṣepāditi sthitam || 146 ||
pūrvaṃ tu guṇapradhānabhāvānāśrayeṇa kriyādvayamātraviṣayaṃ
śaktidvayamuktam | tatetadapādānaṃ 
'dhruvamapāye'pādānam' (gh. 1.4.24)
ityanenaiva lakṣitaṃ sarvamapādānapaṃcamīviṣayaḥ | aparastu 
nirdhāraṇe vibhakte yo bhītrādīnā ca yo vidhiḥ |
upātapekṣitāpāyaḥ so'budhapratipataye || 147 ||
(142) "pañcamī vibhakte" (gh. 2.3.42)
iti vibhaktiviśiṣṭe nirdhāraṇe gamyamāne pañcamīlakṣaṇo yo
vidhiruktaḥ so'pekṣitāpāyaḥ,
apāyasyānumīyamānasyāpekṣaṇādityapādānasaṃjñayaiva siddhaḥ | tasya hi
māthurāḥ pāṭaliputra kebhya āḍhaḷtarā ityetaduhāraṇam | atra
nirdhāraṇasya vibhaktaviṣayatvādatyantavibhaktatāpratipatyarthaṃ
vibhaktagrahaṇamiti pṛthagbhūtā eva pratipādyamānā etasya viṣayaḥ, na tu
gavāṃ kṛṣṇā sampannakṣīratametyādi, gotvenāpṛthagbhāvāt |
tatrātyantavibhāge'pi vivakṣite pūrva māḍhaḷtvena buddhyā
sāmyamavaśyāpekṣyam | tathā hyāḍhayatvena samānena guṇena
buddhyaikatvamāpādyātiśayaviśiṣṭāḍhaḷtāvaśāt pṛthakkriyetyasti
buddhisaṃśleṣapūrvako viśleṣo'pāyākhya ityapādāna ityeva siddhātra
pañcamī | apekṣitakriyaṃ hyaetadapādānam | taduktaṃ bhāṣye
'yastaiḥ sāmyaṃ gatavān sa evaṃ prayuṅkte' (g.eṇa. od gh. 1.4.24)
iti | kārakādhikāre tamabgrahaṇādatiśayo na vivakṣita iti
gauṇe'pyatra bauddhe'pāye'pādanatāviruddhā | tathā hi kārakavyavahāro
buddhyavasthānibandhana eva sarvaḥ | taduktaṃ prāk--
buddhyā samīhitaikatvān pañcālan kurubhiryadā |
punarvibhajate vaktā tadāpāyaḥ pratīyate ||
iti | evaṃ sāmānyenaiva siddhe sūtrāraṃbho bauddhamapāyamapratipadyamānaṃ
prati vivakṣāniyamārtham, apāyavivakṣaivātra kartavyetyerthaḥ | tathā 
'bhītrārthānāṃ bhayahetuḥ'(gh.  1.4.25)
'parājerasoḍhaḥ'(gh. 1.4.26)
ityādiśca yo vidhirapādānasaṃjñālakṣaṇaḥ so'yupāto guṇabhāvena
viṣayo'pāyalakṣaṇa kriyā yena sa evaṃvidha iti
upātaviṣayāpādānabhedodāharaṇarūpaḥ pūrvavadabudhabodhanārtha eva |
tatha hi vṛkebhyo bhayaṃ corād bimetīti ya eṣa manuṣyaḥ
prekṣāpūrvakārī bhavati sa buddhaya paśyati yadi māṃ vṛkāḥ
prāpnuyurdhruvamaniṣṭāpāta iti pūrvaṃ buddhyā vṛkān prāpya
buddhayantareṇa tato vibhāgaṃ pratipadyata iti bhayapūrvikāyāṃ nivṛtau
bibhetervartanād astyevāpāyaḥ | evaṃ caurebhyastrāyata
ityanarthapratighātavacane trāyatāvanarthaṃ corasaṃsargaṃ buddhyā niścitya
tataḥ svajanamapanayati suhṛditi saṃbhavatyapāyaḥ | adhyayanāt
parājayata ityadhyayanābhyāsaparikhinno guruparyupāsanāyāsabhīruḥ |
prāpyādhyayanaṃ tato nivartata ityastyevāpayaḥ | parājayatiścātra
khedapūrvake nivartane vartate , adhyayanāt khinnaḥ sannivartata ityarthaḥ
|
pūrvamevāpravṛtau tu khedo buddhyā samīkṣitaḥ |
kaṣṭena tapasā nityaṃ dṛṣṭopacarato gurūn ||
iti yevebhyo gāṃ vārayatīti yavasaṃprāpto gavāṃ yavavināśakṛtaṃ
pratyavāyamayaṃ nivartayati tata iti mukhya evāpāyaḥ kūpādandhaṃ
vārayati, agnermāṇavakaṃ vārayatīti buddhyā
kūpāgnisaṃprāptimaniṣṭahetuṃ manyamāno'nukampayā tato
nivartayatīti buddhisaṃśleṣapūrvako'pāyaḥ | upādhyāyād antardhatta
ityupādhyāyasamīpaprāptau preṣaṇopālaṃbhādiduḥkhaṃ buddhyā
niścitya tato'paitītyapāyaḥ | upādhyāyādadhīte, āgamayatīti ca
granthamādate tata ityapāyaḥ | upādhyāyamukhodīritā hi śabdāḥ
santānena vartamānaḥ
śiṣyamanupravīśantītyupādhyāyādavadhisthānīyāteṣāmapakramo'vasīyate | taduktaṃ bhāṣye |
'saṃtatattvāt |' (g.eṇa. od gh. 1.4.29)
iti | anupayoge tu naṭasya śṛṇotīty apāyo na vivakṣyate | na hi
naṭād grantham ādatte, api tu naṭasaṃbandhi
śṛṇotīty etāvad atrārthasatattvam | śṛṅgācccharo jāyata iti tato
niḥsaratītyevaṃ lokasya pratipatter asty evāpāyaḥ | śāstre ca
padārtho laukikaḥ pravibhajyate 
ityuktamiti tīrthikaparikalpitaprakriyānādaraḥ | tīrthikānāṃ hi
keṣāṃcit kāryakāraṇayoḥ pariṇāmāśrayeṇa bheda iti vibhāgabhāvaḥ |
himavato gaṅgā prabhavatīti srotorūpā gaṅgā tataḥ prasarpatīti
mukhya evāpāyaḥ | gauṇaṃ cāpāyam āśritya
'jugupsāvirāmapramādarthānām upasaṃkhyānam'(ja. od gh. 1.4.24)
pratyākhyātam | tathāhi adharmājjugupsate viramatītyāstikaḥ paratra
duḥkhānubandhinamadharmamavagamya jugupsāpūrvakaṃ
tato'paitītyastyapāyaḥ | nāstikaḥ punarihaiva kleśaphalaṃ
dharmamavagatya tataḥ pramādena nivartate iti pramādyatītyucyata iti
sarvatra pūrveṇaivāpādānasaṃjñāyāmutaro vidhiḥ prapaṃcāryaḥ |
lakṣaṇaprapaṃcābhyāṃ hi vyākaraṇam | yathoktam tata eva vidhaya
ityādīti siddham || 147 ||
iti bhūtirājatanayahelārājakṛte prakīrṇaprakāśe
sādhanasamuddeśe'pādānādhikāraḥ |

(143)idānīṃ vibhaktividhānukramāyātaṃ ṣaṣṭayarthaṃ śeṣalakṣaṇatikramya
kārakaviśeṣaprakramāyātamadhikaraṇaṃ saptamyarthaṃ vicārayati |
kartṛkarmavyavahitāmasākṣāddhārayat kriyām |
upakurvat kriyāsiddhau śāstre'dhikaraṇaṃ smṛtam || 148 ||
loke'dhikaraṇaviśeṣeṇa dravyaguṇakriyāviṣayamabhidhīyate | śāstre
tvasmin kārakaprakaraṇāt kriyākṣepe kriyāyāmevopakāraviśeṣeṇa
dhāraṇalakṣaṇenādhikaraṇasaṃjñāvatiṣṭhate | tatra kriyā kartari karmaṇi
vāvatiṣṭhata iti tayoḥ kriyādhāraṇādadhikaraṇasaṃjñā vacanāt
paryāyeṇa syāt | karmasthakriyāviṣayā vā kartṛsaṃjñā
kartṛsthakriyāviṣayā vā karmasaṃjñā syāditi tatra yathāyogaṃ
kartṛkarmasaṃjñābāve'dhikaraṇasaṃjñā prasajyata ityāśaṅkyoktaṃ
'kartṛkarmavyavahitām' ityādi | kaṭe āste, sthālyāṃ pacatīti
kartṛkarmadhāraṇāt tatsamavetāyāṃ kriyāyāmupakārakamadhikaraṇaṃ
pāramparyeṇa | asati hyādhāre kartṛkarmaṇī kriyopakāraṃ na
pratipadyeyātāmiti tayorādhāra upakurvan kriyāmapi
tatsthāmupakaroti | kārakādhikāre ca taratamayogo nāstītyatra
paramparayā kriyādhārake'dhikaraṇasaṃjñāyā sāvakāśatvāt sākṣāt
kriyādhārakayoḥ paratvāt kartṛkarmasaṃjñe eva | kārakāṇāṃ ca
kriyānimitatvaṃ yathā tathāpi bhavadāśrīyata iti vyavadhānena
kriyāpakārakatvamaviruddham | 'vyavahitām' ityukte'pi 'asākṣāt'
iti vacanaṃ lokānusāreṇāpi padārthavyavasthāyāmadhikaraṇasya
kriyādhāraṇa prati vyavadhānaparipoṣaṇārtham | tathā hi śabdāt
pratīyamāno'rtho laukikaḥ saṃskāranimitamucyate | pṛthivyāṃ śete,
sthālyāṃ pacyata ityādau cākhyātaśabdāt kriyāviśiṣṭe kartṛkarmaṇī
pratipadyete, na kriyāmātraṃ tato nikṛṣṭarūpamiti
kriyāśrayadhāraṇādeva loke'dhikaraṇasya kriyopakāraḥ pratīyate na
sākṣāt || 148 ||
idānīmaupaśleṣikādibhede'pyadhikaraṇasya
sāmānbhetallakṣaṇamanugatamityāha----
upaśleṣasya cābhedastilākāśakaṭādiṣu |
upakārāstu bhidyante saṃyogisamavāyinām || 149 ||
(144) upaśleṣa ādhārasyādheyena saṃbandhaḥ | yadvaśādasāvādhāraḥ | tasya
triṣvapyadhikaraṇeṣvabhedaḥ | anyathā tu saṃyoginyādhāre saṃbandhaḥ,
anyathā tu samavāyinīti saṃbandhibhedādbhinnatvena vyapadeśaḥ | tathā
hi kaṭe āste devadata iti saṃyoginaḥ kaṭasya sakalāvayavavyāptyā
devadatopaśleṣo na dṛśyate, api tu
katipayāvayavavyāptetyaviśeṣādaupaśleṣika iti
sāmānyasaṃjñayādhāro'yamucyate | tileṣu tailamiti tu samavāyinā
tailarasena tilānāṃ samastāvayavavyāptyā lokaprasiddhyā saṃbandhād
vyāpakametadadhikaraṇam | khe śakunaya ityatra tvākāśasyā
tātvikāvayavābhāvāt kalpitapradeśatayā
saṃbandhādvaiṣayikametadadhikaraṇam | ananyatra bhāvaścātra viṣayārthaḥ |
evaṃ jale matsyā ityādhārapradeśāpekṣayā
caitadaupaśleṣikamadhikaraṇam | gurau vasatīti śiṣyāṇāṃ
gurvadhīnāyāṃ vṛtau vaiṣayikamadhikaraṇaṃ guruḥ | upaśleṣo'pyatra
bauddhaḥ | evaṃ yuddhe sannahyatīti yuddhābhisaṃndhinā sannahanasya
tanutrādibandhanarūpasya pravṛteḥ yuddhaṃ viṣayaḥ | gaṅgāyāṃ gāva iti
gaṅgāśabdaḥ sāmīpyāt pradeśavṛtirityaupaśveṣikamadhikaraṇam |
śatrorabhāve sukhamityādāvabhāvasya bauddhaṃ kārakatvamiti prāgeva
nirṇītam || 149 ||
ke punarupakārā ityāha |
avināśo gurutvasya pratibandhe svatantratā |
digviśeṣādavaccheda ityādyā bhedahetavaḥ || 150 ||
(145) avināśaḥ tailasya tilakṛta upakāraḥ | tilavināśe hi tailaṃ
vikīrṇaṃ naśyet | paryaṅke śeta iti śayiturgurutvapratibandhe
svatantraḥ kartā ādhāraḥ | anyathā gurutvāt patanaṃ bhavet | khe
śakuntādadaya ityādau khena śakuntādīnāmadharadiśā
saṃbandhasyāpāvartanamityayameva tasyādheyopayogaḥ |
ādiśabdāccchakaṭādinādhāreṇa deśaviśeṣasaṃprāptiḥ | guruṇā ca
śiṣyāṇāṃ saṃskārātiśayaḥ paryaṅkādinā ca sausthityamityevamādaya
upakāraḥ pratipādyante | prācyāmāditya udeti pratīcyāmastameti
dakṣiṇasyāmagastya utarasyāṃ dhruva iti
digviśeṣasaṃbandhapratipādanamātropayogametadadhikaraṇam || 150 ||
mūrtānāṃ sarveṣāmanyādhīnāyāṃ sthitau
mūrtasyādhārasyāpyaparamadhikaraṇamiti paryantavarti mukhyamadhikaraṇaṃ
kimityāha |
ākāśameva keṣāṃciddeśabhedaprakalpanāt |
ādhāraśaktiḥ prathamā sarvasaṃyogināṃ matā || 151 ||
(146) grahanakṣatravimānādīnāṃ tāvadākāśabhevādhikaraṇaṃ prasiddham |
yeṣāmapi pṛthivyekadeśo rathādīnāmadhikaraṇaṃ teṣāmapi tasya
pṛthivyekadeśasyākāśādharatvāt pāramparyeṇākāśamevādhāra iti
sarvagrahaṇenāha | samavāyināṃ tu svāvayavā ādhāraḥ | tatrāpi
paryante paramāṇavaḥ | teṣāṃ cākāśameva deśapravibhāgakāraṇamādhāra
iti mukhyādhāraśaktirākāśāśrayā bhāvānām | na caivamekadeśatayā
saṃkaraḥ svāvaṣṭabdhaparimitadeśamukhakabhāga prakalpane bhinnatayākāśaṃ
deśabhāvaṃ pratipadyate yataḥ | taduktam 'deśabhedaprakalpanāt' iti |
saṃyogināṃ deśatayā vyavahriyamāṇasyākāśabhedasya taireva
saṃyogibhiravyāpakaiḥ parikalpanādākāśaṃ
sarveṣāmasaṅkareṇādhikaraṇamityarthaḥ | 'keṣāṃcit' iti, ye
hyākāśamabhyupayanti saṃsarhavādinasteṣāṃ mata ityarthaḥ | tatraitat
syāt, kimidamanadhyakṣamāmāṇamākāśaṃ nāma | śabdena guṇena guṇī
so'numīyata iti cedākāśaguṇatvasyaivāsiddheḥ śabdasyānanvayyetat
| na sparśavadviśeṣaguṇaḥ śabdaḥ |
ayāvaddravyabhāvitvādāśrayādanyatropalabdherityādihetūpanyāso
vāyaṃ rūpādīnāmapi || 151 ||
satyapyāśraye nimitāntarāt parivṛtidarśanādākāśasya ca
dravyatvāsiddhau śabdāśrayatvasya vivādāspadatvād
yāvaddravyabhāvitvaṃ śabde'siddhaṃ karṇaśaṣkulyavaccchinnākāśadeśa
evopalabdheścāśrayādanyatropalabhyamānatvamasiddham,
samānendriyagrāhyakṣaṇikānekaviśeṣaguṇoyogitvamapi sparśavatāṃ
svakṛtāntaniyatamityāśaṅkyāśasadbhāve loke prasiddhaṃ liṅgamāha
|
idamatreti bhāvānāmabhāvānna prakalpate |
vyapadeśastamākāśanimitaṃ saṃpracakṣate || 152 ||
(147) ihādhāraśaktirevānyathānupapaterākāśasadbhāvaṃ gamayati | tathā
hi śabdapramāṇākānāṃ śabdārtho'rthaḥ | asti cāyaṃ śabdaḥ--idaṃ nakṣatramatra
tiṣṭhatīti | atra iti yannirdiṣṭaṃ tatkicana vastu bhavat
pṛthivyāderanyatvādākāśākhyāmavalaṃbate | na cāvastvetat,
sarvasāmarthyavirahalakṣaṇādavastuna evaṃvidhavyapadeśābhāvāt | nirūpya
hi vyapadeśo nirūpaṇā ca vastunibandhanā, śābde vyavahāre
nirūpitasyaiva vastutvāt | ata eva śatropabhāve sukhamityādau
nirūpyamāṇasyābhāvasyādhārābhāva ityasti
lokaprasiddhavyavahāranimitamākāśamādārabhāvapannam | padārthānāṃ
paramārthavicāre tu sarvaṃ prapaṃcarūpamasat, advayasyaiva satvāt,
ityavicāritaramaṇīyalaukikapadārthāśrayā lakṣaṇapravṛtireva
viruddhetyāśayaḥ || 152 ||
ākāśasya sarvabhāvādhāratvaṃ yaduktaṃ tat siddhasvabhāvaviṣayaṃ
sādhyasvabhāvaviṣayaṃ tu kālasyādhāratvamityāha |
kālāt kriyā vibhajyante ākāśāt sarvamūrtayaḥ |
etāvāṃścaiva bhedo'yamabhedopanibandhanaḥ || 153 ||
(148) 'pratibandhābhyanujñābhyāṃ tena viśvaṃ vibhajyate' iti
vakṣyamāṇanayena janmasthitinirodhapravibhāgena bhāveṣupakurvan
kālaḥ kriyāsvādhārabhāvopagamanena pravibhāgamavasthāpayati | ākāśaṃ
tvasarvagatadravyaparimāṇapadārthasaṃyogāhitapravibhāgamasaṅkareṇādhārabhāvamupayanniyatadeśatayā padārthavyaktīrvibhajate | mūrtikriyāvivartarūpaṃ cedaṃ viśvaṃ bhedāvabhāsabahulaṃ labhyate vyavahāre yathāyathamādyāyāṃ daśāyāmabhinnamapītyetadapekṣayā kālākāśākhyābhedanimitaḥ | kālākāśākhyo'bheda upanibandhanaṃ nimitamasya mūrtikriyāvivartasyaityarthaḥ | dikśaktinimito'pi hi mūrtibhedaḥ pūrvāparadeśātmakatvādākāśādhārapravibhāgena niṣṭhāmupaiti | kālākāśayorapi ca bhedaḥ śaktitvādatyantābhinnaparabrahmanimitaḥ tasmāddhi te śaktī pṛthakkriyete ityupapāditaṃ prathamakāṇḍe |
'upānvadhyāṅvasaḥ' (gh. 1.4.48)
ityupādipūrvasya vasaterādhāraḥ karmasaṃjñaḥ uktaḥ | tatra vārtikakāraḥ
prāha |
'vaseraśyarthasya pratiṣedhaḥ |' (ja.od gh. 1.4.48)
iti | grāma upavasatīti | yathā
tiṣṭhatirgatinivṛtiviśiṣṭamavasthānamāha evaṃ
bhojananivṛtiviśiṣṭaṃ vāsamupavasiriti vātikakāro manyate |
tathā copavasestiṣṭhativad grāmeṇāsti saṃbandha iti prāptā
karmasaṃjñā nivartyate | vivakṣāniyamaścānena vārtikenāvedyate | nātra
grāmāderīpsitatamatvena vivakṣā, api tvādhārabhāvenetyarthaḥ | bhāṣye
tu pratyākhyātametadvārtikam |
"nātropapūrvasya vasergrāmo'dhikaraṇam | kasya tarhi ? anupasargasya |
grāme'sau vasaṃstrirātramupavasatīti |" (g.eṇa.od ja od gh. 1.4.48)
tatrāyamāśayo bhāṣyakārasya | iha kālaṣiśeṣāvaccchinnā
bhujinivṛtirupavaserarthaḥ | tatra kālaviśeṣo'ntaraṅgaḥ,
prākṛtameva karma | abhuñjānasya tvavasthānaṃ niradhikaraṇasya
nopapadyate, ityādhāro'pekṣito niyataḥ | sa copapūrvasya vaserna
saṃbandhītyaprasaktaiva karmasaṃjñā || 153 ||
na hyaevamapi yadā kālavaddeśo'pi niyato'pekṣyate tīrthe
upavasatītyādau tadā styevopavaserdeaśena saṃbandha iti syāt
karmasaṃjñetyāśaṅkya bhāṣyaṃ samarthayitumāha |
yadyapyupavasirdeaśaviśeṣamanurudhyate |
śabdapravṛtidharmātu kālamevāvalaṃbate || 154 ||
(149) tīrthādideśaviśeṣamavalaṃbya bhojanādinivṛtāvabhisaṃhitāyāṃ
deśaviśeṣeṇāpi saṃbandho yadyapyupapūrvasya vaserasti tathāpi nāsau
śabdasāmarthyopanipatito deśasabandhaḥ | bhojananivṛtimātraṃ
hyupavāsārthaḥ | sa cāniyatakālo nopapadyata iti
kālaviśeṣo'ntakaṅgo garbhīkṛtaḥ śābda eva śabdasya
pravṛtirabhidhānavyāpāraḥ, tasyā dharmaḥ svabhāvaḥ, śabdaśaktiriti
yāvat | deśastu devadatasatāya nirādhārāyā
anupapaterabhisaṃhito'pi tīrthādiḥ śabdārthe nāntarbhavati,
bhojananivṛtyā deśasya saṃbandhābhāvāt | tatra tvaṅgabhūto yo
vāsastenaiva deśasyābhisaṃbandhaḥ | tathā
cāṅgabhūtavasatikriyādvāreṇopavāse'dhikaraṇatā deśaḥ pratipadyate |
sākṣāt kālenaiva tu tathā yogaḥ | bhojananivṛterniyatakālatvāt
kālavyavadhinā bhojarūpatvāt tasyāṃ tadaṅgastu vāso niyatadeśa
iti taddvāreṇātra deśe'dhikaraṇam | ata eva bhāṣye 
'grāme vaṃsastrirātramupavasati' (g.eṇa od gh. 1.4.48)
iti upavāsāṅgasya vāsasya grāmeṇa saṃbandho darśito na
tūpavāsasyaiva sākṣāt | sā cātra
vasatikriyāṅgabhūtatvādākṣiptaiveti taddvāreṇeṣyate
kriyākārakasaṃbandhaḥ | vasatyaṅgā hi bhojananivṛtirniyatakālā
upavaserarthaḥ | ataśca grāma upadhasatītyukte'ṅgabhūtavasikriyāpekṣā
deśasyādhikaraṇatā || 154 ||
pradhānabhūtabhojananivṛtyapekṣā ca kālasyāprayujyamānasyāpi karmatā
pratīyata ityāha |
vasatāvaprayukte'pi deśo'dhikaraṇaṃ tataḥ |
aprayuktaṃ trirātrādi karmaṃ copavasau smṛtam || 155 ||
(150) bhāṣya iti śeṣaḥ | śrūyamāṇopavāsakriyāsaṃbandhānarhatvād
grāmādiḥ sāmarthyaprāptāyāmaprayujyamānāmapi
vasatikriyāyāmadhikaraṇam | sādhanaṃ hyaśrūyamāṇāmapi yogyāṃ
kriyāmākṣipati | tadyathā praviśa piṇḍīmiti bhakṣikriyāṃ karmaṃ | atra ca
viśeṣapratipatinibandhanābāvādavyabhicaritasaṃbandhā sāmānyarūpeṇa
satā pratīyate | yasya ca grāmādhikaraṇā satā grāme'sau vasatīti
vāsaṃ pratyadhikaraṇaṃ grāmādirdeaśo'vatiṣṭhate | śrūyamāṇāyā
tūpavasatikriyāyāṃ kāla
evāntaraṅgo'dhikaraṇamityaprayujyamānamaryetadyogyatvādākṣipyate
praveśanakriyayeva gṛham | tatra ca sākṣādupavasatyādhāre kṛtārthā
karmasaṃjñāṅgabhūtavasatikriyādvārāyātamādhāraṃ grāmādideśalakṣaṇaṃ
nāvagāhate, pradhānāpradhānayoḥ pradhāne kāryaṃsampratyayāt
kārakādhikāre ca tamabgrahaṇāt sākṣātkārakaśaktīnāṃ prakarṣo
nādriyate, na tu
kriyāsaṃbandhanimitāntaraṅgabahiraṅgabhāvānādaro'nyathā
svavyāpāre svātantryāt karmaṇaḥ kartṛsaṃjñā syāt | karmasaṃjñāyāḥ
sandarśanādibhirīpsitatamāyāṃ kriyāyāṃ tadā karmasaṃjñā deśe'pi
caritārthā grāmamupavasati, adhitiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ | ataśca
bhojananivṛtyarthatāyāmupavaseḥ sākṣātkāla ādhāraḥ karmasaṃjñaḥ,
deśastvabhisaṃhito'pyaṅgabhūtavasidvāreṇaivādhāro na sākṣāditi
sthitam | yastu nivṛtirūpatvādupavaserādhāreṇāsaṃbandha iti
vyācaṣṭe tasya kālenāpyādhāreṇāsaṃbandho'tra ,syāt | asti ca
gatinivṛtivacanasyapi tiṣṭhaterādhāreṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
sarvavyāpāroparamalakṣaṇasya grāme āste iti nivṛteḥ
kriyāntaramapārtayantyāḥ kriyātvamaviruddhabheva | kārakāṇāṃ hi
pravṛtiviśeṣaḥ phalānumeyaḥ kriyā | sa cātra
niyatakālabhojananivṛtirūpaḥ sphuṭa eva | na cāpi saṃkalpaviśeṣa eva
mānaso vyāpāro nivṛtiḥ | sandarśanādīnāṃ pradhānakriyayā saha
bhedābhāvād yuddhāya saṃnahyate ityādāveva bhedavivekṣetyuktam |
tathā cātra saṃkalpapūrvakaḥ kartṛvyāpāro niyatakālavyavadhinā
bhojanādirūpo dhātorupavaserarthaḥ | kriyātmetyasyādhārasaṃbandhe
satyapi sākṣāddeṣalakṣaṇe nādhāreṇa
saṃbandho'ṅgabhūtavasatikriyādvāreṇāyogāditi
nāntarīyakakālākhyādhārasaṃbandha eva karmasaṃjñāvidhiriti siddham ||
155 ||
iti bhūtirājatanayahelārājakṛte prakīrṇaprakāśe
sādhanasamuddeśe'dhikaraṇādhikāraḥ ||
-------------
(151) idānīṃ ṣaṣṭhaḷrthaḥ saptamaḥ kārakabhedo vicāryate | sa ca
śeṣalakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandhaḥ | tasya kathaṃ kārakabhedatvaṃ kathaṃ ca
śeṣatvamityetadupapādayitumāha |
saṃbandhaḥ kārakebhyo'nyaḥ kriyākārakapūrvakaḥ |
śrutāyāmaśrutāyāṃ vā kriyāyāṃ so'bhidhīyate || 156 ||
kārakebhyo'nyaḥ karmādiviśeṣalakṣaṇebhyaḥ ṣaḍbhyo'nyo yaḥ saṃbandhaḥ
sa śeṣa ityupayuktetaravacanaśeṣaśabdāśrayeṇa |
"kārakaṇāmavivakṣā śeṣaḥ" (g.eṇa.od gh. 2.3.50)
iti bhāṣyaṃ vyākhyātam | 'kriyākārakapūrvakaḥ' ityanena kārakatvaṃ
vyācaṣṭe śeṣasya | tathā hi-rājñaḥ puruṣo vṛkṣasya śākhā paśoḥ pādaḥ
pituḥ putra ityādāvaśrūyamāṇa kriyāviṣaye
svasvāmibhāvāvayavāvayavibhāvajanyajanakabhāvādau saṃbandhe
dadātisthitijanyādikriyāprabhāvite
pūrvabhāvikārakatvamutarāvasthāyāmapyanugatamiti bhavatyeva śeṣaḥ
kārakam | rājā hi puruṣāya dadāti yatastato rājñaḥ puruṣa iti
svasvāmibhāvo'vatiṣṭhati | tatra kriyākārakasaṃbandhaḥ kāraṇabhūtaḥ
śeṣasaṃbandhastu phalabhūtaḥ | kriyākārakasaṃbandho hi vṛtaḥ svāśraye
śeṣasaṃbandhaṃ phalaṃ niveśyoparamate | tatra pūrvaṃ kartṛsaṃpradānarūpau
rājapuruṣābhūtām | śeṣasaṃbandhakāle tu
kartrādiviśeṣarūpatānavagama iti taccheṣabhūtaṃ sāmānyaṃ
kārakatvamavatiṣṭhate | karaṇādisādhanabhedopākhyānimitābhāvāt |
kārakebhyo'nyaḥ kārakāṇāmavivakṣā śeṣaḥ | kārakapūrvaka ityatra ca
kārakatvaikārthasamavāyinaḥ karaṇādayaḥ sādhanabhedāḥ
kārakaśabdenocyante, na tu kārakasāmānyamapi | dravyāṇāṃ hi
siddhasvabhāvānāmayaḥ śalākākalpanānāṃ parasparasaṃbandhābhāvāt
kriyākṛta eva saḥ | kriyā hi niḥśrayaṇīva dravyāṇyupaśleṣayati | tathā
ca bhāṣyam---
"prātipadikārthānāṃ kriyākṛtā viśeṣā udbhavanti karma
karaṇamityādi"
(cḍh g.eṇa.od gh. 2.3.50)
tatra karmādirūpavyirekeṇānyasya viśeṣasyādṛṣṭaḥ śeṣākhyaḥ saṃbandhaḥ
karmādirūpo'vatiṣṭhamāno'nubhūtakarmādiviśeṣe svādhāre
padamupanibadhnannavivivakṣitakarmādibhedarūpamātmānamuddarśayati |
tataśca rājapuruṣayorvartamāne dātṛtvasampradānatve eva
phalāvasthāyāmanudriktasvasvarūpe sāmānyarūpeṇa saṃbandha iti
vyapadeśamarhataḥ | tathā cānulliṅgitaviśeṣarūpā saptamīyaṃ
kārakayoniḥ parigaṇyate, śabdārthasampratyayabhedāt | anyo hi
sampratyayo rājā puruṣāya dadātīti | na hyasmānniyamena
svasvāmibhāvanimitaṃ dānaṃ pratīyate | karaṇādināpi nimitena
dānasaṃbhavāt | anyastu rājñaḥ puruṣa iti, svaslāmibhāvāvagamāt |
ataśca pratītibhedādanyatve siddhe karmādiviśeṣeṇeva
śeṣākhyenāpyanena prātipadikārtho bhidyamāno'bhidhīyate |
tadayamaśrūyamāṇakriyāviṣayaḥ saṃbandhaḥ svasvāmibhāvādinimitānāṃ
dānādikriyāṇāmaśruteḥ |
saṃbandhalakṣaṇakāryānyathānupapatyānumīyamānāśca dānādikriyāḥ
saṃnihitā eveti tanmukhena samanvayopapatiḥ saṃbandhinoḥ phalasyāpi
ca saṃbandhasya kriyānimitatvaṃ kārakatvanibandhanamastyeva karmaṇa iveti
sarvamupapadyate | grāmasya samīpādāgaccchati, vṛkṣasya parṇaṃ patati,
ityādāvapyastyeva samīpasamīpibhāvādau saṃbandhe
kriyākārakapūrvakatvam | samīpasya hi grāma āśrayaḥ evaṃ parṇasya vṛkṣa
iti | ayameva sarvatra nyāyaḥ | āgamanādikriyāṃ prati tu
grāmāderakārakatvamucyate | śrūyamāṇakriyāviṣayastu saṃbandhaḥ
kārakabheda eva, avivakṣitakarmādiviśeṣaḥ saṃbandhasāmānyasvabhāvaḥ
kriyākārakasaṃbandha eva sa iti na tatpūrvakaḥ | tadyathā naṭasya
śṛṇotītyādi | karaṇādisaṃjñānāmatra nimitābhāvādapravṛteḥ |
duhyādiparigaṇanāccākathitakarmatvābhāvāt | kārakebhyo'nyo'yaṃ
śeṣaḥ kāraka eva | tathā copayogagrahaṇamarthavadityuktam | evaṃ mātuḥ
smarati, na māṣāṇāmaśnīyāt, sarpiṣo jānīta ityādau
karmādikarākabheda evāvivakṣitatadrūpaḥ śeṣa ityuktam | atra ca śeṣe
ṣaṣṭhīvibhaktiruktā | saṃbandhasya dviṣṭhatvācca dvābhyāmapi saṃbandhibhyāṃ
ṣaṣṭhīprasaṅga iti coditam---
"ṣaṣṭhī śeṣa iti cedviśeṣye pratiṣedhaḥ (ja od gh. 2.3.50)
iti | rājñaḥ puruṣa iti puruṣaśabdādapi ṣaṣṭhīprasaṅge pratiṣedhaḥ
kārya ityarthaḥ | saṃbandhasya codbhūtatvāt prathamā viśeṣyānna
prāpnotīti sāpi vidheyetyuktam---
"tatra prathamāvidhiḥ"(ja od gh. 2.3.50)
iti | atrobhayatrāpi parihārāya 
"uktaṃ pūrveṇa" (g.eṇa. od dyaṇe aḍodhe dyadhro dhaddhdyattas)
ityabhihitam | prātipadikāryasūtre
"paṭasāmānādikaraṇya upasaṃkhyānamadhikatvāt "(ja od gh. 2.3.46)
iti codite 
"na vā vākyārthatvāt (ja od gh. 2.3.46)
ityuktam | tadevātrātidesāyānūditam | rājña
ityetatpadasamanyavayavaśena puruṣasya saṃbandhitvamavagamyata iti
vākyārtho'tra saṃbandho'ntaraṅge padārthamātrāśraye saṃskāre nimitaṃ
na bhavati, bahiraṅgatvāditi vīraḥ puruṣo
nīvamutpalamityādivadatra ṣaṣṭhaḷbhāvaḥ prathamā ca siddhetyarthaḥ | yadyevaṃ
rājaśabdādapi vīrādiśabdādivānena nyāyena ṣaṣṭhī na syāt |
prathamā ca syāt | naitadasti viśeṣyagatasyaivādhikyasya
vākyārthatvāt | taddhi pradhānaṃ
prathamamajahatsvarūpamevopakāryatāmapekṣite guṇe samāpadyata iti
tasyaivādhikyaṃ vākyārthaḥ kathyate | guṇasya tu pradhānopakārāya
pravartamānasya svātmapratiṣṭhatvopakārakatvāsaṃbhavādupakrama eva
pradhānopayogakṣamaṃ saṃbandhisāmānyāpekṣayā rūpāntaraṃ viparivartate
| taduktaṃ bhāṣye---
"artharūpamevaitadevaṃjātīyakam"...(g.eṇa od gh. 2.3.50)
iti | tathā ca guṇāccheṣaṣaṣṭhī nyāyyā | samānādhikaraṇaṃ tu
viśeṣaṇaṃ nīlādi pradhānenotpalādinā sahaikātmyamibāpadyamānaṃ na
vyatirekaṃ bhajata iti na tatra ṣaṣṭhī bhavati || 146 ||
etadvastusatatvaṃ pratipādayati---
dviṣṭho'pyasau parārthatvād guṇeṣu vyatiricyate |
tatrābhidhīyamānaḥ san pradhāne'pyupabhujyate || 157 ||
(152) dviṣṭhaḥ ekaḥ saṃbandhaḥ | tatra caikaiva ṣaṣṭhī nyāyyā | ṣaṣṭhīdvaye
saṃbandhadvayaṃ pratīyeta | sā caikaiva ṣaṣṭhī bhavantī guṇādeva bhavati na
pradhānāt | yasmādasau saṃbandho dvayorapi saṃbandhinoḥ sthitaḥ,
tayorguṇapradhānabhāvenaikavākyatvāt, pradhānānurodhitvād
guṇānāmeva rūpaparivartanasyaucityāt tatraiva phalatīti guṇādeva
saṃbandhakāryā ṣaṣṭhī nyāyyā | tathā hi guṇeṣu vyatirekamādhikyaṃ
darśayati saṃbandhaḥ | pradhānaṃ tu svarūpādapracyavamānameva
saṃbandhamanubhavati | evaṃ hi tasya prādhānyaṃ bhavati yadi
guṇānurodhena rūpaparivartanaṃ nāsādayati | guṇāśrayatayā ca
saṃbandhasyābhidhāne ṣaṣṭhaḷa pradhānagatamapi rūpamupabhuktaṃ
spṛṣṭamevābhihitamevetyarthaḥ | etataducyate pradhānagatasyādhikyasya
vākyārthatvaṃ guṇasaṃbandhena pratīteḥ | parasparasākāṅkṣasya
padasamūhasya vākyatvāt | rājña iti hi
svāmitvamavagamyamānamanyatānupapatyā puruṣe'pi
svatvalakṣaṇamatirekamāvedayate | tathā ca puruṣādantaraṅgāt
prathamaiva bhavatīti bahiraṅgasya saṃbandhasya
padāntarasamanvayānyathānupapatyaiva gamitatvāt prātipadikārtha
evādhikāsyānanupraveśāt | yadā tu viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāve
kāmacārāt puruṣasya rājetyucyate tadā guṇāt puruṣādeva
ṣaṣṭhīti na vyabhicarati lakṣaṇam || 157 ||
uktamidaṃ kriyākārakapūrvakaḥ saṃbandha iti | tatra
kriyākārakasaṃbandho'sya nimitam , sa ca phalabhūtāt
saṃbandhādanumīyamāno na viśeṣeṇānumātuṃ pāryeta, api tu
sāmānyena, evaṃsvabhāvatvādanumānasyeti kuto'yaṃ viśeṣāvasāya
ityāha---
nimitaniyamaḥ śabdāt saṃbandhasya na gṛhyate |
karmapravacanīyaistu sa viśeṣe'varudhyate || 158 ||
(153) pituḥ putraḥ, paśoḥ pāda ityādau
saṃbandhisvarūpaviśeṣāvadhāraṇādeva
niyatasthitijananādikriyāprabhāvitatvaṃ saṃbandhasya pratīyate |
rājapuruṣa ityādau tu yāñcāvinimayāpahāraprabhṛtibhirapi
svasvāmibhāvasya saṃbhavādādyadyapi niyamanimitatvaṃ nāvadhāryate tathāpi
dānādīnāmanyatamāvinābhāvāt kriyānumīyata eva
saṃbandhisvarūpāditi nāyaṃ karmapravacanīyaviṣayaḥ | kvacitu
śabdasāmarthyānnimitaviśeṣo nāvadhāryate | nimitasāmānyāvadhāraṇaṃ
tu | so'yamevaṃvidho viṣayaḥ karmapravacanīyānām |
tairanvādibhirnimitaniyamenāvaccchidyate saṃbandhathaḥ | tathā hi
śākalyasya saṃhitāmanu prāvarṣadityatra
saṃhitāpravarṣaṇayorhetuhetumadbhāvo niśamayatikriyājanita
ityanunāvedyate | tathā kriyayāsyānyatrānuniśamyetyādau
sāhacaryopalabdheḥ karma proktavanta iti ca
karmapravacanīyeṣavtikrāntakriyāprakāśanāṅgīkārāt saṃprati
kriyāmayaṃ na dyotayati | kriyāpadasya cāśruteḥ dyotyābhidhāyakābhāve
tanniṣṭhadyotakatvābhāvāt | svarūpeṇa tu saṃbandho vibhaktyaiva
pratipādyate iti nāpi tadvācakaḥ | kriyāpadākṣepe tu nāsya
sāmarthyaṃm, kārakavibhaktyabhāvāditi saṃbandhaviśeṣāvadyotakatvaṃ
sthāpitaṃ karmapravacanīyānām | taduktamanantarakāṇḍe 
"kriyāyā dyotako nāyaṃ na saṃbandhasya vācakaḥ | 
nāpi kriyāpadākṣepī saṃbandhasya tu bhedakaḥ || 
iti | evamapi "adhi brahmadate pañcālāḥ" iti
paripālanādikriyānimitatvaṃ svasvāmibhāvasyādhināvedyate |
"abhimanyurarjunataḥ prati" iti
paraparājayanādikriyākṛto'nukāryānukaraṇabhāvo'rjunābhimanyvoḥ saṃbandha iti pratinā prakāśyate | evamanyatrāpi yathāyogamanugantavyam | tadayamaśrutakriyāviṣaye saṃbandhe karmapravacanīyānāṃ mahimā | śrutakriyāviṣaye tu śrūyamāṇaiva kriyā niyataṃ nimitamavadhāryata iti || 158 ||
tatra tvidaṃ vicāryate | śeṣa ityevaṃ siddhāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhaḷaṃ 'adhīgartha'
ityādinā punarvidhānaṃ kimartham | 'karmaṇi śeṣe' iti ca
sāmānādhikaraṇyena viśeṣaṇānupapatiḥ (karmatvavivakṣāyāṃ hi na
śeṣaḥ, kārakāṇāmavivakṣā śeṣa ityukteḥ | phalabhūtaśeṣavivakṣāyāṃ ca
tirohitatvāt karmatvasyetyāśaṅkyāha---
sādhanairvyapadiṣṭe ca śrūyamāṇakriye punaḥ |
proktā pratipadaṃ ṣaṣṭhī samāsasya nivṛtaye || 159 ||
(154) karmādīni sādhanāni śeṣasya vyapadeśārthānyatropādīyante na
tu svārtham | śeṣe ṣaṣṭhī | kasmin śeṣe | karmaṇi karaṇa iti vā
bhūtāvasthāgatakarmatvādirūpāśrayeṇa śeṣasya viśeṣaṇāt
karmādyekārthasamavāyini phalabhūte śeṣa ityartho'vatiṣṭhate | tathā ca
viśeṣaṇopapatiḥ | ṣaṣṭhī tu siddhāpi pratipadamadhīgarthetyādinā
niyataviṣayatayā vidhīyate, punarvidhānena samāso yathā
nivartyeteti | punaḥ śrutirhi niyamārthā | tathā ca ṣaṣṭhaḷeva bhavatīti
vākyārthaḥ | niyame cānyanivṛtiḥ pradhānam, anyaśca pratiyogī
vyavacchedyaḥ | sa cātra ṣaṣṭhaḷabhāvaḥ sa ca samāse bhavatīti sāmarthyāt
samāsaniṣedho'yamavatiṣṭhate | tadetaduktam pratipadavidhānā ṣaṣṭhī
na samasyate iti | sūtravyāpāra evānena vārtikena nāpūrvo'rthaḥ | kva
punarayaṃ samāsapratiṣedhaḥ | yatra samāsasya prāptiḥ | kva ca prāptiḥ |
subanteṣu mātuḥ smṛtamiti || 159 ||
tathā hyatra |
niṣṭhāyāṃ karmaviṣayā ṣaṣṭhī na pratiṣidhyate |
śeṣalakṣaṇayā ṣaṣṭhaḷa samāsastatra neṣyate || 160 ||
(155) kṛdyoga ca ṣaṣṭhī samasyata ityuktam | tathā ca 
"kartṛkarmaṇoḥ kṛti"(gh. 2.3.69)
iti yā ṣaṣṭhī tatra bhavatyeva samāsaḥ 'dharmānusmaraṇam,
arthānusmaraṇam' ityādau | na hyaeṣa ṣaṣṭhaḷa niyamaḥ 'kartṛkarmaṇoḥ
kṛti' iti punaḥ karmagrahaṇāccheṣagrahaṇasya
nivṛterityapūrvavidhānamidam | tena ṣaṣṭhaḷeveti
ṣaṣṭhīniyamadvāreṇa samāsapratiṣedhamāvedayate | mātuḥ
smṛtamityādau niṣṭhāprayoge 
"na lokāvyaya".....(gh. 2.3.69)
ityādinā 'kartṛkarmaṇoḥ kṛti' itīyaṃ ṣaṣṭhī niṣiddheti
"adhīgarthadayeśāṃ karmaṇi"(gh. 2.3.52)
ityanenaiva karmaṇi śeṣatvena vivakṣite ṣaṣṭhīti sāmānyena siddhāyāṃ
punarvidhānāt samāsapratiṣedhārthā vijñāyate | nanu ca 
"ktasya ca vartamāne" (gh. 2.3.67)
"napusake bhāva upasaṃkhyānam" (ja od gh. 2.3.67)
ityanenātra ṣaṣṭhī | naitat |
"śeṣavijñānāt siddham" (ja od gh. 2.3.67)
ityasya pratyākhyānāt śeṣaṣaṣṭhayeveyam | punarvidhānena
samāsābhāvaṃ jñāpayati | evamapi mātuḥ smṛtamiti prayogānupapatiḥ
| smarateḥ sakarmakatvāt karmaṇi
niṣṭhāprasaṅgānmātṛsāmānādhikaraṇyānmātā smṛteti syāt |
saṃbandhyantarāpekṣāyāṃ tu mātuḥ smṛtāyā iti prayogo nyāyyaḥ |
satyametat |
"karmādiṣvakarmakavadvacanam"
iti tūktam | tathā cātrākarmakavadbhāve ktapratyayāntena
karmaṇābhidhānāccheṣavivakṣāyāṃ tatra ṣaṣṭhī punarvidhīyamānā
samāsapratiṣedhasya sampadyata iti sthitametat | evaṃ
"jño'vidarthasya karaṇe"(gh. 2.3.51)
ityādau pratipadavidhāne sarpiṣo jñānaṃ madhuno jñānamityādiḥ
samāsapratiṣedha udāhāryaḥ | nanu cātra samāse kṛte saṃbandhamātraṃ
pratīyate, na tu karaṇapūrvakaḥ saṃbandha iti vigrahavākyārthābhidhāne
sāmarthyābhāvādatra samāso na bhaviṣyatīti kimanena yatnena |
naitadasti | vākye'pi na śābdaḥ saṃbandhaviśeṣāvasāyaḥ, sādṛśyāt |
tathā hi karmaṣaṣṭhaḷamapīdṛśameva vākyam | tatra yathārthaprakaraṇādinā
jānātervidarthatvasaṃpratyaye karmaṣaṣṭhaḷa bhavati samāsaḥ tatvajñānaṃ
varṇajñānamityādau | tathā ca sarpiṣo jānāmītyatrāpi
prakaraṇāderavidarthatvasaṃpratyaye karaṇaṣaṣṭhaḷa api bhavet samāsa iti
śeṣagrahaṇānuvṛtyā'trāpi punarvidhānamāśritya pariharaṇīyaḥ sa iti
sthitam | vibhaktividhānasya
svādividhinaikavākyatve'pyavayavavavākyānāṃ svavyāpārasya nivṛteḥ
pratipadavidhānatve na kācidanupapatiḥ || 160 ||
dviṣṭhaḥ saṃbandho guṇeṣu vyatiricyata ityuktam | guṇapradhānabhāvasya
caikatra virodhāt bhāṣya uktam---
"na tarhīdānīmidaṃ bhavati rājñaḥ puruṣasyeti'(g.eṇa od gh. 2.3.51) iti |
tatra pratisamādhānamuktam ---
"bhavati bāhyamarthamapekṣya " (g.eṇa od gh. 2.3.51)
iti | etadbhāṣyaṃ vyācaṣṭe |
anyena vyapadiṣṭasya yasyānyatropajāyate |
vyatirekaḥ sa dharmau dvau labhate viṣayāntare || 161 ||
(156) rājñaḥ puruṣasya kambala ityādau rājñā viśeṣaṇena vyapadiṣṭasya
viśeṣyatayā jñāpitasya puruṣasya pradhānabhūtasya anyatra kambalādau
pratiyogini pradhāne vyatireka ādhikyaṃ saṃbandho jāyate iti sa
bhinniviṣayau guṇapradhānabhāvalakṣaṇau dvau dharmau pratipadyate |
'vyapadiṣṭasya' iti bhūtakālepādānena saṃbandhidvayayaugo
bhinnakālaguṇapradhānanimito na virudhyata ityāha || 161 ||
tataśca |
prādhānyaṃ svaguṇe labdhvā pradhāne yāti śeṣatām |
sahayoge svayoge'taḥ pradhānatvaṃ na hīyate || 162 ||
(157) ato viṣayabhedāddhetoḥ puruṣasya rājānaṃ saṃbandhinaṃ prati
prādhānyaṃ na nivartate | svasmin hi tatra guṇe'pradhāne rājalakṣaṇe
prādhānyaṃ pūrvameva tena labdham | kambalādau tvaparasmin pradhāne
śeṣabhāvaṃ guṇatvaṃ yātīti tatra svasya kambalāderyoge pūrvapratītaṃ
prādhānyaṃ nāvagaccchati, viṣayabhedāt |
guṇapradhānabhāvasyaikatrāvirodhādityarthaḥ | 'sahayoge' iti
prasaṅgānnidarśanamabhinnayogakṣemamāha |
"sahayakte'pradhāne"(gh. 2.3.19)
ityanenāpradhānāt tṛtīyā vidhīyate putreṇa sahāgata iti | tatra
putro devadatasyeti yathā viśeṣyamāṇastadapekṣayā pradhānamapyāgamane
sahārthasāmarthyādāgatasaṃbandhādanumīyamānāgamanasaṃbandho'pradhānamiti bhavati tato'pradhānalakṣaṇā tṛtīyā | tathā puruṣo rājāpekṣayā pradhānamapi svaviśeṣyāpekṣayāpradhānatvādrājñaḥ puruṣasya dhanamiti ṣaṣṭhaĀ pratipadyata iti siddham || 162 ||
iti bhūtirājatanayahelārājakṛte prakīrṇakaprakāśe sādhanasamuddeśe
śeṣādhikāraḥ ||

(158) evaṃ tāvat kārakātmā vibhaktyartho vicāritaḥ | prathamā tu 
"abhihite prathamā"(g.eṇa od 2.3.46)
ityevaṃ kaiścid vihitā | tiṅadibhirabhihite karmādau kārake
prathamā | pacyate odanaḥ, māṃsapacano vanhiḥ, godhno'tithiḥ,
praskandanaḥ staṃbhaḥ , niśā nīśāṇā, pacati devadataḥ iti yathākramaṃ

"astirbhavantīparaḥ prathamapuruṣo'prayujyamāno'pyasti"

"na satāṃ padārtho vyabhicarati"

pravṛtihetuṃ sarveṣāṃ śabdānāmaupacārikīm

(159) saṃbodhane'pi prathamoktā | tatra ca saṃbodhanarūpaṃ vyācaṣṭe |

(161) avyayaṃ vibhakti---(gh. 2.1.6)
iti vibhaktyarthe vartamānamavyayaṃ samarthenotarapadena samasyata
ityuktam , 'adhistri' iti | tatra yadi dravyameva vibhaktyartho bhavet
'tadā strīśabdenaiva tasyoktatvādavyayamaprayogārhameva |
tadasyāprayujyamānasya kutaḥ samāsaḥ | tasmād
vibhaktyarthe'vyayībhāvaśāsanāda vasīyate prātipadikameva karmādīni
sādhanāni nābhidhata iti | tathā
hyadhikaraṇaśartimavyayapadamabhidadhadutarapadenānuvabhavati samāsam |
"dṛṣṭo hyavyatireke'pi vyatireko'nvaye sati |'
ityādinā pūrvamanvayavyatirekābhyāmayamarthaḥ sādhitaḥ | saṃprati
liṅgadarśanenopodbalitaḥ | nanu ca paṃcakaprātipadikārthapakṣe
viśiṣṭasya nirbhāgasya dravyasya prātipadikenoktātvād
viśeṣadyotanāya vibhaktaya iva avyayamapi prayogārhaṃ syāt |
dyotakānāṃ niyamābāvāditi kathametalliṅgaṃ yujyate | satyametat
| kiṃ tu trikapakṣe liṅgamidamupanyasyate | tathā hi 'avyayaṃ
vibhakti....' iti vibhaktyartha upalakṣitaḥ | paṃcakapakṣe tu
dravyātmabhūtasya viśeṣasya vibhaktibhiḥ prakāśanāt padenaivārtha
upalabhyeta, samudāyavācyatvātasya, na kevalayā vibhaktyā |
paṃcakapakṣe'pi vā niyatasya śaktyātmano viśeṣasya
prātipadikāmātrādapratītervibhaktīnāmevāsau viṣayaḥ | tathā ca
prātipadikavācyādaniyataśakterdravyādanyo niyataśaktirvibhaktyartha
iti yuktamucyate | evaṃ ca kṛtvānvayavtirekāvapūrvābhidhāne dyotane
vā yathāyogaṃ pakṣadvaye vyāpriyeti || 165 ||
nanu ca samānakartṛkayoḥ paśau kartari ityevamāderliṅgād
dravyasya sādhanatvāvagamāt kathaṃ dravyādanyo vibhaktyartha ityāśaṃkya
śakteḥ sādhanatvamupapādhayitumāha |
dravyaṃ tu yad yathābhūtaṃ tadatyantaṃ tathā bhavet |
kriyābhede'pi tasyāsau dravyātmā nāvahīyate || 166 ||

"anabhihite hi vidhānam"(ja od gh. 2.3.1)
atha kriyāntarayoge karaṇasya karmātmatayā pariṇāmastadā sakalasyaiva
dravyasvabhāvasya vastvantarāpatiḥ syāt, na karaṇāṃśasyaiva, tasya
dravyādanyatvāt | śaktestu sādhanatve kriyābhedena tadbhede'pi
tadādhārasya dravyasyānvayino'vasthānāt
śaktyantaratyāgopādānayoge yuktaḥ karaṇādirūpatayā saṃbhavaḥ |
asamarthasya dravyasya kriyāsiddhāvanaṃgatvāt sāmarthyameva tadādhāraṃ
sādhanaṃ yuktam | na caitāvatā dravyasyāpi sādhanādhāratvāt
sādhanabhāvaḥ kalpayituṃ nyāyyaḥ | na hi devadatasya pacisādhanatve
tadādhārasya pīṭhasyāpi tat kalpyata iti śaktaya eva sādhanaṃ na tu
śaktimad dravyam |
"samānakartṛkayoḥ"(gh. 3.4.21)
iti tu śaktyādhāradravyaikatvādhyāropanimitaṃ samānatvaṃ
kartṛśaktyoḥ | 'paśau kartari' hastisūcakayoḥ kartroḥ iti
phaśau dravye yaḥ kartā hastisūcakayoḥ dravyayoḥ kartārau iti
vyākhyeyam | athavā kartṛśaktyārśyaḥ paśureva kartā iti
sāmānādhikaraṇyam |
"karmaṇi dvitīyā"(gh. 2.3.2)
ityādāvapi śaktīnāṃ saṃkhyāyogāt tadādhāraṃ dravyameva nirdiśyate |
tena karmaikārthasamavetāyāṃ saṃkhyāyāṃ vibhaktaya ityarthaḥ |
niradhārāyāsca śakteranupapateḥ dravyameva śaktyāviṣṭaṃ sādhanaṃ
lakāravācyam | tacca viśeṣātmakameva kriyākāri
niyatenātmanānabhihitaṃ padāntareṇābhivyajyata iti devadataḥ
pacatītyādisāmānādhikaraṇyena prayogopapatiriti sarvaṃ sustham ||
167 ||
iti bhūtirājatanayahelārājakṛte prakīrṇakaprakāśe sādhanasamuddeśaḥ
saptamaḥ ||
